*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Program Name     : /CCEJ/RDSDFSVI_STLMNT_DTLS_SUB                    *
* Program Title    : Settlement Details                                *
* Program Type     : Include program                                   *
* Description      : There are eight different mode in this report that*
*                    display settlement data based on Visit List.      *
* Mode 1           : DSD Route Settlement: Tour Header Data            *
* Mode 2           : This mode will display list of all Vending        *
*                    machines/Van Sales Customers/Pre-saes customers   *
*                   (Hybrid route) visited during the route execution. *
* Mode 3           : This mode of report will display all the          *
*                    information related to sales and replenishment    *
*                    done for the tour.                                *
* Mode 4           : Display Payment Details                           *
* Mode 5           : Display Check Out/In Details                      *
* Mode 6           : Display Money Differences                         *
* Mode 7           : Display Quantity Differences                      *
* Mode 8          :  Display FSR Documents                             *
* Date             : 16 May 2016                                       *
* Author           : <Abhishek Buckal>  <S404352>                      *
* Run Frequency    :  N/A                                              *
* Transaction      :  /CCEJ/RDOVM_STLMNTDT                             *
* Transport Number :  DCRK9A0JSC                                       *
* RICEF ID         :  <OTC-3161-R-01>                                  *
* Release          :  <CCEJ 5.1>                                       *
* Additional Info  : (EX: Form Name/Output type/Copied from/ etc)      *

*&  Include           /CCEJ/RDSDFSVI_STLMNT_DTLS_SUB
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_GET_DATA
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*     Fetching Shipment related data
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_get_shipment_data.
*********************Internal Table  Declaration**********************
  DATA:l_i_vttk    TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_vttk,
       l_i_status  TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_status,
       l_i_vbak    TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_vbak,
       l_i_likp    TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_likp,          "#EC NEEDED
       l_i_invoice TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_invoice,       "#EC NEEDED
       l_i_bkpf    TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_bkpf,
       l_i_accdoc  TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_bkpf,          "#EC NEEDED
       l_i_mkpf    TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_mkpf,
       l_i_final   TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_final,         "#EC NEEDED
       l_i_vbfa    TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF vbfa.             "#EC NEEDED
*********************Work Area Declaration****************************
  DATA:l_wa_vttk    TYPE ty_vttk,
       l_wa_likp    TYPE ty_likp,                           "#EC NEEDED
       l_wa_invoice TYPE ty_invoice,                        "#EC NEEDED
       l_wa_accdoc  TYPE ty_bkpf,                           "#EC NEEDED
       l_wa_vbco6   TYPE vbco6.                             "#EC NEEDED
*********************Field Symbols Declaration************************
  FIELD-SYMBOLS:<l_fs_vttk>   TYPE  ty_vttk,
                <l_fs_vbak>   TYPE ty_vbak,
                <l_fs_mkpf>   TYPE ty_mkpf,
                <l_fs_bkpf>   TYPE ty_bkpf,
                <l_fs_status> TYPE ty_status.
*********************Constants Declaration****************************
  CONSTANTS:l_c_zero TYPE c  VALUE '0'. "for deleting Leading Zero's
*********************Data Declaration*********************************
  DATA:l_v_seqno TYPE i, "Sequence no. Counter
       l_v_xblnr TYPE xblnr,
       l_v_bktxt TYPE bktxt.
**********************************************************************
  SELECT tknum tplst erdat route /bev1/rpfar1 /bev1/rpmowa
  ##too_many_itab_fields
" *****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_S Conversion **begin of change by EJX9007360 2026.06.11 ATC
* FROM  vttk INTO TABLE l_i_vttk
          FROM  vttk INTO TABLE l_i_vttk "#EC CI_USAGE_OK[2270199]
" *****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_S Conversion **end of change by EJX9007360 2026.06.11 ATC
         WHERE tknum IN s_tknum
         AND   tplst IN s_tplst
         AND   erdat IN s_erdat
         AND   route IN s_route
         AND   /bev1/rpfar1 IN s_driv
         AND   /bev1/rpmowa IN s_vehic.


  IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
    IF l_i_vttk[] IS NOT INITIAL.

      SELECT status_id shipment
             FROM /dsd/st_status INTO TABLE l_i_status FOR ALL ENTRIES
             IN l_i_vttk
             WHERE status_id IN s_status
             AND   shipment EQ l_i_vttk-tknum.

      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
        SORT l_i_status BY shipment.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    SORT l_i_vttk BY tknum.
    LOOP AT l_i_status ASSIGNING <l_fs_status>.
      READ TABLE l_i_vttk ASSIGNING <l_fs_vttk> WITH KEY tknum  =
      <l_fs_status>-shipment BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
        <l_fs_vttk>-xblnr = <l_fs_vttk>-tknum.
        <l_fs_vttk>-bktxt = <l_fs_vttk>-tknum.
        SHIFT <l_fs_vttk>-xblnr  LEFT DELETING LEADING l_c_zero.
        "Deleting leading zero
        SHIFT <l_fs_vttk>-bktxt  LEFT DELETING LEADING l_c_zero.
        "Deleting leading zero
        UNASSIGN <l_fs_vttk>.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
    IF <l_fs_status> IS ASSIGNED.
      UNASSIGN <l_fs_status>.
    ENDIF.
    DELETE l_i_vttk WHERE xblnr IS INITIAL.
    "Delete entry if shipment no. is initial

    IF l_i_vttk[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT vbeln erdat auart xblnr   ##too_many_itab_fields
" *****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1 Conversion **begin of change by EJX9007365 2026.05.25 for ATC
* FROM vbak INTO TABLE l_i_vbak FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_vttk
             FROM vbak INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE l_i_vbak FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_vttk
" *****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1 Conversion **end of change by EJX9007365 2026.05.25 for ATC
             WHERE xblnr EQ l_i_vttk-xblnr.

      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR l_i_vbak.
      ENDIF.

      SELECT bukrs belnr gjahr blart budat
           "BOC For MOD-015
             cputm
             usnam
           "EOC For MOD-015
             xblnr
             FROM bkpf INTO TABLE l_i_bkpf FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_vttk
             WHERE xblnr EQ l_i_vttk-xblnr.

      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR l_i_bkpf.
      ENDIF.

      SELECT mblnr bktxt
             FROM mkpf INTO TABLE l_i_mkpf FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_vttk
             WHERE bktxt EQ l_i_vttk-bktxt.

      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR l_i_mkpf.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

**" Getting sales order,delivery,material doc. and invoice related data
*based on shippment no.

  IF l_i_vbak[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    SORT:l_i_vbak BY xblnr vbeln,
         l_i_vttk BY xblnr.

    LOOP AT l_i_vbak ASSIGNING <l_fs_vbak>.
      IF l_v_xblnr NE <l_fs_vbak>-xblnr.
        CLEAR l_v_seqno.
      ENDIF.
      l_v_seqno = l_v_seqno + 1.
      PERFORM f_build_itab USING l_v_seqno <l_fs_vbak>.
      "Getting Sales Document Flow Data

      READ TABLE l_i_vttk INTO l_wa_vttk WITH KEY xblnr =
      <l_fs_vbak>-xblnr BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
        wa_final-tknum = l_wa_vttk-tknum.
        wa_final-xblnr = l_wa_vttk-xblnr.
        wa_final-bktxt = l_wa_vttk-bktxt.
        wa_final-tplst = l_wa_vttk-tplst.
        wa_final-erdat_s = l_wa_vttk-erdat.
        wa_final-route = l_wa_vttk-route.
        wa_final-/bev1/rpfar1 = l_wa_vttk-/bev1/rpfar1.
      ENDIF.

      READ TABLE l_i_status ASSIGNING <l_fs_status> WITH KEY shipment =
      l_wa_vttk-tknum BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
        wa_final-status_id = <l_fs_status>-status_id.
        UNASSIGN <l_fs_status>.
      ENDIF.

      l_v_xblnr = <l_fs_vbak>-xblnr.

      APPEND wa_final TO i_final.
      CLEAR:l_wa_vttk,l_i_vbfa,wa_final.
    ENDLOOP.
    IF <l_fs_vbak> IS ASSIGNED.
      UNASSIGN <l_fs_vbak>.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM f_get_data. ""Getting delivery,Invoice,Accounting Info.

**" Getting Material document related Data based on shipment no.
  IF l_i_mkpf[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    SORT:l_i_mkpf BY bktxt,
         i_final  BY bktxt seqno DESCENDING.
    LOOP AT l_i_mkpf ASSIGNING <l_fs_mkpf> .
      READ TABLE i_final INTO wa_final WITH KEY bktxt =
      <l_fs_mkpf>-bktxt.                                "#EC CI_READTBL
      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
        CLEAR:wa_final-vbeln_d,wa_final-auart,wa_final-vbeln,
        wa_final-erdat_o,
        wa_final-lfart,wa_final-vbeln_d,wa_final-erdat_d,wa_final-mblnr
        ,wa_final-fkart,
        wa_final-vbeln_i,wa_final-fkdat.

        IF l_v_bktxt IS INITIAL.
          l_v_bktxt = <l_fs_mkpf>-bktxt.
          l_v_seqno = wa_final-seqno.
          l_v_seqno =  l_v_seqno + 1.
        ELSEIF l_v_bktxt NE <l_fs_mkpf>-bktxt.
          l_v_bktxt = <l_fs_mkpf>-bktxt.
          l_v_seqno = wa_final-seqno.
          l_v_seqno =  l_v_seqno + 1.
        ELSE.
          l_v_seqno =  l_v_seqno + 1.
        ENDIF.
        wa_final-seqno =  l_v_seqno.
        wa_final-mblnr = <l_fs_mkpf>-mblnr.
        APPEND wa_final TO i_final.
        CLEAR:wa_final.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
    IF <l_fs_mkpf> IS ASSIGNED.
      UNASSIGN <l_fs_mkpf>.
    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.

**" Getting FI document related Data based on shipment no.
  IF l_i_bkpf[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    CLEAR:l_v_xblnr,l_v_seqno.
    SORT:l_i_bkpf BY xblnr,
         i_final BY xblnr seqno DESCENDING.
    LOOP AT l_i_bkpf ASSIGNING <l_fs_bkpf>.
      READ TABLE i_final INTO wa_final WITH KEY xblnr =
      <l_fs_bkpf>-xblnr.                                "#EC CI_READTBL
      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
        CLEAR:wa_final-vbeln_d,wa_final-auart,wa_final-vbeln,
        wa_final-erdat_o,
        wa_final-lfart,wa_final-vbeln_d,wa_final-erdat_d,wa_final-mblnr
        ,wa_final-fkart,
        wa_final-vbeln_i,wa_final-fkdat.
        IF l_v_xblnr IS INITIAL.
          l_v_xblnr = <l_fs_bkpf>-xblnr.
          l_v_seqno = wa_final-seqno.
          l_v_seqno =  l_v_seqno + 1.
        ELSEIF l_v_xblnr NE <l_fs_bkpf>-xblnr.
          l_v_xblnr = <l_fs_bkpf>-xblnr.
          l_v_seqno = wa_final-seqno.
          l_v_seqno =  l_v_seqno + 1.
        ELSE.
          l_v_seqno =  l_v_seqno + 1.
        ENDIF.

        wa_final-seqno =  l_v_seqno.
        wa_final-belnr = <l_fs_bkpf>-belnr.
        wa_final-bukrs = <l_fs_bkpf>-bukrs.
        wa_final-blart = <l_fs_bkpf>-blart.
        wa_final-gjahr = <l_fs_bkpf>-gjahr.
        wa_final-budat = <l_fs_bkpf>-budat.

        APPEND wa_final TO i_final.
        CLEAR:wa_final.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
    IF <l_fs_bkpf> IS ASSIGNED.
      UNASSIGN <l_fs_bkpf>.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  SORT i_final BY xblnr seqno.
ENDFORM.                    " F_GET_DATA
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_ORDER_FLOW
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*     Fetching Sales flow using FM- RV_ORDER_FLOW_INFORMATION
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_order_flow  USING  fp_s_vbco6 TYPE vbco6
                     CHANGING fp_i_vbfa TYPE tb_vbfa.

*  CALL FUNCTION 'RV_ORDER_FLOW_INFORMATION'
*    EXPORTING
*      comwa         = fp_s_vbco6
*      vorgaenger    = abap_false "***added for MOD-026
*    TABLES
*      vbfa_tab      = fp_i_vbfa
*    EXCEPTIONS
*      no_vbfa       = 1
*      no_vbuk_found = 2
*      OTHERS        = 3.
*  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
*    CLEAR fp_i_vbfa.
*  ENDIF.

  CALL FUNCTION 'SD_VBFA_READ_WITH_VBELV'
    EXPORTING
      i_vbelv            = fp_s_vbco6-vbeln
      i_bypassing_buffer = abap_true
      i_refresh_buffer   = c_buffer
    TABLES
*     ET_VBFAVB          =
      et_vbfa            = fp_i_vbfa
    EXCEPTIONS
      record_not_found   = 1
      OTHERS             = 2.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
* Implement suitable error handling here
  ENDIF.


ENDFORM.                    " F_ORDER_FLOW
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_DISPLAY_ALV
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       ALV Display
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_display_alv USING fp_final TYPE ANY TABLE.
*********************Reference variable Declaration*******************
  DATA:
*    l_o_table      TYPE REF TO cl_salv_table, "***commented for MOD-027
    l_o_functions TYPE REF TO cl_salv_functions_list,
    l_o_display   TYPE REF TO cl_salv_display_settings,     "#EC NEEDED
    l_o_columns   TYPE REF TO cl_salv_columns_table.

***start of addition for MOD-004
  DATA: l_o_layout TYPE REF TO cl_salv_layout,
        l_wa_key   TYPE salv_s_layout_key.
***end of addition for MOD-004
*********************Constants Declaration****************************
  CONSTANTS: l_c_status     TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'FSR_STATUS', "FSR status
***start of addition for MOD-001
             l_c_status_fsr TYPE sypfkey VALUE '100'. "PF Status
***end of addition for MOD-001
**********************************************************************
  IF fp_final[] IS NOT INITIAL.

    TRY.
" *****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **begin of change by EJX9007359 03.06.2026 for ATC
        SORT fp_final .
" *****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **end of change by EJX9007359 03.06.2026 for ATC
        CALL METHOD cl_salv_table=>factory
          IMPORTING
***start of changes for MOD-027
*           r_salv_table = l_o_table
            r_salv_table = o_table
***end of changes for MOD-027
          CHANGING
            t_table      = fp_final.

    ENDIF.

*   BOC MOD-007

    l_o_display = o_table->get_display_settings( ).

    l_o_display->set_striped_pattern( cl_salv_display_settings=>true ).
***end of addition for MOD-001

***start of addition for MOD-027
    PERFORM f_event_fil USING o_table.
*   recount the rows
    v_rows = lines( fp_final ).
    v_string = v_rows.
    CONDENSE v_string.
    CONCATENATE TEXT-c77 TEXT-047 v_string INTO
      v_string SEPARATED BY space.
    l_o_display->set_list_header( v_string ).
***end of addition for MOD-027

***start of changes for MOD-027
*    l_o_table->display( ).
    o_table->display( ).
***end of changes for MOD-027
  ELSE.
    MESSAGE i011. "If no records found based on input given
    LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
  ENDIF.

      l_o_column = fp_columns->get_column( l_c_post_date ).
      l_o_column->set_long_text( TEXT-c20 ).
      l_o_column->set_medium_text( space ).
      l_o_column->set_short_text( space ).
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.                            "#EC NO_HANDLER
  ENDTRY.

  TRY.
      l_o_column = fp_columns->get_column( l_c_settmnt ).
      l_o_column->set_long_text( TEXT-c37 ).
      l_o_column->set_medium_text( space ).
      l_o_column->set_short_text( space ).
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.                            "#EC NO_HANDLER
  ENDTRY.

*BOC For MOD-014

  TRY.
      l_o_column = fp_columns->get_column( l_c_cominv ).
      l_o_column->set_long_text( TEXT-c54 ).
      l_o_column->set_medium_text( space ).
      l_o_column->set_short_text( space ).
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.                            "#EC NO_HANDLER
  ENDTRY.
  "Setting hotspot
  TRY.
      l_o_col_tab ?= fp_columns->get_column( l_c_cominv ).
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.                            "#EC NO_HANDLER
  ENDTRY.
  TRY.
      CALL METHOD l_o_col_tab->set_cell_type
        EXPORTING
          value = if_salv_c_cell_type=>hotspot.
    CATCH cx_salv_data_error .                          "#EC NO_HANDLER
  ENDTRY.
  TRY.
      l_o_column = fp_columns->get_column( l_c_comfi ).
      l_o_column->set_long_text( TEXT-c55 ).
      l_o_column->set_medium_text( space ).
      l_o_column->set_short_text( space ).
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.                            "#EC NO_HANDLER
  ENDTRY.
  "Setting hotspot
  TRY.
      l_o_col_tab ?= fp_columns->get_column( l_c_comfi ).
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.                            "#EC NO_HANDLER
  ENDTRY.
  TRY.
      CALL METHOD l_o_col_tab->set_cell_type
        EXPORTING
          value = if_salv_c_cell_type=>hotspot.
    CATCH cx_salv_data_error .                          "#EC NO_HANDLER
  ENDTRY.

*EOC For MOD-014
*BOC for MOD-018
*Inter Company Invoice Type
  TRY.
      l_o_column = fp_columns->get_column( l_c_cominvtyp ).
      l_o_column->set_long_text( TEXT-c70 ).
      l_o_column->set_medium_text( space ).
      l_o_column->set_short_text( space ).
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.                            "#EC NO_HANDLER
  ENDTRY.

*Inter Company Invoice Date
  TRY.
      l_o_column = fp_columns->get_column( l_c_cominvdate ).
      l_o_column->set_long_text( TEXT-c71 ).
      l_o_column->set_medium_text( space ).
      l_o_column->set_short_text( space ).
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.                            "#EC NO_HANDLER
  ENDTRY.

*Inter Company FI Type
  TRY.
      l_o_column = fp_columns->get_column( l_c_comfityp ).
      l_o_column->set_long_text( TEXT-c72 ).
      l_o_column->set_medium_text( space ).
      l_o_column->set_short_text( space ).
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.                            "#EC NO_HANDLER
  ENDTRY.

*Inter Company FI Date
  TRY.
      l_o_column = fp_columns->get_column( l_c_comfidate ).
      l_o_column->set_long_text( TEXT-c73 ).
      l_o_column->set_medium_text( space ).
      l_o_column->set_short_text( space ).
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.                            "#EC NO_HANDLER
  ENDTRY.
*EOC for MOD-018
ENDFORM.                    " F_SET_COLUMNS

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_GET_VISIT_LIST_DATA
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*     Fetching Visit List related data
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_get_visit_list_data.
*********************Internal Table Declaration***********************
  DATA:l_i_sld_item      TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_/dsd/sl_sld_item,
       l_i_rahd          TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_rahd,
       l_i_sld_flow      TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF /dsd/sl_sld_flow_s,
       l_i_vlh           TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_vlh,
       l_i_radel         TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_/dsd/hh_radelhd,
       l_i_raec          TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_raec,
***start of addition for MOD-002
       l_i_sld_item_temp TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_/dsd/sl_sld_item,
       l_i_status        TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_status_vlid.
***end of addition for MOD-002
*********************Field Symbols Declaration************************
  FIELD-SYMBOLS:<l_fs_sld_item> TYPE ty_/dsd/sl_sld_item,
                <l_fs_rahd>     TYPE ty_rahd,
                <l_fs_sld_flow> TYPE /dsd/sl_sld_flow_s.
*********************Constants Declaration****************************
  CONSTANTS:l_c_doc_typ   TYPE /dsd/hh_doc_typ VALUE '20',
            "Tour Document Type
            l_c_pre_type  TYPE /dsd/sl_pre_doc_type VALUE '998',
            "Settlement Document
            l_c_pre_type1 TYPE /dsd/sl_pre_doc_type VALUE '001'.
  "SD Shipment
**********************************************************************
  "Check HHT document
  IF s_hht[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT tour_id visit_id hh_delvry obj_typ plant bstkd bstdk
           FROM /dsd/hh_radelhd
           INTO TABLE l_i_radel
           WHERE bstkd IN s_hht.
    IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT tour_id          ##too_many_itab_fields    "#EC CI_NOFIELD
             FROM /dsd/hh_raec
             INTO TABLE l_i_raec
             WHERE paymt_descr IN s_hht.
      IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
        MESSAGE i521. "No tour ID can be determined
        LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    IF l_i_radel[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT sld_doc_id tour_id obj_typ obj_id          "#EC CI_NOFIRST
            FROM /dsd/sl_sld_item
            INTO TABLE l_i_sld_item
            FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_radel
            WHERE obj_typ = l_c_doc_typ AND tour_id = l_i_radel-tour_id.
      IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
        CLEAR l_i_sld_item.
      ENDIF.

    ENDIF.

    IF l_i_raec[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT sld_doc_id tour_id obj_typ obj_id          "#EC CI_NOFIRST
            FROM /dsd/sl_sld_item
            INTO TABLE l_i_sld_item
            FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_raec
            WHERE obj_typ = l_c_doc_typ AND tour_id = l_i_raec-tour_id.
      IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
        CLEAR l_i_sld_item.
      ENDIF.

    ENDIF.

    IF l_i_sld_item[] IS NOT INITIAL.
*   get Plant/route/settlement date based on visit list
      SELECT werks
             route
             creation_date
             visitlist
***start of addition for MOD-022
             idoc_number
***end of addition for MOD-022
             "BOC For MOD-015
             zzernam
             zzerzzt
             "EOC For MOD-015
        FROM /ccej/t_inb_stat                           "#EC CI_NOFIRST
                       INTO TABLE i_inb_stat
                       FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_sld_item
                        WHERE visitlist = l_i_sld_item-obj_id(10).
      IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
        CLEAR i_inb_stat.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
  IF s_hht[] IS INITIAL.
    IF s_tknum[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT sld_doc_id
             tour_id
             obj_typ
             obj_id
         FROM /dsd/sl_sld_item INTO TABLE l_i_sld_item  "#EC CI_NOFIRST
                WHERE obj_typ = l_c_doc_typ AND obj_id IN s_tknum.
      IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
        CLEAR l_i_sld_item.
      ENDIF.

      IF l_i_sld_item[] IS NOT INITIAL.
*   get Plant/route/settlement date based on visit list
        SELECT werks
               route
               creation_date
               visitlist
***start of addition for MOD-022
               idoc_number
***end of addition for MOD-022
              "BOC For MOD-015
               zzernam
               zzerzzt
             "EOC For MOD-015
          FROM /ccej/t_inb_stat                         "#EC CI_NOFIRST
                         INTO TABLE i_inb_stat
                         FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_sld_item
                          WHERE visitlist = l_i_sld_item-obj_id(10).
        IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
          CLEAR i_inb_stat.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ELSE.
      "Get visit list data based on plant/route/settlement date
      PERFORM f_get_vl_2  CHANGING i_inb_stat.
      IF i_inb_stat[] IS NOT INITIAL.
        SORT i_inb_stat BY vlid.
        SELECT sld_doc_id
           tour_id
           obj_typ
           obj_id
         FROM /dsd/sl_sld_item INTO TABLE l_i_sld_item  "#EC CI_NOFIRST
          FOR ALL ENTRIES IN i_inb_stat
          WHERE obj_typ = l_c_doc_typ
           AND obj_id = i_inb_stat-vlid.
        IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
          CLEAR l_i_sld_item.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  IF l_i_sld_item[] IS NOT INITIAL.
***start of addition for MOD-002
*    filter TOUR_ID based on FSR Status if FSR Status is filled
    IF s_status-low IS NOT INITIAL.
*      set TOUR_ID in format of TOURID /DSD/ST_TOURID- CHAR 12
      LOOP AT l_i_sld_item ASSIGNING <l_fs_sld_item>.
        <l_fs_sld_item>-tourid = <l_fs_sld_item>-tour_id.
      ENDLOOP.
      IF <l_fs_sld_item> IS ASSIGNED.
        UNASSIGN <l_fs_sld_item>.
      ENDIF.
      l_i_sld_item_temp[] = l_i_sld_item[].
      SORT l_i_sld_item_temp BY tourid.
      DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_i_sld_item_temp COMPARING tourid.
      IF l_i_sld_item_temp[] IS NOT INITIAL.
        SELECT tourid status_id FROM /dsd/st_status
                INTO TABLE l_i_status
                FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_sld_item_temp
                WHERE tourid EQ l_i_sld_item_temp-tourid
                  AND status_id EQ s_status-low.
        IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
          SORT l_i_status BY tourid.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

*      filter entries based on TOUR_ID
      LOOP AT l_i_sld_item ASSIGNING <l_fs_sld_item>.
        READ TABLE l_i_status TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS WITH KEY
                                      tourid = <l_fs_sld_item>-tourid
                                      BINARY SEARCH.
        IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
*    if entry does not exist then set TOURID as X to delete this record
          <l_fs_sld_item>-tourid = abap_true.
        ENDIF.
      ENDLOOP.
      IF <l_fs_sld_item> IS ASSIGNED.
        UNASSIGN <l_fs_sld_item>.
      ENDIF.
      DELETE l_i_sld_item WHERE tourid EQ abap_true.
    ENDIF.
    IF l_i_sld_item[] IS NOT INITIAL.
***end of addition for MOD-002

      SELECT tour_id obj_typ obj_id vehicle  driver ##too_many_itab_fields
                FROM /dsd/hh_rahd INTO TABLE l_i_rahd  FOR ALL ENTRIES IN
                l_i_sld_item WHERE tour_id = l_i_sld_item-tour_id
                             AND vehicle IN s_vehic
                             AND driver IN s_driv.
      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
        SORT l_i_sld_item BY tour_id.
        LOOP AT l_i_rahd ASSIGNING <l_fs_rahd>.
          <l_fs_rahd>-visitid =  <l_fs_rahd>-obj_id.
          READ TABLE l_i_sld_item ASSIGNING <l_fs_sld_item>
           WITH KEY tour_id = <l_fs_rahd>-tour_id BINARY SEARCH.
          IF  sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
            <l_fs_rahd>-sld_doc_id = <l_fs_sld_item>-sld_doc_id.
            UNASSIGN <l_fs_sld_item>.
          ENDIF.
        ENDLOOP.
        IF <l_fs_rahd> IS ASSIGNED.
          UNASSIGN <l_fs_rahd>.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
***start of addition for MOD-002
    ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-002
  ENDIF.

  IF l_i_rahd[] IS NOT INITIAL.

    SELECT vlid erdat
        "BOC For MOD-015
         erzet
         ernam
         "EOC For MOD-015
          FROM /dsd/vc_vlh
          INTO TABLE l_i_vlh
          FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_rahd
          WHERE vlid =  l_i_rahd-visitid.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR l_i_vlh.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT * FROM /dsd/sl_sld_flow INTO TABLE l_i_sld_flow
             FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_rahd
      WHERE ( tour_id = l_i_rahd-tour_id ) OR
            ( pre_doc_type =  l_c_pre_type AND
              pre_doc_id   = l_i_rahd-sld_doc_id ) OR
            ( pre_doc_type =  l_c_pre_type1 AND
              pre_doc_id   = l_i_rahd-obj_id ).
    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
      SORT l_i_sld_flow BY pre_doc_type pre_doc_id pre_doc_item
                           fol_doc_type fol_doc_id fol_doc_item
                           tour_id visit_id.
      DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_i_sld_flow COMPARING
      pre_doc_type pre_doc_id pre_doc_item fol_doc_type fol_doc_id
      fol_doc_item tour_id visit_id.
      SORT l_i_sld_flow BY pre_doc_type pre_doc_id.
      LOOP AT l_i_sld_flow ASSIGNING <l_fs_sld_flow>.
        CASE <l_fs_sld_flow>-pre_doc_type.
          WHEN l_c_pre_type. ""Update Tour ID based on Pre_doctype
            READ TABLE l_i_rahd ASSIGNING <l_fs_rahd> WITH KEY
         sld_doc_id = <l_fs_sld_flow>-pre_doc_id.       "#EC CI_READTBL
            IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
              <l_fs_sld_flow>-tour_id = <l_fs_rahd>-tour_id.
              UNASSIGN <l_fs_rahd>.
            ENDIF.
          WHEN l_c_pre_type1.  ""Update Tour ID based on Pre_doctype
            READ TABLE l_i_rahd ASSIGNING <l_fs_rahd> WITH KEY obj_id =
            <l_fs_sld_flow>-pre_doc_id.                 "#EC CI_READTBL
            IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
              <l_fs_sld_flow>-tour_id = <l_fs_rahd>-tour_id.
              UNASSIGN <l_fs_rahd>.
            ENDIF.
          WHEN OTHERS.
            "Do Nothing
        ENDCASE.
      ENDLOOP.
      IF <l_fs_sld_flow> IS ASSIGNED.
        UNASSIGN <l_fs_sld_flow>.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM f_sld_flow_logic USING l_i_sld_flow CHANGING l_i_rahd l_i_vlh.
ENDFORM.                    " F_GET_VISIT_LIST_DATA
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_GET_SHIPMENT_SALES
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*    Fetching sales replinshment data
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_get_sales .
*********************Internal Table Declaration***********************
  DATA:l_i_status      TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_/dsd/st_status,
       l_i_rahd        TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_/dsd/hh_rahd,
       l_i_racvhd      TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_/dsd/hh_racvhd,
       l_i_radelhd     TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_/dsd/hh_radelhd,
       l_i_radelcnd    TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_/dsd/hh_radelcnd,
       l_i_radelit     TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_/dsd/hh_radelit,
       l_i_radelit_tmp TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_/dsd/hh_radelit,
       l_i_makt        TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_makt,
       l_i_mara        TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_mara.
*********************Data Declaration*********************************
  DATA:l_v_index         TYPE sy-tabix,
       l_v_split         TYPE char17,
       l_v_money(2)      TYPE n,
       l_v_set(3)        TYPE n,
       l_v_money_code(4) TYPE n,
       l_wa_final1       TYPE ty_final1,
       l_v_value         TYPE bapicurr-bapicurr, "Contolling area currency
***start of addition for MOD-001
       l_v_yjva          TYPE /dsd/hh_amnt,
       l_v_yjcn          TYPE /dsd/hh_amnt,
       l_v_yjpr          TYPE /dsd/hh_amnt,
       l_v_yjp0          TYPE /dsd/hh_amnt.

  CONSTANTS: l_c_yjva       TYPE kschl VALUE 'YJVA',
             l_c_yjcn       TYPE kschl VALUE 'YJCN',
             l_c_yjpr       TYPE kschl VALUE 'YJPR',
             l_c_yjp0       TYPE kschl VALUE 'YJP0',
             l_c_visit_list TYPE /dsd/hh_doc_typ VALUE '20'.
***end of addition for MOD-001
***start of addition for MOD-002
  DATA: l_i_racvhd_temp TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_/dsd/hh_racvhd,
        l_i_kna1        TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_kna1,
***end of addition for MOD-002
***start of addition for MOD-011
        l_i_racocihd    TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_racocihd.
***end of addition for MOD-011

*********************Field Symbols Declaration************************
  FIELD-SYMBOLS:<l_fs_status>    TYPE  ty_/dsd/st_status,
                <l_fs_rahd>      TYPE  ty_/dsd/hh_rahd,
                <l_fs_racvhd>    TYPE  ty_/dsd/hh_racvhd,
                <l_fs_radelhd>   TYPE  ty_/dsd/hh_radelhd,
                <l_fs_radelcnd>  TYPE  ty_/dsd/hh_radelcnd,
                <l_fs_radelcnd1> TYPE  ty_/dsd/hh_radelcnd,
                <l_fs_radelit>   TYPE  ty_/dsd/hh_radelit,
                <l_fs_stat>      TYPE  ty_/ccej/t_inb_stat,
                <l_fs_makt>      TYPE  ty_makt,
                <l_fs_mara>      TYPE  ty_mara,
***start of addition for MOD-002
                <l_fs_kna1>      TYPE ty_kna1,
***end of addition for MOD-002
***start of addition for MOD-011
                <l_fs_racocihd>  TYPE ty_racocihd.
***end of addition for MOD-011
**********************************************************************
***start of addition for MOD-001
  CLEAR: l_v_yjva, l_v_yjcn, l_v_yjpr, l_v_yjp0.
***end of addition for MOD-001

  PERFORM f_status  CHANGING l_i_status.

  IF l_i_status[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    PERFORM f_rahd USING l_i_status CHANGING l_i_rahd.
  ENDIF.
  IF l_i_rahd[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    PERFORM f_racvhd USING l_i_rahd CHANGING l_i_racvhd.
***start of addition for MOD-002
    IF l_i_racvhd[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      l_i_racvhd_temp[] = l_i_racvhd[].
      SORT l_i_racvhd_temp BY custnr.
      DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_i_racvhd_temp COMPARING custnr.
      IF l_i_racvhd_temp[] IS NOT INITIAL.
        SELECT kunnr ktokd
***start of addition for MOD-011
               katr3
***end of addition for MOD-011
               katr4 /scl/equp_ownr FROM kna1
                         INTO TABLE l_i_kna1
                         FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_racvhd_temp
                         WHERE kunnr EQ l_i_racvhd_temp-custnr.
        IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
          SORT l_i_kna1 BY kunnr.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-002

    SELECT tour_id visit_id hh_delvry obj_typ plant bstkd bstdk
           FROM /dsd/hh_radelhd	INTO TABLE 	l_i_radelhd FOR ALL ENTRIES
           IN   l_i_rahd
           WHERE tour_id = l_i_rahd-tour_id.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR l_i_radelhd.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT tour_id visit_id hh_delvry hh_delvry_it cond amount
           FROM /dsd/hh_radelcnd  INTO TABLE l_i_radelcnd FOR ALL
           ENTRIES IN l_i_rahd
           WHERE tour_id = l_i_rahd-tour_id.

    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR l_i_radelcnd.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT tour_id  visit_id hh_delvry hh_delvry_it be_delvry_it matnr
           plant quan uom ta_code reason charg /scl/orig_qty
           FROM /dsd/hh_radelit INTO TABLE l_i_radelit FOR ALL ENTRIES
           IN  l_i_rahd
           WHERE tour_id = l_i_rahd-tour_id.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR l_i_radelit.
    ENDIF.

    IF l_i_radelit[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      l_i_radelit_tmp[] = l_i_radelit[].
      SORT l_i_radelit_tmp BY matnr.
      DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_i_radelit_tmp
             COMPARING matnr.
      IF l_i_radelit_tmp[] IS NOT INITIAL.
        SELECT matnr maktx FROM makt
             INTO TABLE l_i_makt FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_radelit_tmp
             WHERE matnr = l_i_radelit_tmp-matnr
             AND spras = sy-langu.
        IF sy-subrc NE 0.
          CLEAR l_i_makt.
        ENDIF.

        SELECT matnr /scl/pkggroup FROM mara
              INTO TABLE l_i_mara FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_radelit_tmp
              WHERE matnr = l_i_radelit_tmp-matnr.
        IF sy-subrc NE 0.
          CLEAR l_i_mara.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
***start of addition for MOD-011
    IF l_i_rahd[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT tour_id checker FROM /dsd/hh_racocihd
                             INTO TABLE l_i_racocihd
                             FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_rahd
                             WHERE tour_id EQ l_i_rahd-tour_id.
      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
        SORT l_i_racocihd BY tour_id.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-011
  ENDIF.

  SORT:
***start of commenting for MOD-001
*       l_i_radelit BY tour_id visit_id hh_delvry,
***end of commenting for MOD-001
***start of addition for MOD-001
       l_i_radelit BY tour_id visit_id hh_delvry hh_delvry_it,
***end of addition for MOD-001
       l_i_status BY tourid,
       l_i_rahd BY tour_id,
       l_i_radelhd BY tour_id visit_id hh_delvry,
       l_i_racvhd BY tour_id visit_id,
       l_i_radelcnd  BY tour_id visit_id hh_delvry hh_delvry_it,
       l_i_makt BY matnr,
       l_i_mara BY matnr.

  LOOP AT l_i_radelit ASSIGNING <l_fs_radelit>.

    l_wa_final1-tourid = <l_fs_radelit>-tour_id.
    l_wa_final1-visit_id = <l_fs_radelit>-visit_id.
    l_wa_final1-hh_delvry = <l_fs_radelit>-hh_delvry.
    l_wa_final1-hh_delvry_it = <l_fs_radelit>-hh_delvry_it.
    l_wa_final1-matnr = <l_fs_radelit>-matnr.
    l_wa_final1-quan = <l_fs_radelit>-quan.
    l_wa_final1-uom = <l_fs_radelit>-uom.
    l_wa_final1-ta_code = <l_fs_radelit>-ta_code.
    l_wa_final1-reason = <l_fs_radelit>-reason.
    l_wa_final1-charg = <l_fs_radelit>-charg.
    l_wa_final1-/scl/orig_qty = <l_fs_radelit>-/scl/orig_qty.

    READ TABLE l_i_makt ASSIGNING <l_fs_makt>
             WITH KEY matnr = l_wa_final1-matnr
             BINARY SEARCH.
    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
      l_wa_final1-maktx = <l_fs_makt>-maktx.
      UNASSIGN <l_fs_makt>.
    ENDIF.

    READ TABLE l_i_mara ASSIGNING <l_fs_mara>
         WITH KEY matnr = l_wa_final1-matnr
         BINARY SEARCH.

    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
      l_wa_final1-/scl/pkggroup  = <l_fs_mara>-/scl/pkggroup .
      UNASSIGN <l_fs_mara>.
    ENDIF.


    IF l_wa_final1-/scl/orig_qty IS  NOT INITIAL.
      CLEAR:l_v_money,l_v_set,l_v_money_code,l_v_split.
      l_v_split = l_wa_final1-/scl/orig_qty.
      PERFORM f_split_value CHANGING l_v_money l_v_set l_v_money_code
      l_v_split  . ""Split original qty as money type and split ID.
      l_wa_final1-money_type = l_v_money.
      l_wa_final1-set_id = l_v_set.
      l_wa_final1-money_code = l_v_money_code.
    ENDIF.

    READ TABLE l_i_status ASSIGNING <l_fs_status> WITH KEY tourid =
    <l_fs_radelit>-tour_id BINARY SEARCH.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      l_wa_final1-status_id = <l_fs_status>-status_id.
      l_wa_final1-shipment = <l_fs_status>-shipment.
      l_wa_final1-vlid     = <l_fs_status>-vlid.

      READ TABLE i_inb_stat ASSIGNING <l_fs_stat>
      WITH KEY vlid = <l_fs_status>-vlid  BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
        l_wa_final1-werks = <l_fs_stat>-werks.
        l_wa_final1-route = <l_fs_stat>-route.
        l_wa_final1-date = <l_fs_stat>-date.
        UNASSIGN <l_fs_stat>.
      ENDIF.
      UNASSIGN <l_fs_status>.
    ENDIF.


    READ TABLE l_i_rahd	ASSIGNING <l_fs_rahd> WITH KEY tour_id =
    <l_fs_radelit>-tour_id BINARY SEARCH.

    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      l_wa_final1-driver = <l_fs_rahd>-driver.
      l_wa_final1-credate  = <l_fs_rahd>-credate.

***start of addition for MOD-011
      READ TABLE l_i_racocihd ASSIGNING <l_fs_racocihd> WITH KEY
                                        tour_id = <l_fs_rahd>-tour_id
                                        BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
        l_wa_final1-scenario = <l_fs_racocihd>-checker+10(1).
        UNASSIGN <l_fs_racocihd>.
      ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-011
      UNASSIGN <l_fs_rahd>.
    ENDIF.

    READ TABLE l_i_radelhd ASSIGNING <l_fs_radelhd> WITH KEY tour_id =
    <l_fs_radelit>-tour_id visit_id = <l_fs_radelit>-visit_id
    hh_delvry = <l_fs_radelit>-hh_delvry BINARY SEARCH.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      l_wa_final1-obj_typ = <l_fs_radelhd>-obj_typ.
      l_wa_final1-plant1 = <l_fs_radelhd>-plant.
      l_wa_final1-ponumber = <l_fs_radelhd>-ponumber.
      l_wa_final1-podate  = <l_fs_radelhd>-podate.

      READ TABLE l_i_racvhd ASSIGNING <l_fs_racvhd> WITH KEY tour_id =
      <l_fs_radelit>-tour_id visit_id = <l_fs_radelit>-visit_id
                                       BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        l_wa_final1-custnr = <l_fs_racvhd>-custnr.
        UNASSIGN <l_fs_racvhd>.

***start of addition for MOD-002
        READ TABLE l_i_kna1 ASSIGNING <l_fs_kna1> WITH KEY
                                              kunnr = l_wa_final1-custnr
                                              BINARY SEARCH.
        IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
***start of addition for MOD-011
          l_wa_final1-katr3 = <l_fs_kna1>-katr3.
***end of addition for MOD-011
          l_wa_final1-katr4 = <l_fs_kna1>-katr4.
          l_wa_final1-/scl/equp_ownr = <l_fs_kna1>-/scl/equp_ownr.
          UNASSIGN <l_fs_kna1>.
        ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-002
      ENDIF.
      UNASSIGN <l_fs_radelhd>.
    ENDIF.


*Parallel Cursor technique
    READ TABLE l_i_radelcnd TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS WITH KEY tour_id =
    <l_fs_radelit>-tour_id  visit_id = <l_fs_radelit>-visit_id
                                         hh_delvry =
                                         <l_fs_radelit>-hh_delvry
                                         hh_delvry_it =
                                         <l_fs_radelit>-hh_delvry_it
                                         BINARY SEARCH.
    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
      l_v_index = sy-tabix.

      LOOP AT l_i_radelcnd ASSIGNING <l_fs_radelcnd> FROM l_v_index.
        IF <l_fs_radelcnd>-tour_id NE <l_fs_radelit>-tour_id OR
        <l_fs_radelcnd>-visit_id NE <l_fs_radelit>-visit_id
           OR <l_fs_radelcnd>-hh_delvry NE <l_fs_radelit>-hh_delvry OR
           <l_fs_radelcnd>-hh_delvry_it NE <l_fs_radelit>-hh_delvry_it.
          CLEAR l_v_index.
          EXIT.                                       "#EC CI_EXITININC
        ENDIF.

***start of commenting for MOD-001
*        l_wa_final1-cond = <l_fs_radelcnd>-cond.
***end of commenting for MOD-001
        l_wa_final1-amount = <l_fs_radelcnd>-amount.
        PERFORM f_currency_conversion
        CHANGING  l_wa_final1-amount   l_v_value.

***start of commenting for MOD-001
*        APPEND l_wa_final1 TO i_final1.
***end of commenting for MOD-001
***start of addition for MOD-001
        CASE <l_fs_radelcnd>-cond.
          WHEN l_c_yjva.
* do summation of YJVA output type value for the unique combination of
* TOUR_ID, VISIT_ID, HH_DELVRY, HH_DELVRY_IT
            l_v_yjva = l_v_yjva + l_wa_final1-amount.
*            COND to be filled for YJCN/YJVA
            l_wa_final1-cond = <l_fs_radelcnd>-cond.
          WHEN l_c_yjcn.
* do summation of YJCN output type value for the unique combination of
* TOUR_ID, VISIT_ID, HH_DELVRY, HH_DELVRY_IT
            l_v_yjcn = l_v_yjcn + l_wa_final1-amount.
*            COND to be filled for YJCN/YJVA
            l_wa_final1-cond = <l_fs_radelcnd>-cond.
          WHEN l_c_yjpr. "YJPR
* do summation of YJPR output type value for the unique combination of
* TOUR_ID, VISIT_ID, HH_DELVRY, HH_DELVRY_IT
            l_v_yjpr = l_v_yjpr + l_wa_final1-amount.
          WHEN l_c_yjp0. "YJP0
* do summation of YJP0 output type value for the unique combination of
* TOUR_ID, VISIT_ID, HH_DELVRY, HH_DELVRY_IT
            l_v_yjp0 = l_v_yjp0 + l_wa_final1-amount.
          WHEN OTHERS.
*            do nothing
        ENDCASE.
***end of addition for MOD-001

        "l_Wa_final1 not be cleared to use it again.
      ENDLOOP.
      IF <l_fs_radelcnd> IS ASSIGNED.
        UNASSIGN <l_fs_radelcnd>.
      ENDIF.

***start of addition for MOD-001
* it is assumed that for a unique record only one out of YJCN/YJVA would
* be present
      IF l_v_yjva IS NOT INITIAL.
        l_wa_final1-amount = l_v_yjva.
      ELSEIF l_v_yjcn IS NOT INITIAL.
        l_wa_final1-amount = l_v_yjcn.
      ENDIF.

*      assign values of YJPR and YJP0
      IF l_v_yjpr IS NOT INITIAL.
        l_wa_final1-yjpr = l_v_yjpr.
*        set flag to denote at least one YJPR exists
        v_flag1 = abap_true.
      ENDIF.

      IF l_v_yjp0 IS NOT INITIAL.
        l_wa_final1-yjp0 = l_v_yjp0.
*        set flag to denote at least one YJP0 exists
        v_flag2 = abap_true.
      ENDIF.

*      assign value in SALES_AMOUNT
      IF l_wa_final1-obj_typ EQ l_c_visit_list. "'20'
        l_wa_final1-sales_amt = l_wa_final1-quan * l_wa_final1-amount.
      ENDIF.
      APPEND l_wa_final1 TO i_final1.
      CLEAR: l_v_yjva, l_v_yjcn, l_v_yjpr, l_v_yjp0.
      ""l_wa_final1 not be cleared to use it again.
***end of addition for MOD-001
    ELSE.
      APPEND l_wa_final1 TO i_final1.
      ""l_wa_final1 not be cleared to use it again.
    ENDIF.

    AT END OF hh_delvry.
*        add header condition type and amount
      READ TABLE l_i_radelcnd ASSIGNING <l_fs_radelcnd1> WITH KEY
   tour_id = <l_fs_radelit>-tour_id  visit_id = <l_fs_radelit>-visit_id
   hh_delvry = <l_fs_radelit>-hh_delvry hh_delvry_it = space "#EC WARNOK
                                       BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
        l_wa_final1-cond = <l_fs_radelcnd1>-cond.
        l_wa_final1-amount = <l_fs_radelcnd1>-amount.
        PERFORM f_currency_conversion
               CHANGING  l_wa_final1-amount   l_v_value.
        CLEAR:l_wa_final1-hh_delvry_it,l_wa_final1-matnr,
        l_wa_final1-plant1 ,l_wa_final1-quan,l_wa_final1-uom,
        l_wa_final1-ta_code,l_wa_final1-reason,
        l_wa_final1-charg,l_wa_final1-/scl/orig_qty,
        l_wa_final1-money_type,l_wa_final1-set_id,
        l_wa_final1-money_code,
***start of addition for MOD-001
        l_wa_final1-yjpr, l_wa_final1-yjp0, l_wa_final1-sales_amt.
***end of addition for MOD-001
        APPEND l_wa_final1 TO i_final1.
        CLEAR l_wa_final1.
        UNASSIGN <l_fs_radelcnd1>.
      ENDIF.
    ENDAT.

    CLEAR l_wa_final1.
  ENDLOOP.
  IF <l_fs_radelit> IS ASSIGNED.
    UNASSIGN <l_fs_radelit>.
  ENDIF.
  SORT i_final1 BY hh_delvry hh_delvry_it.

*BOC For MOD-014
*Filter Data by Equipment Owner
  IF s_equp IS NOT INITIAL.
    DELETE i_final1 WHERE /scl/equp_ownr NOT IN s_equp.
  ENDIF.

*Filter by customer
  IF s_kunnr IS NOT INITIAL.
    DELETE i_final1 WHERE custnr NOT IN s_kunnr.
  ENDIF.

*Filter by business extension
  IF s_katr4 IS NOT INITIAL.
    DELETE i_final1 WHERE katr4 NOT IN s_katr4.
  ENDIF.
*EOC For MOD-014

*BOC For MOD-019
*Filter by HHT No
  IF s_hht IS NOT INITIAL.
    DELETE i_final1 WHERE ponumber NOT IN s_hht.
  ENDIF.

*Filter by Object Type
  IF s_objtyp IS NOT INITIAL.
    DELETE i_final1 WHERE obj_typ NOT IN s_objtyp.
  ENDIF.
*EOC For MOD-019


ENDFORM.                    " F_GET_SHIPMENT_SALES
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_OUTPUT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Screen Modification based on Radio Button
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_output .


  "Disable shipment radio button
  PERFORM f_disable_ship.

***start of addition for MOD-025
  PERFORM f_clear_screen.
***end of addition for MOD-025

  IF ( rb_ship EQ abap_true  OR rb_visi EQ abap_true ) AND rb_driv
     EQ abap_true.
    PERFORM f_enable_shipment.
    "Modify screen based on Shipment/Visit list and Tour details

  ELSEIF ( rb_ship EQ abap_true  OR rb_visi EQ abap_true ) AND rb_visit
       EQ abap_true.
    PERFORM f_enable_shipment.
    "Modify screen based on Shipment/Visit list and Visit details

  ELSEIF ( rb_ship EQ abap_true  OR rb_visi EQ abap_true ) AND rb_slrep
   EQ abap_true.
    PERFORM f_enable_shipment.
    "Modify screen based on Shipment/Visit list and Sales replenishment

  ELSEIF ( rb_ship EQ abap_true OR rb_visi EQ abap_true ) AND rb_patdt
  EQ abap_true.
    PERFORM f_enable_shipment.
    "Modify screen based on Shipment/Visit list and Sales replenishment

  ELSEIF ( rb_ship EQ abap_true OR rb_visi EQ abap_true ) AND rb_check
  EQ abap_true.
    PERFORM f_enable_shipment.
    "Modify screen based on Shipment/Visit list and Check Out/In

  ELSEIF ( rb_ship EQ abap_true OR rb_visi EQ abap_true ) AND rb_money
  EQ abap_true.
    PERFORM f_enable_shipment.
    "Modify screen based on Shipment/Visit list and Money differences

  ELSEIF ( rb_ship EQ abap_true OR rb_visi EQ abap_true ) AND rb_quan EQ
  abap_true.
    PERFORM f_enable_shipment.
    "Modify screen based on Shipment/Visit list and Quantity differences

  ELSEIF rb_visi EQ abap_true AND rb_fsdoc EQ abap_true.
    PERFORM f_enable_visit.
    "Modify screen based on Visit list and FSR Documents
  ELSEIF rb_ship EQ abap_true AND rb_fsdoc EQ abap_true.
    PERFORM f_enable_ship.
    "Modify screen based on Shipment and FSR Documents
***start of addition for MOD-013
  ELSEIF rb_cash EQ abap_true.
    PERFORM f_enable_cash.
***end of addition for MOD-013
  ENDIF.

*BOC MOD-018
  PERFORM f_enable_disable_fields.
*EOC MOD-018

ENDFORM.                    " F_OUTPUT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_ENABLE_SHIPMENT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      Screen Modify group M1/M2/M3 wise
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_enable_shipment.
***start of commenting for MOD-025
*  CLEAR:s_tplst[],s_route[],s_vehic[],s_driv[],s_status[],s_erdat[],
*        s_hht[],
****start of addition for MOD-013
*       s_kunnr[], s_equp[], s_katr4[], s_katr3[], s_vistyp[], s_sum_st[].
***end of addition for MOD-013
*       s_light[]. "***added for MOD-024
***end of commenting for MOD-025
*********************Constants Declaration****************************
  CONSTANTS:l_c_grp1      TYPE screen-group1 VALUE 'M1',
            l_c_grp2      TYPE screen-group1 VALUE 'M2',
            l_c_grp3      TYPE screen-group1 VALUE 'M3',
            l_c_grp4      TYPE screen-group1 VALUE 'M4',
            l_c_date      TYPE screen-name   VALUE 'S_DATE-HIGH',
            l_c_push      TYPE screen-name   VALUE
            '%_S_DATE_%_APP_%-VALU_PUSH',
            "BOC For MOD-014
            l_c_katr3     TYPE screen-name VALUE 'S_KATR3-LOW',
            l_c_text      TYPE screen-name VALUE '%_S_KATR3_%_APP_%-TEXT',
            "EOC For MOD-014
            "BOC For MOD-017
            l_c_light     TYPE screen-name VALUE 'S_LIGHT-LOW',
            l_c_light_txt TYPE screen-name VALUE '%_S_LIGHT_%_APP_%-TEXT'.
  "EOC For MOD-017

*********************Screen Modification******************************
  IF rb_driv EQ abap_true.
    LOOP AT SCREEN.
      IF ( screen-group1 EQ l_c_grp1 ) OR ( screen-group1 EQ l_c_grp2 )
         OR ( screen-group1 EQ l_c_grp3 AND
*       BOC MOD-017
***start of commenting for MOD-025
*        ( screen-name NE l_c_light AND screen-name NE l_c_light_txt )
***end of commenting for MOD-025
***start of addition for MOD-025
        screen-name NS c_s_light
***end of addition for MOD-025
*       EOC MOD-017
            )
***start of addition for MOD-013
        OR ( screen-group1 EQ c_grp5 )
***end of addition for MOD-013
        "BOC For MOD-014
         OR ( screen-group1 EQ c_grp7 )
        "EOC For MOD-014
        OR ( screen-group1 EQ c_grp8 )."MOD-016
        screen-invisible = 1.
        screen-input = 0.
        MODIFY SCREEN.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
  ELSE.
*    CLEAR:s_date[]. "***commented for MOD-025
    LOOP AT SCREEN.
      IF ( screen-group1 EQ l_c_grp1 ) OR ( screen-group1 EQ l_c_grp2 )
         OR ( screen-group1 EQ l_c_grp3 AND
*       BOC MOD-017
***start of commenting for MOD-025
*        ( screen-name NE l_c_light AND screen-name NE l_c_light_txt )
***end of commenting for MOD-025
***start of addition for MOD-025
        screen-name NS c_s_light
***end of addition for MOD-025
         )
*       EOC MOD-017
         OR ( screen-group1 EQ l_c_grp4 )
*Begin of comment MOD-014
*         OR ( screen-name EQ l_c_date )
*         OR ( screen-name EQ l_c_push )
*End of comment MOD-014
***start of addition for MOD-013
*        OR ( screen-group1 EQ c_grp5 )"Comment MOD-014
***end of addition for MOD-013
        "BOC For MOD-014
         OR ( screen-group1 EQ c_grp6 )
         OR ( screen-group1 EQ c_grp7
         AND rb_quan EQ abap_false )
        "EOC For MOD-014
        "BOC For MOD-016
          OR ( screen-group1 EQ c_grp8
         AND rb_patdt EQ abap_false ).
        "EOC For MOD-016
        screen-invisible = 1.
        screen-input = 0.
        MODIFY SCREEN.
* BOC For MOD-014
      ELSEIF ( screen-group1 EQ c_grp5 )
      AND ( rb_visit NE abap_true
      AND rb_slrep NE abap_true
      AND rb_patdt NE abap_true
      AND rb_cash NE abap_true ).
        screen-invisible = 1.
        screen-input = 0.
        MODIFY SCREEN.
        "Excluding business type for visit details/sales repl.
        "and payment details
      ELSEIF ( screen-name = l_c_katr3
      OR screen-name = l_c_text
***start of addition for MOD-013
      OR screen-name CS c_s_vistyp
      OR screen-name CS c_s_sum_st_name
***end of addition for MOD-013
        )
      AND ( rb_visit EQ abap_true
      OR rb_slrep EQ abap_true
      OR rb_patdt EQ abap_true ).
        screen-invisible = 1.
        screen-input = 0.
        MODIFY SCREEN.
* EOC For MOD-014
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " F_ENABLE_SHIPMENT

*     Screen Modify group M1 wise
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_enable_visit .
***start of commenting for MOD-025
*  CLEAR:s_tplst[],s_route[],s_status[],s_erdat[],
*          "BOC For MOD-014
*        s_kunnr[],s_katr4[].
***end of commenting for MOD-025
  "EOC For MOD-014
*********************Constants Declaration****************************
  CONSTANTS:l_c_grp1 TYPE screen-group1 VALUE 'M1',
            "BOC For MOD-014
            l_c_equp TYPE screen-name VALUE 'S_EQUP-LOW',
            l_c_text TYPE screen-name VALUE '%_S_EQUP_%_APP_%-TEXT'.
  "EOC For MOD-014

*********************Screen Modification******************************
  LOOP AT SCREEN.
    IF screen-group1 EQ l_c_grp1
***start of addition for MOD-013
        OR  ( screen-group1 EQ c_grp5
      "BOC For MOD-014
      AND screen-name NE l_c_equp
      AND screen-name NE l_c_text )
      "EOC For MOD-014
***end of addition for MOD-013
        "BOC For MOD-014
         OR ( screen-group1 EQ c_grp6 )
         OR ( screen-group1 EQ c_grp7 )
      "EOC For MOD-014
      "BOC For MOD-016
        OR ( screen-group1 EQ c_grp8 ).
      "EOC For MOD-016
      screen-invisible = 1.
      screen-input = 0.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
ENDFORM.                    " F_ENABLE_VISIT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_SET_COLUMNS1
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Sales Info. Columns description
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_set_columns1  USING  fp_columns TYPE REF TO cl_salv_columns_table
.
*********************Data Declaration*********************************
  DATA:l_o_column TYPE REF TO cl_salv_column.
*********************Constants Declaration****************************
  CONSTANTS:l_c_id        TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'TOURID',
            l_c_otype     TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'OBJ_TYP', "Object type
            l_c_ship      TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'SHIPMENT',
            l_c_visit_id  TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'VLID',
            l_c_date      TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'CREDATE',
            l_c_status    TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'STATUS_ID',
            l_c_delvry    TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'HH_DELVRY',
            l_c_po        TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'PONUMBER',
            l_c_con       TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'COND',
            l_c_amnt      TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'AMOUNT',
            l_c_del_it    TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'HH_DELVRY_IT',
            l_c_unit      TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'UOM',
            l_c_code      TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'TA_CODE',
            l_c_rson      TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'REASON',
            l_c_qty       TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'QUAN',
            l_c_type      TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'MONEY_TYPE',
            l_c_sid       TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'SET_ID',
            l_c_mcode     TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'MONEY_CODE',
            l_c_orig_qty  TYPE lvc_fname VALUE '/SCL/ORIG_QTY',
            l_c_settmnt   TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'DATE', "Settlmnt date
            l_c_pkgrp     TYPE lvc_fname VALUE '/SCL/PKGGROUP', "Package grp
***start of addition for MOD-001
            l_c_yjpr      TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'YJPR', "Amount YJPR
            l_c_yjp0      TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'YJP0', "Amount YJP0
            l_c_sales_amt TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'SALES_AMT', "Sales Amount
***end of addition for MOD-001
***start of addition for MOD-011
            l_c_scenario  TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'SCENARIO'. "Scenario
***end of addition for MOD-011

  TRY.
      l_o_column = fp_columns->get_column( l_c_id ).

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_SLD_FLOW_LOGIC
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Documents Selected based on Document type i.e sales order,material
* document and accounting document.
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_sld_flow_logic  USING  fp_sld_flow TYPE tb_t_sld_flow
                       CHANGING fp_rahd TYPE tb_t_rahd
                              fp_vlh  TYPE tb_t_vlh.
*********************Internal Table Declaration***********************
  DATA:l_i_vbak    TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_vbak,
       l_i_vbak1   TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_vbak1,
       l_i_sd      TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_sd,
       l_i_mat     TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_mat,
       l_i_acc     TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_acc,
       l_i_bkpf    TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_bkpf,
       l_i_mkpf    TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_mkpf,          "#EC NEEDED
       l_i_vbkd    TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF  ty_vbkd,
       l_i_raec    TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_raec,
       l_i_sld_tmp TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF /dsd/sl_sld_flow_s,
       l_i_status  TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF  ty_/dsd/st_status,
       l_i_option  TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF selopt,
***start of addition for MOD-002
       l_i_kna1    TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_kna,
       l_i_racvhd  TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_/dsd/hh_racvhd,
***end of addition for MOD-002

***start of addition for MOD-003
       l_i_sd_tmp  TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_sd.
***end  of addition for MOD-003

***start of addition for MOD-005
  DATA:l_i_vbak_tmp TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_vbak.
***start of addition for MOD-005
  DATA:l_i_final      TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_final, "MOD-009
       l_i_radelhd
                 TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_/dsd/hh_radelhd,"MOD-009
       l_i_racvhd_tmp
                 TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_/dsd/hh_racvhd. "MOD-009
*********************Work Area Declaration****************************
  DATA:l_wa_sd   TYPE ty_sd,
       l_wa_mat  TYPE ty_mat,
       l_wa_acc  TYPE ty_acc,
       l_wa_bkpf TYPE ty_bkpf,                              "#EC NEEDED
       l_wa_mkpf TYPE ty_mkpf,                              "#EC NEEDED
       l_wa_vbkd TYPE  ty_vbkd,
       l_wa_raec TYPE ty_raec,                              "#EC NEEDED
       l_wa_vlh  TYPE ty_vlh.
*********************Data Declaration*********************************
  DATA:l_v_seqno  TYPE i,
       l_v_obj_id TYPE /dsd/sl_pre_doc_id,                  "#EC NEEDED
***start of addition for MOD-002
       l_v_flag   TYPE abap_bool.
***end of addition for MOD-002

***start of addition for MOD-003
  DATA:l_wa_sld_tmp TYPE /dsd/sl_sld_flow_s,
***end  of addition for MOD-003
       l_i_radelit  TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_radelit,
       l_v_counter  TYPE i.
*********************Constants Declaration****************************
  CONSTANTS:
    "Sales type
    l_c_type  TYPE /dsd/sl_fol_doc_type VALUE '12',
    "Accounting type
    l_c_type1 TYPE /dsd/sl_fol_doc_type VALUE '15',
    "Material Doc. type
    l_c_type2 TYPE /dsd/sl_fol_doc_type VALUE '18',
    "DSD
    l_c_dsd   TYPE katr3 VALUE '01', "MOD-009
    "FSV
    l_c_fsv   TYPE katr3 VALUE '04', "MOD-009
    l_c_10    TYPE /dsd/hh_del_doctyp VALUE '10', "Delivery
    l_c_20    TYPE /dsd/hh_del_doctyp VALUE '20'. "Order

*********************Field Symbols Declaration************************
  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <l_fs_sld_flow> TYPE /dsd/sl_sld_flow_s,
                 <l_fs_rahd>     TYPE ty_rahd,
                 <l_fs_vbak>     TYPE ty_vbak,
                 <l_fs_vbak1>    TYPE ty_vbak1,
                 <l_fs_sd>       TYPE ty_sd,
                 <l_fs_racvhd>   TYPE ty_/dsd/hh_racvhd,
                 <l_fs_status>   TYPE   ty_/dsd/st_status,
                 <l_fs_stat>     TYPE  ty_/ccej/t_inb_stat,
                 <l_fs_radelhd>  TYPE  ty_/dsd/hh_radelhd. "MOD-009
**********************************************************************
  IF fp_rahd[] IS NOT INITIAL.

    SELECT tourid
           status_id
           shipment
           vlid  FROM /dsd/st_status
                INTO TABLE l_i_status
                FOR ALL ENTRIES IN fp_rahd
                WHERE tourid = fp_rahd-tour_id(12).
    IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
      CLEAR l_i_status.
    ENDIF.
    SORT:fp_rahd BY tour_id.
  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT fp_sld_flow ASSIGNING <l_fs_sld_flow>.
    CASE <l_fs_sld_flow>-fol_doc_type.
        "When Sales Document Selected
      WHEN l_c_type.
        l_wa_sd-vbeln = <l_fs_sld_flow>-fol_doc_id.
        l_wa_sd-pre_doc_id = <l_fs_sld_flow>-pre_doc_id.
        READ TABLE fp_rahd  ASSIGNING <l_fs_rahd> WITH KEY tour_id =
        <l_fs_sld_flow>-tour_id BINARY SEARCH.
        IF  sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
          l_wa_sd-obj_id = <l_fs_rahd>-obj_id.
          l_wa_sd-tour_id = <l_fs_rahd>-tour_id.
          l_wa_sd-visit_id = <l_fs_sld_flow>-visit_id.
          l_wa_sd-vehicle = <l_fs_rahd>-vehicle.
          l_wa_sd-driver = <l_fs_rahd>-driver.
          APPEND l_wa_sd TO l_i_sd.
          CLEAR l_wa_sd.
          UNASSIGN <l_fs_rahd>.
        ENDIF.
        "When Accounting Document Selected
      WHEN l_c_type1.
        l_wa_acc-belnr = <l_fs_sld_flow>-fol_doc_id.
        l_wa_acc-pre_doc_id = <l_fs_sld_flow>-pre_doc_id.
        READ TABLE fp_rahd  ASSIGNING <l_fs_rahd> WITH KEY tour_id =
        <l_fs_sld_flow>-tour_id BINARY SEARCH.
        IF  sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
          l_wa_acc-obj_id  = <l_fs_rahd>-obj_id.
          l_wa_acc-vehicle = <l_fs_rahd>-vehicle.
          l_wa_acc-driver = <l_fs_rahd>-driver.
          l_wa_acc-tour_id = <l_fs_rahd>-tour_id.
          l_wa_acc-visit_id = <l_fs_sld_flow>-visit_id.
          l_wa_acc-xblnr  = <l_fs_rahd>-obj_id.
          APPEND l_wa_acc TO l_i_acc.
          CLEAR l_wa_acc.
          UNASSIGN <l_fs_rahd>.
        ENDIF.
        "When Material Document Selected
      WHEN l_c_type2.
        l_wa_mat-mblnr = <l_fs_sld_flow>-fol_doc_id.
        l_wa_mat-pre_doc_id = <l_fs_sld_flow>-pre_doc_id.
        READ TABLE fp_rahd  ASSIGNING <l_fs_rahd> WITH KEY tour_id =
        <l_fs_sld_flow>-tour_id BINARY SEARCH.
        IF  sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
          l_wa_mat-obj_id  = <l_fs_rahd>-obj_id.
          l_wa_mat-tour_id = <l_fs_rahd>-tour_id.
          l_wa_mat-visit_id = <l_fs_sld_flow>-visit_id.
          l_wa_mat-vehicle = <l_fs_rahd>-vehicle.
          l_wa_mat-driver = <l_fs_rahd>-driver.
          APPEND l_wa_mat TO l_i_mat.
          CLEAR l_wa_mat.
          UNASSIGN <l_fs_rahd>.
        ENDIF.
      WHEN OTHERS.
        "Do nothing
    ENDCASE.
  ENDLOOP.
  IF <l_fs_sld_flow> IS ASSIGNED.
    UNASSIGN <l_fs_sld_flow>.
  ENDIF.

***start of addition for MOD-003
  PERFORM f_get_presales CHANGING l_i_sd
                                 fp_rahd.
***end  of addition for MOD-003

  "Move sales flow data to temporary table
  l_i_sld_tmp[] = fp_sld_flow[].
***start of addition for MOD-003
  "Move to temporary table
  l_i_sd_tmp[] = l_i_sd[].
  IF l_i_sd_tmp[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    SORT :l_i_sd_tmp BY tour_id visit_id,
          l_i_sld_tmp BY tour_id visit_id.
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_i_sd_tmp
       COMPARING tour_id visit_id.
    LOOP AT l_i_sd_tmp INTO l_wa_sd.
      "Checking if DSD: Document Flow table
      "not contain any entry based on Tour ID
      "and Visit ID
      READ TABLE l_i_sld_tmp TRANSPORTING
           NO FIELDS WITH KEY tour_id = l_wa_sd-tour_id
                              visit_id = l_wa_sd-visit_id.
*                              BINARY SEARCH."--MOD-032
      IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
        l_wa_sld_tmp-tour_id = l_wa_sd-tour_id.
        l_wa_sld_tmp-visit_id = l_wa_sd-visit_id.
        APPEND l_wa_sld_tmp TO l_i_sld_tmp.
        CLEAR:l_wa_sld_tmp,l_wa_sd.
      ENDIF.
      CLEAR l_wa_sd.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.
***end  of addition for MOD-003
  IF  l_i_sld_tmp[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    SORT l_i_sld_tmp BY tour_id visit_id.
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_i_sld_tmp COMPARING
           tour_id visit_id.
    SELECT tour_id
           visit_id
           custnr
           man_proc "MOD-009
           FROM /dsd/hh_racvhd
          INTO TABLE i_racvhd
          FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_sld_tmp
          WHERE tour_id = l_i_sld_tmp-tour_id AND
               visit_id = l_i_sld_tmp-visit_id.

    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR i_racvhd.
***start of addition for MOD-002
    ELSE.
      CLEAR: l_i_racvhd, i_kna1.
      l_i_racvhd[] = i_racvhd[].
      SORT l_i_racvhd BY custnr.
      DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_i_racvhd COMPARING custnr.
      IF l_i_racvhd[] IS NOT INITIAL.
*        fetch KATR4 Business Type Extension
        SELECT kunnr
               katr3 "MOD-009
               katr4
               /scl/equp_ownr "MOD-014
               FROM kna1
                           INTO TABLE i_kna1
                           FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_racvhd
                           WHERE kunnr EQ l_i_racvhd-custnr.
        IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
          SORT i_kna1 BY kunnr.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-002
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  IF l_i_sd[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT vbeln erdat
          "BOC For MOD-015
           erzet
           ernam
          "EOC For MOD-015
           auart    ##too_many_itab_fields
***start of addition for MOD-029
           vkorg
***end of addition for MOD-029
           FROM vbak INTO TABLE l_i_vbak FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_sd
           WHERE vbeln EQ l_i_sd-vbeln.

    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR l_i_vbak.
***start of addition for MOD-029
    ELSE.
      i_vbak[] = l_i_vbak[].
      SORT i_vbak BY vbeln.
***end of addition for MOD-029
    ENDIF.


    IF l_i_vbak[] IS NOT INITIAL.
***start of addition for MOD-005
      l_i_vbak_tmp[] = l_i_vbak[].
      SORT l_i_vbak_tmp BY auart.
      DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM
        l_i_vbak_tmp COMPARING auart.

      IF l_i_vbak_tmp[] IS NOT INITIAL.
        SELECT auart
               lfarv
               fkarv FROM tvak INTO TABLE i_tvak
               FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_vbak_tmp
         WHERE auart EQ l_i_vbak_tmp-auart.
        IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
          CLEAR i_tvak.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-005

***start of comment for MOD-005
*      SELECT auart
*             lfarv
*             fkarv FROM tvak INTO TABLE i_tvak
*             FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_vbak
*       WHERE auart EQ l_i_vbak-auart.
*      IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
*        CLEAR i_tvak.
*      ENDIF.
***end of comment for MOD-005
      " Populate the particular order type
      PERFORM f_order_type CHANGING l_i_option.

      SELECT vbeln
             auart
             kunnr FROM vbak INTO TABLE l_i_vbak1
             FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_vbak
             WHERE vbeln EQ l_i_vbak-vbeln
             AND auart IN l_i_option.
      IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
        CLEAR l_i_vbak1.
***start of addition for MOD-002
      ELSE.
        IF l_i_vbak1[] IS NOT INITIAL.
          SELECT kunnr katr4 FROM kna1
                             INTO TABLE l_i_kna1
                             FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_vbak1
                             WHERE kunnr EQ l_i_vbak1-kunnr.
          IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
            SORT l_i_kna1 BY kunnr.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-002
      ENDIF.

    ENDIF.

    SELECT vbeln bstkd FROM vbkd
            INTO TABLE l_i_vbkd FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_sd
            WHERE vbeln EQ l_i_sd-vbeln
            AND bstkd IN s_hht.

    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR l_i_vbkd.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  IF l_i_acc[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT bukrs belnr gjahr blart budat
        "BOC For MOD-015
         cputm
         usnam
        "EOC For MOD-015
         xblnr
         FROM bkpf INTO TABLE l_i_bkpf FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_acc
         WHERE belnr EQ l_i_acc-belnr AND xblnr EQ l_i_acc-xblnr.

    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR l_i_bkpf.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT tour_id paymt paymt_descr cardnr oi_csh_post
         FROM /dsd/hh_raec INTO TABLE l_i_raec FOR ALL ENTRIES IN
         l_i_acc
         WHERE tour_id EQ l_i_acc-tour_id
          AND oi_csh_post EQ l_i_acc-belnr

FORM f_build_itab  USING  fp_seqno TYPE i
                          fp_vbak TYPE ty_vbak.
*********************Internal Table Declaration***********************
  DATA:l_i_vbfa       TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF vbfa,
       l_i_vbfa_inv   TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF vbfa, "***added for MOD-010
*--Start of addition for MOD-023
       l_i_vbfa_tmp   TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF vbfa,
       l_i_vbfa_tmp_1 TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF vbfa.
*--End of addition for MOD-023
*********************Work Area Declaration****************************
  DATA:l_wa_vbco6 TYPE vbco6,
       l_wa_vbfa  TYPE vbfa,
       l_v_posnr  TYPE posnv.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS <l_fs_knb1> TYPE ty_knb1. "***added for MOD-029
*********************Constants Declaration***************************
  CONSTANTS:
    l_c_del    TYPE vbtypl_n  VALUE 'J', "delivery type
    l_c_del1   TYPE vbtypl_n  VALUE 'T', "Return delivery type
    l_c_mat    TYPE vbtypl_n  VALUE 'R', "material document type
    l_c_inv    TYPE vbtypl_n  VALUE 'M', "Invoice type
    "BOC for MOD-014
    l_c_cominv TYPE vbtypl_n VALUE '5', "Intercom. Invice type
    "EOC for MOD-014
    l_c_debit  TYPE vbtypl_n  VALUE 'P', "Debit Memo
    l_c_credit TYPE vbtypl_n  VALUE 'O', "Credit Memo
    l_c_dot    TYPE c VALUE '.', "Dot
    "BOC for MOD-014
    l_c_equ    TYPE /scl/mdmd_equp_own VALUE '05'. "Equipment owner
  "EOC for MOD-014
**********************************************************************
  CLEAR:l_i_vbfa[].
  wa_final-vbeln = fp_vbak-vbeln.
  "BOC For MOD-015
*--Time zone
  IF fp_vbak-ernam NOT IN r_user.
    PERFORM f_get_local_time
               USING   fp_vbak-erzet
               CHANGING  fp_vbak-erdat.
  ENDIF.
  "EOC For MOD-015
  wa_final-erdat_o = fp_vbak-erdat.
  CONCATENATE wa_final-erdat_o+6(2) wa_final-erdat_o+4(2)
     wa_final-erdat_o(4) INTO wa_final-erdat_o
         SEPARATED BY l_c_dot.
  wa_final-auart = fp_vbak-auart.
  wa_final-seqno = fp_seqno.

  IF fp_vbak-vbeln IS NOT INITIAL.
    "For multiple settlement date
    IF i_vbfa[] IS INITIAL.
      l_wa_vbco6-vbeln = fp_vbak-vbeln.
      PERFORM f_order_flow  USING  l_wa_vbco6
                            CHANGING l_i_vbfa.
    ELSE.
      "For single settlement date
      LOOP AT i_vbfa INTO l_wa_vbfa USING KEY s1
                          WHERE vbelv = fp_vbak-vbeln.
        APPEND l_wa_vbfa TO l_i_vbfa.
        CLEAR:l_wa_vbfa.
      ENDLOOP.
    ENDIF.
***start of addition for MOD-010
    IF l_i_vbfa[] IS NOT INITIAL.
*      Filter relevant Invoices- which have FKSTO and SFAKN as blank
      PERFORM f_filter_inv USING l_i_vbfa
                           CHANGING l_i_vbfa_inv.
    ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-010
  ENDIF.

*--Start of commenting for MOD-023
*  READ TABLE l_i_vbfa INTO l_wa_vbfa WITH KEY vbelv = fp_vbak-vbeln
*  vbtyp_n = l_c_del. " Fetch delivery no.  "#EC CI_READTBL
*  IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
*    wa_final-vbeln_d = l_wa_vbfa-vbeln.
*  ELSE.
*    READ TABLE l_i_vbfa INTO l_wa_vbfa WITH KEY vbelv = fp_vbak-vbeln
*  vbtyp_n = l_c_del1. " Fetch returnable delivery no.  "#EC CI_READTBL
*    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
*      wa_final-vbeln_d = l_wa_vbfa-vbeln.
*    ENDIF.
*
*  ENDIF.
*  CLEAR l_wa_vbfa.
*--End of commenting for MOD-023
*--Start of addition for MOD-023
  IF r_deltyp IS INITIAL.
    PERFORM f_r_deltyp USING l_c_del. "Delivery type
    PERFORM f_r_deltyp USING l_c_del1. "Returnable Delivery type
  ENDIF.
  l_i_vbfa_tmp[] = l_i_vbfa[].
*--Delete entry apart from delivery
  DELETE l_i_vbfa_tmp WHERE vbtyp_n NOT IN r_deltyp.
  SORT l_i_vbfa_tmp BY vbeln.
*--Delete duplicate delivery
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_i_vbfa_tmp
                         COMPARING vbeln.
  LOOP AT l_i_vbfa_tmp  INTO l_wa_vbfa WHERE vbelv = fp_vbak-vbeln.
    l_v_posnr  = l_wa_vbfa-posnv.
    wa_final-vbeln_d = l_wa_vbfa-vbeln.
*--End of addition for MOD-023

    IF wa_final-vbeln_d IS NOT INITIAL.
      wa_final-xblnr2 = wa_final-vbeln_d.
      "BOC For MOD-014
***start of addition for MOD-029
*      if KNB1-BUKRS=7827 & VBAK-VKORG NE 0801, then skip check for
*      Equip Owner 05
      READ TABLE i_knb1 ASSIGNING <l_fs_knb1> WITH KEY
                                      kunnr = wa_final-/bev1/rpfar1
                                      BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
        IF <l_fs_knb1>-bukrs EQ c_jp_bukrs1 AND
           fp_vbak-vkorg NE c_vkorg1.
*-For intercompany invoice
          READ TABLE l_i_vbfa_inv INTO l_wa_vbfa WITH KEY
          vbelv = wa_final-vbeln
          posnv = l_v_posnr
          vbtyp_n = l_c_cominv " Fetch invoice no.
          BINARY SEARCH.
          IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
            APPEND l_wa_vbfa TO l_i_vbfa_tmp_1.
            PERFORM f_com_invoice_value USING l_wa_vbfa-vbeln.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
        UNASSIGN <l_fs_knb1>.
      ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-029

*-When equipment owner is 5
      IF wa_final-/scl/equp_ownr EQ l_c_equ.
*-For intercompany invoice
        READ TABLE l_i_vbfa_inv INTO l_wa_vbfa WITH KEY
        vbelv = wa_final-vbeln
        posnv = l_v_posnr
        vbtyp_n = l_c_cominv " Fetch invoice no.
        BINARY SEARCH.
        IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
          APPEND l_wa_vbfa TO l_i_vbfa_tmp_1.
          PERFORM f_com_invoice_value USING l_wa_vbfa-vbeln.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      "EOC For MOD-014
      "If delivery found
***start of changes for MOD-010
*    READ TABLE l_i_vbfa INTO l_wa_vbfa WITH KEY vbelv = wa_final-vbeln_d
*    vbtyp_n = l_c_inv. " Fetch invoice no."#EC CI_READTBL
      READ TABLE l_i_vbfa_inv INTO l_wa_vbfa WITH KEY
                                   vbelv = wa_final-vbeln
                                   posnv = l_v_posnr
                                   vbtyp_n = l_c_inv " Fetch invoice no.
                                   BINARY SEARCH.
***end of changes for MOD-010
      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
        APPEND l_wa_vbfa TO l_i_vbfa_tmp_1.
        PERFORM f_invoice_value USING l_wa_vbfa-vbeln.
      ELSE.
        READ TABLE l_i_vbfa INTO l_wa_vbfa WITH KEY vbelv =
        wa_final-vbeln
        posnv = l_v_posnr
       vbtyp_n = l_c_debit. " Fetch Debit Memo "#EC CI_READTBL
        IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
          APPEND l_wa_vbfa TO l_i_vbfa_tmp_1.
          PERFORM f_invoice_value USING l_wa_vbfa-vbeln.
        ELSE.
          READ TABLE l_i_vbfa INTO l_wa_vbfa WITH KEY vbelv =
          wa_final-vbeln
          posnv = l_v_posnr
         vbtyp_n = l_c_credit. " Fetch Credit Memo "#EC CI_READTBL
          IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
            APPEND l_wa_vbfa TO l_i_vbfa_tmp_1.
            PERFORM f_invoice_value USING l_wa_vbfa-vbeln.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
*--Start of addition for MOD-023
    CLEAR l_wa_vbfa.
    READ TABLE l_i_vbfa INTO l_wa_vbfa WITH KEY vbelv = wa_final-vbeln
                                                posnv = l_v_posnr
    vbtyp_n = l_c_mat."Fetch material document "#EC CI_READTBL
    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
      wa_final-mblnr = l_wa_vbfa-vbeln.
    ENDIF.
    APPEND wa_final TO i_final.
    CLEAR :l_wa_vbfa,wa_final-vbeln_d,wa_final-vbeln_i,wa_final-mblnr.
    "Flag set to avoid same line multiple times
    v_flag = abap_true.
    CLEAR:l_v_posnr.
  ENDLOOP.
*--End of addition for MOD-023
  IF wa_final-vbeln_d IS INITIAL
    AND l_i_vbfa_inv[] IS NOT INITIAL.
*--Start of commenting for MOD-023
    "BOC For MOD-014
*-When equipment owner is 5
*    IF wa_final-/scl/equp_ownr EQ l_c_equ.
**-For intercompany invoice
*      READ TABLE l_i_vbfa_inv INTO l_wa_vbfa WITH KEY
*      vbelv = fp_vbak-vbeln
*      vbtyp_n = l_c_cominv " Fetch invoice no.
*      BINARY SEARCH.
*      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
*        PERFORM f_com_invoice_value USING l_wa_vbfa-vbeln.
*      ENDIF.
*    ENDIF.
    "EOC For MOD-014
    "If delivery not found
***start of changes for MOD-010
*    READ TABLE l_i_vbfa INTO l_wa_vbfa WITH KEY vbelv = fp_vbak-vbeln
*  vbtyp_n = l_c_inv. " Fetch invoice no.    "#EC CI_READTBL
*    READ TABLE l_i_vbfa_inv INTO l_wa_vbfa WITH KEY
*                                 vbelv = fp_vbak-vbeln
*                                 vbtyp_n = l_c_inv " Fetch invoice no.
*                                 BINARY SEARCH.
****end of changes for MOD-010
*    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
*      PERFORM f_invoice_value USING l_wa_vbfa-vbeln.
*    ELSE.
*      READ TABLE l_i_vbfa INTO l_wa_vbfa WITH KEY vbelv = fp_vbak-vbeln
*      vbtyp_n = l_c_debit. " Fetch Debit Memo    "#EC CI_READTBL
*      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
*        PERFORM f_invoice_value USING l_wa_vbfa-vbeln.
*      ELSE.
*        READ TABLE l_i_vbfa INTO l_wa_vbfa WITH KEY vbelv =
*        fp_vbak-vbeln
*          vbtyp_n = l_c_credit. " Fetch Credit Memo   "#EC CI_READTBL
*        IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
*          PERFORM f_invoice_value USING l_wa_vbfa-vbeln.
*        ENDIF.
*      ENDIF.
*    ENDIF.
*  ENDIF.
*    READ TABLE l_i_vbfa INTO l_wa_vbfa WITH KEY vbelv = wa_final-vbeln_d
*    vbtyp_n = l_c_mat."Fetch material document "#EC CI_READTBL
*    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
*      wa_final-mblnr = l_wa_vbfa-vbeln.
*    ENDIF.
*    CLEAR l_wa_vbfa.
*--End of commenting for MOD-023
*--Start of addition for MOD-023
    SORT l_i_vbfa_tmp_1 BY vbelv vbeln.
    SORT l_i_vbfa_inv BY vbelv vbeln.
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_i_vbfa_inv
                        COMPARING vbelv vbeln.
    LOOP AT l_i_vbfa_inv INTO l_wa_vbfa WHERE vbelv = fp_vbak-vbeln.
      READ TABLE l_i_vbfa_tmp_1 TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS
                 WITH KEY vbelv = fp_vbak-vbeln
                          vbeln = l_wa_vbfa-vbeln
                          BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
        PERFORM f_invoice_value USING l_wa_vbfa-vbeln.
        APPEND wa_final TO i_final.
        CLEAR:l_wa_vbfa,wa_final-vbeln_i.
        v_flag = abap_true.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
    CLEAR:l_i_vbfa_tmp_1[].
  ENDIF.
*--End of addition for MOD-023
ENDFORM.                    " F_BUILD_ITAB
