FORM f_get_data .

*********************Internal Table Declaration***********************
  DATA: l_i_likp    TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_likp,
        l_i_invoice TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_invoice,
        l_i_accdoc  TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_bkpf,
***start of commenting for MOD-001
*        l_i_accdoc_d   TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_bkpf_d,
***end of commenting for MOD-001
***start of commenting for MOD-014
*        l_i_final    TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_final,
***start of commenting for MOD-014
        "BOC For MOD-014
        l_i_inv     TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_inv,
        l_i_final   TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_final.
  "EOC For MOD-014
*********************Work Area Declaration****************************
  DATA:l_wa_invoice  TYPE ty_invoice,
       l_wa_likp     TYPE ty_likp,
       l_i_bkpf      TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_bkpf,        "#EC NEEDED
       l_wa_bkpf     TYPE ty_bkpf,                          "#EC NEEDED
       l_wa_accdoc   TYPE ty_bkpf,
       l_wa_accdoc_d TYPE ty_bkpf_d,
       l_wa_tvak     TYPE ty_tvak.
*********************Constants Declaration****************************
  CONSTANTS:
    "Billing Tcode
    l_c_tcode TYPE sy-tcode VALUE 'VL02N',
    "Empty field
    l_c_blank TYPE char10 VALUE '-----------',
    "Dot
    l_c_dot   TYPE c VALUE '.'.
*********************Field Symbols Declaration************************
  FIELD-SYMBOLS:<l_fs_final> TYPE ty_final.
**********************************************************************
  IF i_final[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT vbeln
         "BOC For MOD-015
           ernam
           erzet
         "EOC For MOD-015
           erdat
           lfart
           FROM likp INTO TABLE l_i_likp FOR ALL ENTRIES IN i_final
           WHERE vbeln EQ i_final-vbeln_d.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR l_i_likp.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT vbeln fkart fkdat
           "BOC For MOD-015
            ernam
            erzet
           "EOC For MOD-015
           "BOC For MOD-021
           rfbsk
           "EOC For MOD-021
           FROM vbrk INTO TABLE l_i_invoice FOR ALL ENTRIES IN i_final
           WHERE vbeln EQ i_final-vbeln_i.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR l_i_invoice.
    ENDIF.

**" Getting FI document related Data based on Invoice no.
*** start of commenting for MOD-014
*    l_i_final[] = i_final[].
*** End of commenting for MOD-014
***    start of commenting for MOD-001
*    SORT l_i_final BY xblnr1.

***    end of commenting for MOD-001
***    start of addition for MOD-001

*** start of commenting for MOD-014

*    DELETE l_i_final WHERE awkey EQ space.
***    end of addition for MOD-001
*** End of commenting for MOD-014
*    IF l_i_final[] IS NOT INITIAL."Commented for MOD-014
    "BOC For MOD-014
    l_i_inv[] = i_inv[].
    SORT l_i_inv BY awkey.
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_i_inv COMPARING awkey.
    IF l_i_inv[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      "EOC For MOD-014
      SELECT bukrs belnr gjahr blart budat
           "BOC For MOD-015
             cputm
             usnam
           "EOC For MOD-015
             xblnr
***    start of addition for MOD-001
             awkey
***    end of addition for MOD-001

**         WHERE xblnr EQ l_i_final-xblnr1. "commented for MOD-001

*** end of commenting for MOD-014

        "BOC For MOD-014
         FROM bkpf INTO TABLE l_i_accdoc FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_inv
         WHERE awkey EQ l_i_inv-awkey.
      "EOC For MOD-014
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR l_i_accdoc.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
**""

***start of commenting for MOD-001
***" Getting FI document related Data based on Delivery no.
*    CLEAR l_i_final[].
*    l_i_final[] = i_final[].

*    DELETE l_i_final WHERE xblnr2 EQ space.

*         FROM bkpf INTO TABLE l_i_accdoc_d FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_final
*         WHERE xblnr EQ l_i_final-xblnr2.

*    ENDIF.

*BOC for MOD-019
*Get Inter Company Document details
    CLEAR l_i_final[].
    l_i_final[] = i_final[].
    SORT l_i_final BY cominv.
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_i_final COMPARING cominv.
    DELETE l_i_final WHERE cominv EQ space.
    DELETE l_i_final WHERE cominv EQ l_c_blank.

    IF l_i_final[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT vbeln fkart fkdat
                 ernam
                 erzet
               "BOC For MOD-021
                 rfbsk
               "EOC For MOD-021
                FROM vbrk
                APPENDING TABLE l_i_invoice
                FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_final
                WHERE vbeln EQ l_i_final-cominv.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR l_i_final.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
*EOC for MOD-019
  ENDIF.


  SORT: l_i_likp BY vbeln,
        l_i_invoice BY vbeln,
*        l_i_accdoc BY xblnr, "commented for MOD-001
        l_i_accdoc BY awkey, "added for MOD-001
*        l_i_accdoc_d BY xblnr, "commented for MOD-001
        i_tvak BY auart.
  LOOP AT i_final ASSIGNING <l_fs_final>.
    READ TABLE l_i_likp INTO l_wa_likp WITH KEY vbeln =
    <l_fs_final>-vbeln_d BINARY SEARCH.
    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.

      "BOC For MOD-015

      IF l_wa_likp-ernam NOT IN r_user.
        PERFORM f_get_local_time
                   USING   l_wa_likp-erzet
                   CHANGING l_wa_likp-erdat.
      ENDIF.
      "EOC For MOD-015
      <l_fs_final>-erdat_d = l_wa_likp-erdat.
      CONCATENATE <l_fs_final>-erdat_d+6(2) <l_fs_final>-erdat_d+4(2)
     <l_fs_final>-erdat_d(4) INTO <l_fs_final>-erdat_d
                     SEPARATED BY l_c_dot.

      <l_fs_final>-lfart = l_wa_likp-lfart.
    ENDIF.

    READ TABLE l_i_invoice INTO l_wa_invoice WITH KEY vbeln =
    <l_fs_final>-vbeln_i BINARY SEARCH.
    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.

      "BOC For MOD-015
*--Time zone
      IF l_wa_invoice-ernam NOT IN r_user.
        PERFORM f_get_local_time
                   USING   l_wa_invoice-erzet
                   CHANGING  l_wa_invoice-fkdat.
      ENDIF.
      "EOC For MOD-015
      <l_fs_final>-fkart = l_wa_invoice-fkart.
      <l_fs_final>-fkdat = l_wa_invoice-fkdat.
      CONCATENATE <l_fs_final>-fkdat+6(2) <l_fs_final>-fkdat+4(2)
           <l_fs_final>-fkdat(4) INTO <l_fs_final>-fkdat
               SEPARATED BY l_c_dot.
    ENDIF.

*BOC for MOD-019
*Inter company Invoice Details
    IF <l_fs_final>-cominv NE space AND <l_fs_final>-cominv NE l_c_blank.
      CLEAR:l_wa_invoice.
      READ TABLE l_i_invoice INTO l_wa_invoice WITH KEY vbeln =
          <l_fs_final>-cominv BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
*--Time zone
        IF l_wa_invoice-ernam NOT IN r_user.
          PERFORM f_get_local_time
                     USING   l_wa_invoice-erzet
                     CHANGING  l_wa_invoice-fkdat.
        ENDIF.
        <l_fs_final>-cominvtyp = l_wa_invoice-fkart.
        CONCATENATE l_wa_invoice-fkdat+6(2) l_wa_invoice-fkdat+4(2)
             l_wa_invoice-fkdat(4) INTO <l_fs_final>-cominvdate
                 SEPARATED BY l_c_dot.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
*EOC For MOD-019
    "Based on Invoice no.
***start of commenting for MOD-001
*    READ TABLE l_i_accdoc INTO l_wa_accdoc WITH KEY xblnr =
*    <l_fs_final>-xblnr1 BINARY SEARCH.
***end of commenting for MOD-001

    <l_fs_final>-awkey BINARY SEARCH.
***end of addition for MOD-001
    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
      "BOC For MOD-015
*--Time zone
      IF l_wa_accdoc-usnam NOT IN r_user.
        PERFORM f_get_local_time
                   USING   l_wa_accdoc-cputm
                   CHANGING  l_wa_accdoc-budat.
      ENDIF.
      "EOC For MOD-015
      <l_fs_final>-belnr = l_wa_accdoc-belnr.
      <l_fs_final>-bukrs = l_wa_accdoc-bukrs.
      <l_fs_final>-blart = l_wa_accdoc-blart.
      <l_fs_final>-gjahr = l_wa_accdoc-gjahr.
      <l_fs_final>-budat = l_wa_accdoc-budat.
      CONCATENATE <l_fs_final>-budat+6(2) <l_fs_final>-budat+4(2)
             <l_fs_final>-budat(4) INTO <l_fs_final>-budat
                 SEPARATED BY l_c_dot.
***start of commenting for MOD-001
*    ELSE.
*      "Based on Delivery no.
*      READ TABLE l_i_accdoc_d INTO l_wa_accdoc_d WITH KEY xblnr =
*   <l_fs_final>-xblnr2 BINARY SEARCH.
*      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
*        <l_fs_final>-belnr = l_wa_accdoc_d-belnr.
*        <l_fs_final>-bukrs = l_wa_accdoc_d-bukrs.
*        <l_fs_final>-blart = l_wa_accdoc_d-blart.
*        <l_fs_final>-gjahr = l_wa_accdoc_d-gjahr.
*        <l_fs_final>-budat = l_wa_accdoc_d-budat.
*        CONCATENATE <l_fs_final>-budat+6(2) <l_fs_final>-budat+4(2)
*                 <l_fs_final>-budat(4) INTO <l_fs_final>-budat
*                     SEPARATED BY l_c_dot.
*      ENDIF.
***end of commenting for MOD-001
    ENDIF.
    "BOC For MOD-014
*--For Intercompany FI
    READ TABLE l_i_accdoc INTO l_wa_accdoc WITH KEY awkey(10) =
    <l_fs_final>-cominv BINARY SEARCH.
    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
      <l_fs_final>-comfi = l_wa_accdoc-belnr.
      <l_fs_final>-comfi_bukrs = l_wa_accdoc-bukrs.
      <l_fs_final>-comfi_gjahr = l_wa_accdoc-gjahr.
      "BOC For MOD-019
*     Intercompany FI Type and Date
      <l_fs_final>-comfityp = l_wa_accdoc-blart.
*--Local Time zone
      IF l_wa_accdoc-usnam NOT IN r_user.
        PERFORM f_get_local_time
                   USING   l_wa_accdoc-cputm
                   CHANGING l_wa_accdoc-budat.
      ENDIF.

      CONCATENATE l_wa_accdoc-budat+6(2) l_wa_accdoc-budat+4(2)
          l_wa_accdoc-budat(4) INTO <l_fs_final>-comfidate
              SEPARATED BY l_c_dot.
      "EOC For MOD-019
    ENDIF.
    "EOC For MOD-014

    READ TABLE i_tvak INTO l_wa_tvak
             WITH KEY auart = <l_fs_final>-auart BINARY SEARCH.
    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
      "When billing type is blank
      IF  l_wa_tvak-fkarv	IS INITIAL.
        <l_fs_final>-vbeln_i = l_c_blank.
        <l_fs_final>-fkart = l_c_blank.
        <l_fs_final>-fkdat = l_c_blank.
        <l_fs_final>-blart = l_c_blank.
        <l_fs_final>-belnr = l_c_blank.
        <l_fs_final>-budat = l_c_blank.
        <l_fs_final>-bukrs = l_c_blank.
        <l_fs_final>-gjahr = l_c_blank.
        <l_fs_final>-paymt = l_c_blank.
        <l_fs_final>-cardnr = l_c_blank.
        "BOC For MOD-014
        <l_fs_final>-cominv = l_c_blank.
        <l_fs_final>-cominvdate = l_c_blank.
        <l_fs_final>-cominvtyp = l_c_blank.
        <l_fs_final>-comfi = l_c_blank.
        <l_fs_final>-comfidate = l_c_blank.
        <l_fs_final>-comfityp = l_c_blank.
        "EOC For MOD-014
        " When delivery type is blank and billing type is not blank.
      ELSEIF ( l_wa_tvak-lfarv IS INITIAL )
          AND ( l_wa_tvak-fkarv	IS NOT INITIAL ).
        <l_fs_final>-lfart = l_c_blank.
        <l_fs_final>-vbeln_d = l_c_blank.
        <l_fs_final>-erdat_d = l_c_blank.
        <l_fs_final>-mblnr = l_c_blank.
      ENDIF.

    ENDIF.
    " When sales doc. no is not blank
    IF <l_fs_final>-vbeln IS NOT INITIAL.
      <l_fs_final>-paymt = l_c_blank.
      <l_fs_final>-cardnr = l_c_blank.
    ELSE.
      " When sales doc. no is blank
      <l_fs_final>-auart = l_c_blank.
      <l_fs_final>-vbeln  = l_c_blank.
      <l_fs_final>-erdat_o = l_c_blank.
      <l_fs_final>-lfart = l_c_blank.
      <l_fs_final>-vbeln_d = l_c_blank.
      <l_fs_final>-erdat_d = l_c_blank.
      <l_fs_final>-mblnr = l_c_blank.
      <l_fs_final>-vbeln_i = l_c_blank.
      <l_fs_final>-fkart = l_c_blank.
      <l_fs_final>-fkdat = l_c_blank.
      "BOC For MOD-014
      <l_fs_final>-cominv = l_c_blank.
      <l_fs_final>-cominvtyp = l_c_blank.
      <l_fs_final>-cominvdate = l_c_blank.
      <l_fs_final>-comfi = l_c_blank.
      <l_fs_final>-comfidate = l_c_blank.
      <l_fs_final>-comfityp = l_c_blank.
      "EOC For MOD-014
    ENDIF.

    "BOC For MOD-014
*--Intercom. invoice
    PERFORM f_intercom_value
    CHANGING <l_fs_final>.
    "EOC For MOD-014

    "BOC For MOD-015
*--Setting the status
    PERFORM f_set_status CHANGING <l_fs_final>.
    "EOC For MOD-015
*--BOC For MOD-021
    IF l_wa_invoice-vbeln IS NOT INITIAL
*--Status for transfer to accounting
      AND l_wa_invoice-rfbsk EQ c_rfbsk.
*--Green status
      <l_fs_final>-fsr_status = c_green.
    ENDIF.
*--EOC For MOD-021
    CLEAR: l_wa_likp,l_wa_invoice,l_wa_accdoc,l_wa_accdoc_d,l_wa_tvak.
  ENDLOOP.
  IF <l_fs_final> IS ASSIGNED.
    UNASSIGN <l_fs_final>.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " F_GET_DATA
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_ACC_DATA
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*    Getting Accounting Info based on Shipment/Visit list
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_acc_data  USING fp_status TYPE tb_t_status
                 CHANGING  fp_acc TYPE tb_t_acc fp_bkpf TYPE tb_t_bkpf
                        fp_vlh TYPE tb_t_vlh fp_raec TYPE tb_t_raec.

*********************Field Symbols Declaration************************
  FIELD-SYMBOLS:<l_fs_acc>    TYPE ty_acc,
                <l_fs_bkpf>   TYPE ty_bkpf,
                <l_fs_vlh>    TYPE ty_vlh,
                <l_fs_raec>   TYPE ty_raec,
                <l_fs_racvhd> TYPE ty_/dsd/hh_racvhd,
                <l_fs_status> TYPE   ty_/dsd/st_status,
                <l_fs_stat>   TYPE  ty_/ccej/t_inb_stat.
*********************Constants Declaration****************************
  CONSTANTS:
    "Empty field
    l_c_blank TYPE char10 VALUE '-----------',
    "Dot
    l_c_dot   TYPE c VALUE '.'.

***start of addition for MOD-002
  DATA l_v_flag TYPE abap_bool.
***end of addition for MOD-002
**********************************************************************
  IF fp_acc[] IS NOT INITIAL.

    "Check HHT document no.
    IF s_hht[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      SORT fp_raec BY  oi_csh_post.
      LOOP AT fp_acc ASSIGNING <l_fs_acc>.
        READ TABLE fp_raec
         TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS WITH KEY oi_csh_post = <l_fs_acc>-belnr
         BINARY SEARCH.
        IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
          "Delete if Accounting Doc. not found
          DELETE fp_acc.
        ENDIF.
      ENDLOOP.
      IF <l_fs_acc> IS ASSIGNED.
        UNASSIGN <l_fs_acc>.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    SORT: fp_acc BY obj_id,
          fp_raec BY  tour_id oi_csh_post,
          fp_bkpf BY belnr xblnr,
          fp_vlh BY vlid.

    LOOP AT fp_acc ASSIGNING <l_fs_acc>.
      wa_final-tknum = <l_fs_acc>-obj_id.
      wa_final-pre_doc_id = <l_fs_acc>-pre_doc_id.
      wa_final-/bev1/rpfar1 = <l_fs_acc>-driver.

      READ TABLE i_inb_stat ASSIGNING <l_fs_stat>
            WITH KEY vlid = <l_fs_acc>-obj_id(10) BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
        wa_final-werks = <l_fs_stat>-werks.
        wa_final-route = <l_fs_stat>-route.
        wa_final-date = <l_fs_stat>-date.
        "BOC For MOD-015
*--Time zone
***start of commenting for MOD-025
*        IF <l_fs_stat>-zzernam NOT IN r_user.
*          PERFORM f_get_local_time
*                     USING   <l_fs_stat>-zzerzzt
*                     CHANGING wa_final-date.
*        ENDIF.
***end of commenting for MOD-025
        "EOC For MOD-015
        UNASSIGN <l_fs_stat>.
      ENDIF.

***start of addition for MOD-002
*if Plant, Route, Settlement date and HHT document number are entered on
*sel screen then only consider records which exist in /CCEJ/T_INB_STAT
      IF s_werks[] IS NOT INITIAL AND s_route1[] IS NOT INITIAL AND
         s_date[] IS NOT INITIAL AND s_hht[] IS NOT INITIAL.
        PERFORM f_filter USING wa_final-werks
                               wa_final-route
                               wa_final-date
                         CHANGING l_v_flag.
        IF l_v_flag EQ abap_true.
          CLEAR: wa_final-tknum, wa_final-pre_doc_id,
                 wa_final-/bev1/rpfar1, wa_final-werks, wa_final-route,
                 wa_final-date, l_v_flag.
*      filter out this record if doesnt match as entered on sel screen
          CONTINUE.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-002

      READ TABLE fp_status ASSIGNING <l_fs_status>
      WITH KEY tourid = <l_fs_acc>-tour_id(12) BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
        wa_final-status_id = <l_fs_status>-status_id.
        UNASSIGN <l_fs_status>.
      ENDIF.

      READ TABLE i_racvhd  ASSIGNING <l_fs_racvhd>
           WITH KEY tour_id = <l_fs_acc>-tour_id
                    visit_id = <l_fs_acc>-visit_id BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
        wa_final-visit_id = <l_fs_racvhd>-visit_id.
        wa_final-custnr   = <l_fs_racvhd>-custnr.
        UNASSIGN <l_fs_racvhd>.
***start of addition for MOD-002
        READ TABLE i_kna1 ASSIGNING <fs_kna1> WITH KEY
                                          kunnr = wa_final-custnr
                                          BINARY SEARCH.
        IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
          wa_final-katr4 = <fs_kna1>-katr4.
          wa_final-/scl/equp_ownr = <fs_kna1>-/scl/equp_ownr."MOD-014
          UNASSIGN <fs_kna1>.
        ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-002
      ENDIF.

      READ TABLE fp_vlh ASSIGNING <l_fs_vlh> WITH KEY vlid =
      wa_final-tknum BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        wa_final-erdat_s = <l_fs_vlh>-erdat.
        "BOC For MOD-015
*--Time zone
        IF <l_fs_vlh>-ernam NOT IN r_user.
          PERFORM f_get_local_time
                     USING   <l_fs_vlh>-erzet
                     CHANGING wa_final-erdat_s.
        ENDIF.
        "EOC For MOD-015
        UNASSIGN <l_fs_vlh>.
      ENDIF.

      READ TABLE fp_raec ASSIGNING <l_fs_raec> WITH KEY tour_id =
      <l_fs_acc>-tour_id oi_csh_post = <l_fs_acc>-belnr BINARY SEARCH.
      IF  sy-subrc = 0.
        wa_final-paymt = <l_fs_raec>-paymt.
        IF wa_final-bstkd IS INITIAL.
          wa_final-bstkd = <l_fs_raec>-paymt_descr.
        ENDIF.
        wa_final-cardnr = <l_fs_raec>-cardnr.
        IF wa_final-cardnr IS INITIAL.
          wa_final-cardnr = l_c_blank.
        ENDIF.
        UNASSIGN <l_fs_raec>.
      ENDIF.


      READ TABLE fp_bkpf  ASSIGNING <l_fs_bkpf> WITH KEY belnr =
      <l_fs_acc>-belnr xblnr = <l_fs_acc>-xblnr  BINARY SEARCH.
      IF  sy-subrc = 0.
        wa_final-belnr = <l_fs_bkpf>-belnr.
        wa_final-bukrs = <l_fs_bkpf>-bukrs.
        wa_final-blart = <l_fs_bkpf>-blart.
        wa_final-gjahr = <l_fs_bkpf>-gjahr.
        "BOC For MOD-015
*--Time zone
        IF <l_fs_bkpf>-usnam NOT IN r_user.
          PERFORM f_get_local_time
                     USING   <l_fs_bkpf>-cputm
                     CHANGING <l_fs_bkpf>-budat.
        ENDIF.
        wa_final-budat = <l_fs_bkpf>-budat.
        CONCATENATE wa_final-budat+6(2) wa_final-budat+4(2)
         wa_final-budat(4) INTO wa_final-budat
             SEPARATED BY l_c_dot.

        wa_final-auart = l_c_blank.
        wa_final-vbeln  = l_c_blank.
        wa_final-erdat_o = l_c_blank.
        wa_final-lfart = l_c_blank.
        wa_final-vbeln_d = l_c_blank.
        wa_final-erdat_d = l_c_blank.
        wa_final-mblnr = l_c_blank.
        wa_final-vbeln_i = l_c_blank.
        wa_final-fkart = l_c_blank.
        wa_final-fkdat = l_c_blank.
        "BOC For MOD-014
        wa_final-cominv = l_c_blank.
        wa_final-cominvdate = l_c_blank.
        wa_final-cominvtyp = l_c_blank.
        wa_final-comfi = l_c_blank.
        wa_final-comfidate = l_c_blank.
        wa_final-comfityp = l_c_blank.
        "EOC For MOD-014

        "BOC For MOD-014
*--Intercom. invoice
        PERFORM f_intercom_value
        CHANGING wa_final.
        "EOC For MOD-014

        "BOC For MOD-015
*--Setting the status
        PERFORM f_set_status CHANGING wa_final.
        "EOC For MOD-015
        APPEND wa_final TO i_final.
        CLEAR:wa_final.
        UNASSIGN <l_fs_bkpf> .
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
    IF <l_fs_acc> IS ASSIGNED.
      UNASSIGN <l_fs_acc>.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                    " F_ACC_DATA

FORM f_mat_data  USING fp_vlh TYPE tb_t_vlh
                       fp_status TYPE tb_t_status
                 CHANGING fp_mat TYPE tb_t_mat.

*********************Constants Declaration****************************
  CONSTANTS:
       "Empty field
       l_c_blank TYPE char10 VALUE '-----------'.
**********************************************************************
***start of addition for MOD-002
  DATA l_v_flag TYPE abap_bool.
***end of addition for MOD-002

  IF fp_mat[] IS NOT INITIAL.

    SORT:fp_mat BY obj_id mblnr.

    LOOP AT fp_mat ASSIGNING <l_fs_mat>.
      wa_final-tknum = <l_fs_mat>-obj_id.
      wa_final-pre_doc_id = <l_fs_mat>-pre_doc_id.
      wa_final-/bev1/rpfar1 = <l_fs_mat>-driver.
      wa_final-mblnr = <l_fs_mat>-mblnr.

      READ TABLE i_inb_stat ASSIGNING <l_fs_stat>
       WITH KEY vlid = <l_fs_mat>-obj_id(10) BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
        wa_final-werks = <l_fs_stat>-werks.
        wa_final-route = <l_fs_stat>-route.
        wa_final-date = <l_fs_stat>-date.
        "BOC For MOD-015
*--Time zone
***start of commenting for MOD-025
*        IF <l_fs_stat>-zzernam NOT IN r_user.
*          PERFORM f_get_local_time
*                     USING   <l_fs_stat>-zzerzzt
*                     CHANGING wa_final-date.
*        ENDIF.
***end of commenting for MOD-025
        "EOC For MOD-015
        UNASSIGN <l_fs_stat>.
      ENDIF.

***start of addition for MOD-002
*if Plant, Route, Settlement date and HHT document number are entered on
*sel screen then only consider records which exist in /CCEJ/T_INB_STAT
      IF s_werks[] IS NOT INITIAL AND s_route1[] IS NOT INITIAL AND
         s_date[] IS NOT INITIAL AND s_hht[] IS NOT INITIAL.
        PERFORM f_filter USING wa_final-werks
                               wa_final-route
                               wa_final-date
                         CHANGING l_v_flag.
        IF l_v_flag EQ abap_true.
          CLEAR: wa_final-tknum, wa_final-pre_doc_id,
                 wa_final-/bev1/rpfar1, wa_final-mblnr, wa_final-werks,
                 wa_final-route, wa_final-date, l_v_flag.
*      filter out this record if doesnt match as entered on sel screen
          CONTINUE.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-002

      READ TABLE fp_status ASSIGNING <l_fs_status>
      WITH KEY tourid = <l_fs_mat>-tour_id(12) BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
        wa_final-status_id = <l_fs_status>-status_id.
        UNASSIGN <l_fs_status>.
      ENDIF.

      READ TABLE i_racvhd  ASSIGNING <l_fs_racvhd>
      WITH KEY tour_id = <l_fs_mat>-tour_id
               visit_id = <l_fs_mat>-visit_id BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
        wa_final-visit_id = <l_fs_racvhd>-visit_id.
        wa_final-custnr   = <l_fs_racvhd>-custnr.
        UNASSIGN <l_fs_racvhd>.
***start of addition for MOD-002
        READ TABLE i_kna1 ASSIGNING <fs_kna1> WITH KEY
                                          kunnr = wa_final-custnr
                                          BINARY SEARCH.
        IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
          wa_final-katr4 = <fs_kna1>-katr4.
          wa_final-/scl/equp_ownr = <fs_kna1>-/scl/equp_ownr."MOD-014
          UNASSIGN <fs_kna1>.
        ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-002
      ENDIF.

      READ TABLE fp_vlh ASSIGNING <l_fs_vlh> WITH KEY vlid =
      wa_final-tknum BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
        wa_final-erdat_s = <l_fs_vlh>-erdat.
        "BOC For MOD-015
*--Time zone
        IF <l_fs_vlh>-ernam NOT IN r_user.
          PERFORM f_get_local_time
                     USING   <l_fs_vlh>-erzet
                     CHANGING wa_final-erdat_s.
        ENDIF.
        "EOC For MOD-015
        UNASSIGN <l_fs_vlh>.
      ENDIF.

      wa_final-auart = l_c_blank.
      wa_final-vbeln  = l_c_blank.
      wa_final-erdat_o = l_c_blank.
      wa_final-lfart = l_c_blank.
      wa_final-vbeln_d = l_c_blank.
      wa_final-erdat_d = l_c_blank.
      wa_final-mblnr = l_c_blank.
      wa_final-vbeln_i = l_c_blank.
      wa_final-fkart = l_c_blank.
      wa_final-fkdat = l_c_blank.
      "BOC For MOD-014
      wa_final-cominv = l_c_blank.
      wa_final-cominvdate = l_c_blank.
      wa_final-cominvtyp = l_c_blank.
      wa_final-comfidate = l_c_blank.
      wa_final-comfityp = l_c_blank.
      wa_final-comfi = l_c_blank.
      "EOC For MOD-014

      "BOC For MOD-014
*--Intercom. invoice
      PERFORM f_intercom_value
      CHANGING wa_final.
      "EOC For MOD-014

      "BOC For MOD-015
*--Setting the status
      PERFORM f_set_status CHANGING wa_final.
      "EOC For MOD-015

      APPEND wa_final TO i_final.
      CLEAR:wa_final.
    ENDLOOP.
    IF <l_fs_mat> IS ASSIGNED.
      UNASSIGN <l_fs_mat>.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " F_MAT_DATA
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_GET_PAYMENT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*    Fetching Payment related data
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_get_payment.
*********************Internal Table Declaration***********************
  DATA:l_i_status      TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_/dsd/st_status,
       l_i_pay         TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF /dsd/hh_raec,
       l_i_bseg        TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_bseg,
       l_i_bsegtmp     TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_bseg, "MOD-017
       l_i_racvhd      TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_/dsd/hh_racvhd,
***start of addition for MOD-002
       l_i_racvhd_temp TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_/dsd/hh_racvhd,
       l_i_kna1        TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_kna,
***end of addition for MOD-002
       l_i_vlh         TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_dsd_vc_vlh, "++MOD-031
***start of addition for MOD-005
       l_i_pay_tmp     TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF /dsd/hh_raec,
       l_i_bseg_tmp    TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF bseg.
***end of addition for MOD-005
*********************Data Declaration*********************************
  DATA:l_v_index   TYPE sy-tabix,
       l_wa_final2 TYPE ty_final2,
       l_v_value   TYPE bapicurr-bapicurr. "Contolling area currency
***start of addition for MOD-005
  DATA:l_wa_bseg      TYPE ty_bseg,
***end of addition for MOD-005
*BOC MOD-016
       l_i_driver     TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_dummyacc,
       l_i_customer   TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_dummyacc,
*EOC MOD-016
***start of addition for MOD-027
       l_i_inb_stat   TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_inb_stat_xblnr,
       l_wa_inb_stat  TYPE ty_inb_stat_xblnr,
       l_i_bkpf_p     TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_bkpf_p,
       l_i_bseg_p     TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_bseg_p,
       l_i_bseg_p_tmp TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_bseg_p,
       l_i_bkpf_temp  TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_bkpf_p,
       l_v_driver     TYPE kunnr,
       l_v_lines      TYPE i,
       l_v_flag       TYPE abap_bool.
***end of addition for MOD-027
*********************Field Symbols Declaration************************
  FIELD-SYMBOLS:<l_fs_status>   TYPE ty_/dsd/st_status,
                <l_fs_pay>      TYPE /dsd/hh_raec,
                <l_fs_bseg>     TYPE ty_bseg,
                <l_fs_racvhd>   TYPE ty_/dsd/hh_racvhd,
                <l_fs_stat>     TYPE  ty_/ccej/t_inb_stat,
***start of addition for MOD-005
                <l_fs_bseg_tmp> TYPE bseg,
***end of addition for MOD-005
***start of addition for MOD-027
                <l_fs_bkpf_p>   TYPE ty_bkpf_p,
                <l_fs_bseg_p>   TYPE ty_bseg_p,
                <l_fs_bseg_p2>  TYPE ty_bseg_p,
                <l_fs_inb_stat> TYPE ty_inb_stat_xblnr.
***end of addition for MOD-027
*********************Constants Declaration****************************
  CONSTANTS:
    "Settlement status
    l_c_status     TYPE /dsd/st_status_id VALUE '804000',
*          BOC MD-016
    "FSR - Settlement started
    l_c_sett_start TYPE /dsd/st_status_id VALUE '804000',
    "FSR - Settlement completed
    l_c_sett_compl TYPE /dsd/st_status_id VALUE '804090',
    l_c_80300      TYPE /dsd/st_status_id VALUE '803000'.
*          EOC MD-016
**********************************************************************

* BOC MOD-020
*Get Payment Log Selected
  PERFORM f_plog_status USING s_plog-low.
* EOC MOD-020

  PERFORM f_status CHANGING l_i_status.

  IF l_i_status[] IS NOT INITIAL.
***start of addition for MOD-027
* check if input entered in Document Type- if only DZ is input
* then RADB select not required as below as it wont retreive DZ doc
    IF s_blart1[] IS NOT INITIAL.
*      check number of entries in Document type
      DESCRIBE TABLE s_blart1 LINES l_v_lines.
      IF l_v_lines EQ 1 AND
         s_blart1-low EQ c_dz AND
         s_blart1-sign EQ c_i AND
         s_blart1-option EQ c_eq.
        l_v_flag = abap_true.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    IF l_v_flag EQ abap_false.
***end of addition for MOD-027

      SELECT * FROM /dsd/hh_raec INTO TABLE l_i_pay        "#EC CI_STAR
               FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_status
               WHERE tour_id = l_i_status-tourid.
      SELECT tour_id
             visit_id
             custnr FROM /dsd/hh_racvhd  INTO TABLE l_i_racvhd
               FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_status
               WHERE tour_id = l_i_status-tourid.
***start of addition for MOD-002
      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
        SORT l_i_racvhd BY custnr.
        l_i_racvhd_temp[] = l_i_racvhd[].
        SORT l_i_racvhd_temp BY custnr.
        DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_i_racvhd_temp
                                       COMPARING custnr.
        IF l_i_racvhd_temp[] IS NOT INITIAL.
          SELECT kunnr katr3 katr4
                 /scl/equp_ownr "MOD-014
                  FROM kna1  INTO TABLE l_i_kna1
                             FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_racvhd_temp
                             WHERE kunnr EQ l_i_racvhd_temp-custnr.
          IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
            SORT l_i_kna1 BY kunnr.
          ENDIF.

        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-002
    ENDIF. "***added for MOD-027
  ENDIF.

  IF l_i_pay[] IS NOT INITIAL.
***start of addition for MOD-005
    l_i_pay_tmp[] = l_i_pay[].
    SORT l_i_pay_tmp BY compcod oi_csh_post fisc_year.
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_i_pay_tmp
       COMPARING compcod oi_csh_post fisc_year.

    LOOP AT l_i_pay_tmp ASSIGNING <l_fs_pay>.

***Reading the Accounting documents
" *****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **begin of change by EJX9007359 03.06.2026 for ATC
      CALL FUNCTION 'FI_DOCUMENT_READ' "#EC CI_USAGE_OK[2628706]
" *****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **end of change by EJX9007359 03.06.2026 for ATC
        EXPORTING
          i_bukrs     = <l_fs_pay>-compcod
          i_belnr     = <l_fs_pay>-oi_csh_post
          i_gjahr     = <l_fs_pay>-fisc_year
        TABLES
          t_bseg      = l_i_bseg_tmp
        EXCEPTIONS
          wrong_input = 1
          not_found   = 2
          OTHERS      = 3.

      LOOP AT l_i_bseg_tmp ASSIGNING <l_fs_bseg_tmp>.
        MOVE-CORRESPONDING  <l_fs_bseg_tmp>
           TO l_wa_bseg.
        APPEND l_wa_bseg TO l_i_bseg.
        CLEAR l_wa_bseg.
      ENDLOOP.
      IF  <l_fs_bseg_tmp> IS ASSIGNED.
        UNASSIGN  <l_fs_bseg_tmp>.
      ENDIF.
      CLEAR l_i_bseg_tmp.

    ENDLOOP.
    IF <l_fs_pay> IS ASSIGNED.

              CLEAR l_wa_final2.
            ENDLOOP.
            IF <l_fs_bseg_p> IS ASSIGNED.
              UNASSIGN <l_fs_bseg_p>.
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-027
*BOC For MOD-014
*Filter Data by Equipment Owner
  IF s_equp IS NOT INITIAL.
    DELETE i_final2 WHERE /scl/equp_ownr NOT IN s_equp.
  ENDIF.

*Filter by customer
  IF s_kunnr IS NOT INITIAL.
    DELETE i_final2 WHERE cust NOT IN s_kunnr.
  ENDIF.

*Filter by business extension
  IF s_katr4 IS NOT INITIAL.
    DELETE i_final2 WHERE katr4 NOT IN s_katr4.
  ENDIF.
*EOC For MOD-014

*BOC MOD-016
*Filter by Dummy Account Flag
  IF s_dummy IS NOT INITIAL.
    DELETE i_final2 WHERE dummy_flag NOT IN s_dummy.
  ENDIF.
*EOC MOD-016

*BOC MOD-019
*Filter by Dummy Account Flag
  IF s_docno IS NOT INITIAL.
    DELETE i_final2 WHERE  oi_csh_post NOT IN s_docno.
  ENDIF.
*EOC MOD-019

*BOC MOD-020
*Filter by Payment Log
  IF s_plog IS NOT INITIAL.
    DELETE i_final2 WHERE  plog NOT IN s_plog.
  ENDIF.
*EOC MOD-020

***start of addition for MOD-027
  IF s_bschl IS NOT INITIAL.
    DELETE i_final2 WHERE bschl NOT IN s_bschl.
  ENDIF.
  IF s_blart1 IS NOT INITIAL.
    DELETE i_final2 WHERE blart NOT IN s_blart1.
  ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-027
ENDFORM.                    " F_GET_PAYMENT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*

* Visit details-Payment-Checkout/In-Money-Quantity Diff
* columns description

FORM f_set_columns2  USING  fp_columns TYPE REF TO cl_salv_columns_table
.
*********************Reference variable Declaration*******************
  DATA:l_o_column  TYPE REF TO cl_salv_column,
       l_o_col_tab TYPE REF TO cl_salv_column_table.
*********************Constants Declaration****************************
  CONSTANTS:
    "Visit list
    l_c_shmnt   TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'SHIPMENT',
    l_c_settmnt TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'DATE', "Settlmnt date
    l_c_acc     TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'OI_CSH_POST', "Acc. Doc.
    l_c_sid     TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'STATUS_ID', "Status ID
    l_c_cout    TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'AMOUNT_CO', "Check out
    l_c_cin     TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'AMOUNT_CI', "Check in
    l_c_cust    TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'KUNNR', "Customer
***start of addition for MOD-001
    l_c_checknr TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'CHECKNR', "Check number
    l_c_buzid   TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'BUZID', "Item ID
***end of addition for MOD-001
***start of addition for MOD-006
    l_c_cus     TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'CUST',
    l_c_cust1   TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'CUST1',
***end of addition for MOD-006
    l_c_dummy   TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'DUMMY_FLAG'. "MOD-016


**********************************************************************
  TRY.
      l_o_column = fp_columns->get_column( l_c_shmnt  ).
      l_o_column->set_long_text( TEXT-c01 ).
      l_o_column->set_medium_text( space ).
      l_o_column->set_short_text( space ).
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.                            "#EC NO_HANDLER
  ENDTRY.

  TRY.
      l_o_column = fp_columns->get_column( l_c_settmnt ).
      l_o_column->set_long_text( TEXT-c37 ).
      l_o_column->set_medium_text( space ).
      l_o_column->set_short_text( space ).
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.                            "#EC NO_HANDLER
  ENDTRY.

  TRY.
      l_o_column = fp_columns->get_column(  l_c_cout ).
      l_o_column->set_long_text( TEXT-c46 ).
      l_o_column->set_medium_text( space ).
      l_o_column->set_short_text( space ).
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.                            "#EC NO_HANDLER
  ENDTRY.

  TRY.
      l_o_column = fp_columns->get_column(  l_c_cin ).
      l_o_column->set_long_text( TEXT-c43 ).
      l_o_column->set_medium_text( space ).
      l_o_column->set_short_text( space ).
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.                            "#EC NO_HANDLER
  ENDTRY.

  TRY.
      l_o_column = fp_columns->get_column( l_c_sid ).
      l_o_column->set_long_text( TEXT-c45 ).
      l_o_column->set_medium_text( space ).
      l_o_column->set_short_text( space ).
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.                            "#EC NO_HANDLER
  ENDTRY.

  TRY.
      l_o_column = fp_columns->get_column( l_c_cust ).
      l_o_column->set_visible( abap_false ).
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.                            "#EC NO_HANDLER
  ENDTRY.

***start of addition for MOD-001
  TRY.
      l_o_column = fp_columns->get_column( l_c_checknr ).
      IF v_flag1 EQ abap_false.
        l_o_column->set_visible( abap_false ).

    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.                            "#EC NO_HANDLER
  ENDTRY.

  TRY.
      l_o_column = fp_columns->get_column( l_c_buzid ).
      IF v_flag2 EQ abap_false.
        l_o_column->set_visible( abap_false ).
      ENDIF.
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.                            "#EC NO_HANDLER
  ENDTRY.
***end of addition for MOD-001

***start of addition for MOD-006
  IF v_flag3 EQ abap_true.
    TRY.
        l_o_column = fp_columns->get_column( l_c_cus ).
        l_o_column->set_long_text( TEXT-c50 ).
        l_o_column->set_medium_text( space ).

    ENDTRY.
  ENDIF.

  TRY.
      l_o_column = fp_columns->get_column( l_c_cust1 ).
      IF v_flag3 EQ abap_false.
        l_o_column->set_visible( abap_false ).
      ENDIF.
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.                            "#EC NO_HANDLER
  ENDTRY.
***end of addition for MOD-006


  "Setting hotspot
  TRY.
      l_o_col_tab ?= fp_columns->get_column( l_c_acc ).
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.                            "#EC NO_HANDLER
  ENDTRY.

  IF l_o_col_tab IS NOT INITIAL.
    TRY.
        CALL METHOD l_o_col_tab->set_cell_type
          EXPORTING
            value = if_salv_c_cell_type=>hotspot.

      CATCH cx_salv_data_error .                        "#EC NO_HANDLER
    ENDTRY.
  ENDIF.

*BOC MOD-016
  TRY.
      l_o_column = fp_columns->get_column(  l_c_dummy ).
      l_o_column->set_long_text( TEXT-c66 ).
      l_o_column->set_medium_text( space ).
      l_o_column->set_short_text( space ).
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.                            "#EC NO_HANDLER
  ENDTRY.
*EOC MOD-016
ENDFORM.                    " F_SET_COLUMNS2
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_GET_CHECK
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*    Fetching check out/in details
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_get_check.
*********************Internal Table Declaration***********************
  DATA:l_i_status        TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_/dsd/st_status,
       l_i_racocimi      TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF /dsd/hh_racocimi,
       l_i_racocici      TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF  /dsd/hh_racocici,
***start of addition for MOD-002
       l_i_racocimi_temp TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF /dsd/hh_racocimi,
       l_i_makt          TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_makt,
***end of addition for MOD-002

***start of addition for MOD-005
       l_i_racocihd      TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_hh_racocihd.
***start of addition for MOD-005
*********************Data Declaration********************************
  DATA:l_wa_final3 TYPE ty_final3,
       l_v_value   TYPE bapicurr-bapicurr. "Contolling area currency
*********************Field Symbols Declaration************************
  FIELD-SYMBOLS:<l_fs_status>   TYPE ty_/dsd/st_status,
                <l_fs_racocimi> TYPE /dsd/hh_racocimi,
                <l_fs_racocici> TYPE /dsd/hh_racocici,
                <l_fs_stat>     TYPE  ty_/ccej/t_inb_stat,
***start of addition for MOD-002
                <l_fs_makt>     TYPE ty_makt.
***end of addition for MOD-002
**********************************************************************
  PERFORM f_status  CHANGING l_i_status.

  IF l_i_status[] IS NOT INITIAL.

    SELECT * FROM /dsd/hh_racocimi INTO TABLE l_i_racocimi "#EC CI_STAR
          FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_status
          WHERE  tour_id = l_i_status-tourid.
***start of addition for MOD-002
    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
      l_i_racocimi_temp[] = l_i_racocimi[].
      SORT l_i_racocimi_temp BY matnr.
      DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_i_racocimi_temp
                                        COMPARING matnr.
      IF l_i_racocimi_temp[] IS NOT INITIAL.
        SELECT matnr maktx FROM makt
                           INTO TABLE l_i_makt
                           FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_racocimi_temp
                           WHERE matnr EQ l_i_racocimi_temp-matnr
                             AND spras EQ sy-langu.
        IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
          SORT l_i_makt BY matnr.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-002

***Start of addition for MOD-005
    SELECT tour_id
          check_id FROM /dsd/hh_racocihd
          INTO TABLE l_i_racocihd
          FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_status
          WHERE  tour_id = l_i_status-tourid.
    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.

      IF l_i_racocihd IS NOT INITIAL.
        SELECT * FROM /dsd/hh_racocici INTO
           TABLE l_i_racocici                              "#EC CI_STAR
           FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_racocihd
           WHERE  tour_id = l_i_racocihd-tour_id
           AND check_id = l_i_racocihd-check_id.
        IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
          CLEAR l_i_racocici.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

    ENDIF.

***end of addition for MOD-005

  ENDIF.

***Start of comment for MOD-005
*  IF l_i_racocimi[] IS NOT INITIAL.
*    SELECT * FROM /dsd/hh_racocici INTO
*           TABLE l_i_racocici "#EC CI_STAR
*         FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_racocimi
*         WHERE  tour_id = l_i_racocimi-tour_id
*         AND check_id = l_i_racocimi-check_id
*         AND itemnr = l_i_racocimi-itemnr.
*  ENDIF.
***End of comment for MOD-005

  SORT:l_i_racocici BY tour_id check_id itemnr,
       l_i_status BY tourid.
  LOOP AT l_i_racocimi ASSIGNING <l_fs_racocimi>.
    MOVE-CORRESPONDING <l_fs_racocimi> TO l_wa_final3.  "#EC CI_MOVCPND

    READ TABLE l_i_racocici ASSIGNING <l_fs_racocici>
    WITH KEY tour_id = <l_fs_racocimi>-tour_id
             check_id = <l_fs_racocimi>-check_id
             itemnr = <l_fs_racocimi>-itemnr BINARY SEARCH.
    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
      l_wa_final3-amount = <l_fs_racocici>-amount.
      l_wa_final3-curr = <l_fs_racocici>-curr.
      l_wa_final3-paymt = <l_fs_racocici>-paymt.
      UNASSIGN <l_fs_racocici>.
    ENDIF.

    READ TABLE l_i_status ASSIGNING <l_fs_status> WITH KEY tourid =
    <l_fs_racocimi>-tour_id BINARY SEARCH.
    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
      IF rb_ship EQ abap_true.
        l_wa_final3-shipment = <l_fs_status>-shipment.
      ELSEIF rb_visi EQ abap_true.
        l_wa_final3-shipment  = <l_fs_status>-vlid.
      ENDIF.
      l_wa_final3-status_id = <l_fs_status>-status_id.
      READ TABLE i_inb_stat ASSIGNING <l_fs_stat>
      WITH KEY vlid = <l_fs_status>-vlid  BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
        l_wa_final3-werks = <l_fs_stat>-werks.
        l_wa_final3-route = <l_fs_stat>-route.
        l_wa_final3-date  = <l_fs_stat>-date.
        UNASSIGN <l_fs_stat>.
      ENDIF.
      UNASSIGN <l_fs_status>.
    ENDIF.

    "Amount
    PERFORM f_currency_conversion
                  CHANGING l_wa_final3-amount  l_v_value.

***start of addition for MOD-002
    READ TABLE l_i_makt ASSIGNING <l_fs_makt> WITH KEY
                                      matnr = l_wa_final3-matnr
                                      BINARY SEARCH.
    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
      l_wa_final3-maktx = <l_fs_makt>-maktx.
      UNASSIGN <l_fs_makt>.
    ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-002
    APPEND l_wa_final3 TO i_final3.
    CLEAR l_wa_final3.
  ENDLOOP.
  IF <l_fs_racocimi> IS ASSIGNED.
    UNASSIGN <l_fs_racocimi>.
  ENDIF.

*EOC For MOD-018
*Filter by Matnr
  IF s_matnr IS NOT INITIAL.
    DELETE i_final3 WHERE matnr NOT IN s_matnr.
  ENDIF.

*Filter by checkid
  IF s_chkid IS NOT INITIAL.
    DELETE i_final3 WHERE check_id NOT IN s_chkid.
  ENDIF.
*EOC For MOD-018

ENDFORM.                    " F_GET_CHECK
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_STATUS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      Status ID based on Shipment/Visit List
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_status  CHANGING fp_status TYPE tb_t_status.

**  DATA:l_i_inb_stat  TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_/ccej/t_inb_stat.
  IF rb_ship EQ abap_true.
    SELECT tourid
           status_id
           shipment
           vlid  FROM /dsd/st_status INTO TABLE fp_status WHERE shipment
           IN s_tknum.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR  fp_status.
    ENDIF.
  ELSEIF rb_visi EQ abap_true.
    IF s_tknum[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      IF rb_driv IS NOT INITIAL.
        SELECT tourid
          status_id
          shipment
          vlid  FROM /dsd/st_status
          INTO TABLE fp_status
          WHERE vlid IN s_tknum AND status_id IN s_status.
        IF sy-subrc NE 0.
          CLEAR  fp_status.
        ENDIF.
      ELSE.
        SELECT tourid
               status_id
               shipment
               vlid  FROM /dsd/st_status
               INTO TABLE fp_status
               WHERE vlid IN s_tknum.
        IF sy-subrc NE 0.
          CLEAR  fp_status.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ELSE.
      "Get visit list data based on plant/route/settlement date
      PERFORM f_get_vl_2  CHANGING i_inb_stat.

      IF i_inb_stat[] IS NOT INITIAL.
        SORT i_inb_stat BY vlid.

        IF rb_driv IS NOT INITIAL.
          SELECT tourid
            status_id
            shipment
            vlid  FROM /dsd/st_status
                       INTO TABLE fp_status
                       FOR ALL ENTRIES IN i_inb_stat
***start of comment for MOD-005
**                        WHERE vlid EQ i_inb_stat-vlid
**                       AND status_id IN s_status.
***end of comment for MOD-005

***start of addition for MOD-005
                       WHERE status_id IN s_status
                       AND vlid EQ i_inb_stat-vlid.
***end of addition for MOD-005
          IF sy-subrc NE 0.
            CLEAR  fp_status.
          ENDIF.
        ELSE.
          SELECT tourid
                 status_id
                 shipment
                 vlid FROM /dsd/st_status
                            INTO TABLE fp_status
                            FOR ALL ENTRIES IN i_inb_stat
                            WHERE vlid EQ i_inb_stat-vlid.
          IF sy-subrc NE 0.
            CLEAR  fp_status.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

    ENDIF.
    IF i_inb_stat[] IS INITIAL.
      IF fp_status[] IS NOT INITIAL.
*   get Plant/route/settlement date based on visit list
        SELECT werks
               route
               creation_date
               visitlist
***start of addition for MOD-022
               idoc_number
               zzernam
               zzerzzt
***end of addition for MOD-022
                FROM /ccej/t_inb_stat                   "#EC CI_NOFIRST
                         INTO TABLE i_inb_stat
                         FOR ALL ENTRIES IN fp_status
                          WHERE visitlist = fp_status-vlid.
        IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
          CLEAR i_inb_stat.
        ELSE.
          SORT i_inb_stat BY vlid.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                    " F_STATUS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_MODE_CHOOSE
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*     Fetching data based on Mode of Radio Button
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_mode_choose .

**"Fetch data from table
  IF rb_ship EQ abap_true AND rb_fsdoc EQ abap_true.
    ""If Shipment and FSR documents selected
    PERFORM f_get_shipment_data. ""Based on shipment no.

  ELSEIF rb_visi EQ abap_true AND rb_fsdoc EQ abap_true.
    ""If Visit list and FSR documents selected
    PERFORM f_get_visit_list_data. ""Based on Visit list
  ENDIF.

  IF ( rb_ship EQ abap_true OR rb_visi EQ abap_true ) AND rb_driv
  EQ abap_true.""If Shipment/Visit List and Tour Details selected
    PERFORM f_get_driver_details. ""Tour details data
  ELSEIF ( rb_ship EQ abap_true OR rb_visi EQ abap_true ) AND rb_visit
  EQ abap_true. ""If Shipment/Visit List and Visit Details selected
    PERFORM f_get_visit_details. ""Visit details data
  ELSEIF ( rb_ship EQ abap_true OR rb_visi EQ abap_true ) AND rb_slrep
  EQ abap_true. ""If Shipment/Visit List and sales selected
    PERFORM f_get_sales. ""Sales replenishment data

  ELSEIF ( rb_ship EQ abap_true OR rb_visi EQ abap_true ) AND rb_patdt
  EQ abap_true. ""If Shipment/Visit List and payment selected
    PERFORM f_get_payment. ""Payment data

  ELSEIF ( rb_ship EQ abap_true OR rb_visi EQ abap_true ) AND rb_check
  EQ abap_true. ""If Shipment/Visit List and Check Out/In selected
    PERFORM f_get_check. ""Check Out/In data

  ELSEIF ( rb_ship EQ abap_true OR rb_visi EQ abap_true ) AND rb_money
  EQ abap_true. ""If Shipment/Visit List and Money differences selected
    PERFORM f_get_money. ""Money differences

  ELSEIF ( rb_ship EQ abap_true OR rb_visi EQ abap_true ) AND rb_quan EQ
  abap_true. ""If Shipment/Visit List and Quantity differences selected
    PERFORM f_get_quan . ""Quantity differences
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " F_MODE_CHOOSE
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_GET_MONEY
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*   Fetching Money Differences related Info.
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_get_money.
*********************Internal Table Declaration***********************
  DATA:l_i_sld_item TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_sld_item,
       l_i_mbal     TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF /dsd/sl_sld_mbal.
*********************Data Declaration********************************
  DATA:l_wa_final4 TYPE ty_final4,
       l_v_value   TYPE bapicurr-bapicurr. "Contolling area currency
*********************Field Symbols Declaration************************
  FIELD-SYMBOLS:<l_fs_sld_item> TYPE ty_sld_item,
                <l_fs_mbal>     TYPE /dsd/sl_sld_mbal,
                <l_fs_stat>     TYPE  ty_/ccej/t_inb_stat.
**********************************************************************
  PERFORM f_get_sld_item USING s_tknum[] CHANGING l_i_sld_item.

  IF  l_i_sld_item[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT * FROM /dsd/sl_sld_mbal                         "#EC CI_STAR
            INTO TABLE l_i_mbal
            FOR ALL ENTRIES
            IN l_i_sld_item
            WHERE sld_doc_id = l_i_sld_item-sld_doc_id
*           BOC for MOD-019
              AND payment_type IN s_pay
              AND amount_diff IN s_mdiff.
*           EOC for MOD-019

    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR l_i_mbal.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  SORT:l_i_mbal BY sld_doc_id,
       l_i_sld_item BY sld_doc_id.
  LOOP AT l_i_mbal ASSIGNING <l_fs_mbal>.
    MOVE-CORRESPONDING <l_fs_mbal> TO l_wa_final4.      "#EC CI_MOVCPND
    READ TABLE l_i_sld_item ASSIGNING <l_fs_sld_item> WITH KEY
    sld_doc_id  = <l_fs_mbal>-sld_doc_id BINARY SEARCH.
    IF  sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
      l_wa_final4-shipment = <l_fs_sld_item>-shipment.
      l_wa_final4-tour_id = <l_fs_sld_item>-tour_id.

      READ TABLE i_inb_stat ASSIGNING <l_fs_stat>
      WITH KEY vlid = <l_fs_sld_item>-shipment(10) BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
        l_wa_final4-werks = <l_fs_stat>-werks.
        l_wa_final4-route = <l_fs_stat>-route.
        l_wa_final4-date  = <l_fs_stat>-date.
        UNASSIGN <l_fs_stat>.
      ENDIF.
      UNASSIGN <l_fs_sld_item>.
    ENDIF.
    "Conversion of Currency Amounts into External Data Format

    "Check-Out Control
    PERFORM f_currency_conversion
            CHANGING  l_wa_final4-amount_co	 l_v_value.
    "Expenditures Amount
    PERFORM f_currency_conversion
            CHANGING  l_wa_final4-amount_expenses	 l_v_value.
    "Receipts
    PERFORM f_currency_conversion
            CHANGING  l_wa_final4-amount_earnings	 l_v_value.
    "Check-in Amount
    PERFORM f_currency_conversion
        CHANGING  l_wa_final4-amount_ci	 l_v_value.
    "Difference Amount
    PERFORM f_currency_conversion
        CHANGING  l_wa_final4-amount_diff	 l_v_value.
    "Planned Amount
    PERFORM f_currency_conversion
        CHANGING  l_wa_final4-amount_plan	 l_v_value.
    APPEND l_wa_final4 TO i_final4.
    CLEAR l_wa_final4.
  ENDLOOP.
  IF  <l_fs_mbal> IS ASSIGNED.
    UNASSIGN <l_fs_mbal>.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " F_GET_MONEY
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_GET_SLD_ITEM
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*   Fetching Settlement document item data
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_get_sld_item  USING fp_tknum TYPE ty_tknum CHANGING fp_sld_item
TYPE tb_sld_item.
*********************Constants Declaration****************************
  CONSTANTS:l_c_doc_typ  TYPE /dsd/hh_doc_typ VALUE '10',
            "For Shipment type
            l_c_doc_typ1 TYPE /dsd/hh_doc_typ VALUE '20'.
  "For Visit List type
**********************************************************************
  IF  rb_ship EQ abap_true .

    SELECT sld_doc_id
         tour_id
         obj_typ
         obj_id
        FROM /dsd/sl_sld_item INTO TABLE fp_sld_item
            WHERE obj_typ = l_c_doc_typ AND obj_id IN fp_tknum.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR fp_sld_item.
    ENDIF.

  ELSEIF rb_visi EQ abap_true.

    IF fp_tknum[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT sld_doc_id
        tour_id
        obj_typ
        obj_id
       FROM /dsd/sl_sld_item INTO TABLE fp_sld_item
           WHERE obj_typ = l_c_doc_typ1 AND obj_id IN fp_tknum.

      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR fp_sld_item.
      ENDIF.

    ELSE.
      "Get visit list data based on plant/route/settlement date
      PERFORM f_get_vl_2  CHANGING i_inb_stat.

      IF i_inb_stat[] IS NOT INITIAL.
        SORT i_inb_stat BY vlid.
        SELECT sld_doc_id
          tour_id
          obj_typ
          obj_id
         FROM /dsd/sl_sld_item INTO TABLE fp_sld_item
             FOR ALL ENTRIES IN i_inb_stat
             WHERE obj_typ = l_c_doc_typ1
              AND obj_id = i_inb_stat-vlid.

        IF sy-subrc NE 0.
          CLEAR fp_sld_item.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    IF i_inb_stat[] IS INITIAL.
      IF fp_sld_item[] IS NOT INITIAL.
*   get Plant/route/settlement date based on visit list
        SELECT werks
               route
               creation_date
               visitlist
***start of addition for MOD-022
               idoc_number
               zzernam
               zzerzzt
***end of addition for MOD-022
          FROM /ccej/t_inb_stat                         "#EC CI_NOFIRST
                         INTO TABLE i_inb_stat
                         FOR ALL ENTRIES IN fp_sld_item
                          WHERE visitlist = fp_sld_item-shipment(10).
        IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
          CLEAR i_inb_stat.
        ELSE.
          SORT i_inb_stat BY vlid.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " F_GET_SLD_ITEM
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_GET_QUAN
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*    Fetching Quantity Differences related Info.
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_get_quan.

*********************Internal Table Declaration***********************
  DATA:l_i_sld_item TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_sld_item,
       l_i_qbal     TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF  /dsd/sl_sld_qbal.
*********************Data Declaration*********************************
  DATA:l_wa_final5   TYPE ty_final5.

***start of addition for MOD-002
  DATA: l_i_qbal_temp TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF /dsd/sl_sld_qbal,
        l_i_makt      TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_makt.
***end of addition for MOD-002

*********************Field Symbols Declaration************************
  FIELD-SYMBOLS:<l_fs_sld_item> TYPE ty_sld_item,
                <l_fs_qbal>     TYPE /dsd/sl_sld_qbal,
                <l_fs_stat>     TYPE  ty_/ccej/t_inb_stat,
***start of addition for MOD-002
                <l_fs_makt>     TYPE ty_makt.
***end of addition for MOD-002
**********************************************************************
  PERFORM f_get_sld_item USING s_tknum[] CHANGING l_i_sld_item.

  IF  l_i_sld_item[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT * FROM /dsd/sl_sld_qbal INTO TABLE l_i_qbal
          FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_sld_item                  "#EC CI_STAR
          WHERE sld_doc_id = l_i_sld_item-sld_doc_id
                "BOC For MOD-014
                AND matnr IN s_matnr
                AND quan_final_diff IN s_qty.
    "EOC For MOD-014
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR l_i_qbal.
***start of addition for MOD-002
    ELSE.
      l_i_qbal_temp[] = l_i_qbal[].
      SORT l_i_qbal_temp BY matnr.
      DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_i_qbal_temp COMPARING matnr.
      IF l_i_qbal_temp[] IS NOT INITIAL.
*      get material description
        SELECT matnr maktx FROM makt
                           INTO TABLE l_i_makt
                           FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_qbal_temp
                           WHERE matnr EQ l_i_qbal_temp-matnr
                             AND spras EQ sy-langu.
        IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
          SORT l_i_makt BY matnr.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-002
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  SORT:l_i_qbal BY sld_doc_id,
       l_i_sld_item BY sld_doc_id.
  LOOP AT l_i_qbal ASSIGNING <l_fs_qbal>.
    MOVE-CORRESPONDING <l_fs_qbal> TO l_wa_final5.      "#EC CI_MOVCPND
    READ TABLE l_i_sld_item ASSIGNING <l_fs_sld_item> WITH KEY
    sld_doc_id  = <l_fs_qbal>-sld_doc_id BINARY SEARCH.
    IF  sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
      l_wa_final5-shipment = <l_fs_sld_item>-shipment.
      READ TABLE i_inb_stat ASSIGNING <l_fs_stat>
     WITH KEY vlid = <l_fs_sld_item>-shipment(10)  BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
        l_wa_final5-werks = <l_fs_stat>-werks.
        l_wa_final5-route = <l_fs_stat>-route.
        l_wa_final5-date  = <l_fs_stat>-date.
        UNASSIGN <l_fs_stat>.
      ENDIF.
      UNASSIGN <l_fs_sld_item>.
    ENDIF.

***start of addition for MOD-002
    READ TABLE l_i_makt ASSIGNING <l_fs_makt> WITH KEY
                                             matnr = l_wa_final5-matnr
                                             BINARY SEARCH.
    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
      l_wa_final5-maktx = <l_fs_makt>-maktx.
    ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-002
    APPEND l_wa_final5 TO i_final5.
    CLEAR l_wa_final5.
  ENDLOOP.
  IF  <l_fs_qbal> IS ASSIGNED.
    UNASSIGN <l_fs_qbal>.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " F_GET_QUAN
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_RAHD
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*     Fetching Tour Header Data
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_rahd  USING  fp_status TYPE tb_t_status
             CHANGING fp_rahd TYPE tb_t_hh_rahd.

  IF fp_status[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT tour_id
         driver
         credate
*        BOC MOD-017
         cretime
         creuser
*        EOC MOD-017
         FROM /dsd/hh_rahd  INTO TABLE  fp_rahd FOR ALL ENTRIES
         IN fp_status
                 WHERE tour_id = fp_status-tourid.

    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR fp_rahd.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                    " F_RAHD
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_RACVHD
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*     Fetching Customer Visit - Header Data
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_racvhd  USING  fp_rahd TYPE tb_t_hh_rahd
               CHANGING fp_racvhd TYPE tb_t_hh_racvhd.

  IF fp_rahd[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT tour_id
         visit_id
         custnr FROM /dsd/hh_racvhd	INTO TABLE fp_racvhd FOR ALL ENTRIES
         IN fp_rahd
                WHERE tour_id = fp_rahd-tour_id.

    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR fp_racvhd.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                    " F_RACVHD
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_GET_VISIT_DETAILS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*    Fetching Visit details data
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_get_visit_details.
*********************Internal Table Declaration***********************
  DATA:l_i_status  TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_/dsd/st_status,
       l_i_rahd    TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_/dsd/hh_rahd,
       l_i_racvhd  TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_hh_racvhd,
       l_i_kna1    TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_kna1,
*      BOC for MOD-020
       l_i_vc_vlh  TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_dsd_vc_vlh,
       l_wa_vc_vlh TYPE ty_dsd_vc_vlh,
       l_i_vc_vlp  TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_dsd_vc_vlp.
*      EOC for MOD-020
*********************Data Declaration********************************
  DATA:l_wa_final6   TYPE ty_final6.
*********************Field Symbols Declaration************************
  FIELD-SYMBOLS:<l_fs_status> TYPE  ty_/dsd/st_status,
                <l_fs_rahd>   TYPE  ty_/dsd/hh_rahd,
                <l_fs_racvhd> TYPE ty_hh_racvhd,
                <l_fs_kna1>   TYPE ty_kna1,
                <l_fs_stat>   TYPE  ty_/ccej/t_inb_stat.
**********************************************************************
* BOC MOD-017
  CONSTANTS:"Green light
    l_c_green  TYPE c VALUE '3',
    "Red light
    l_c_red    TYPE c VALUE '1',
    "Yellow light
    l_c_yellow TYPE c VALUE '2'.
*Get FSR Status Selected
  PERFORM f_fsr_status.
*Get Exclustion user list
  PERFORM f_user_name.
* EOC MOD-017

  PERFORM f_status CHANGING l_i_status.""Getting the status data

  IF l_i_status[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    PERFORM f_rahd USING l_i_status CHANGING l_i_rahd.
    ""Getting the Route settlement
  ENDIF.
  IF l_i_rahd[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    ""Getting Customer visit data
    SELECT tour_id visit_id custnr vkorg vtweg spart viscod cngdate
    cngtime cnguser status man_proc
              FROM /dsd/hh_racvhd INTO TABLE l_i_racvhd FOR ALL ENTRIES
              IN l_i_rahd
            WHERE tour_id = l_i_rahd-tour_id.

    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR l_i_racvhd .
    ENDIF.

*   BOC for MOD-020
*   Get Visit List from tour id
    CLEAR:l_i_vc_vlh[].
    LOOP AT l_i_rahd ASSIGNING <l_fs_rahd>.
      l_wa_vc_vlh-vlid = <l_fs_rahd>-tour_id+2(10).
      APPEND l_wa_vc_vlh TO l_i_vc_vlh.
      CLEAR l_wa_vc_vlh.
    ENDLOOP.

*   DSD: Visit List - Item Data
    IF l_i_vc_vlh[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      SORT l_i_vc_vlh BY vlid.
      DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_i_vc_vlh
      COMPARING vlid.
      SELECT vlid kunnr
        FROM /dsd/vc_vlp
        INTO TABLE l_i_vc_vlp
        FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_vc_vlh
        WHERE vlid = l_i_vc_vlh-vlid.
      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
        SORT l_i_vc_vlp BY vlid kunnr.
      ENDIF.

    ENDIF.
*   EOC for MOD-020
  ENDIF.

  IF l_i_racvhd[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    PERFORM f_kna1 USING l_i_racvhd  CHANGING l_i_kna1.
    ""Getting Customer Header Data
  ENDIF.

  SORT:l_i_status BY tourid,
       l_i_rahd BY tour_id,
       l_i_kna1 BY kunnr.
  LOOP AT l_i_racvhd  ASSIGNING <l_fs_racvhd>.

    l_wa_final6-visit_id = <l_fs_racvhd>-visit_id.
    l_wa_final6-custnr = <l_fs_racvhd>-custnr.
    l_wa_final6-vkorg = <l_fs_racvhd>-vkorg.
    l_wa_final6-vtweg = <l_fs_racvhd>-vtweg.
    l_wa_final6-spart = <l_fs_racvhd>-spart.
    l_wa_final6-viscod = <l_fs_racvhd>-viscod.
    l_wa_final6-cngdate =  <l_fs_racvhd>-cngdate .
    l_wa_final6-cngtime =   <l_fs_racvhd>-cngtime.
    l_wa_final6-cnguser =   <l_fs_racvhd>-cnguser.
    l_wa_final6-status  =   <l_fs_racvhd>-status.
    l_wa_final6-man_proc =   <l_fs_racvhd>-man_proc.


    READ TABLE l_i_kna1 ASSIGNING <l_fs_kna1> WITH KEY kunnr =
    <l_fs_racvhd>-custnr BINARY SEARCH.
    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
      l_wa_final6-ktokd = <l_fs_kna1>-ktokd.
      l_wa_final6-katr4 = <l_fs_kna1>-katr4.
***start of addition for MOD-002
      l_wa_final6-/scl/equp_ownr = <l_fs_kna1>-/scl/equp_ownr.
***end of addition for MOD-002
      UNASSIGN  <l_fs_kna1>.
    ENDIF.

    READ TABLE l_i_rahd ASSIGNING <l_fs_rahd> WITH KEY tour_id =
    <l_fs_racvhd>-tour_id BINARY SEARCH.
    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
      l_wa_final6-driver = <l_fs_rahd>-driver.
      l_wa_final6-credate = <l_fs_rahd>-credate.
*Set Time zone
      IF <l_fs_rahd>-creuser NOT IN r_user
        AND l_wa_final6-credate IS NOT INITIAL
        AND <l_fs_rahd>-cretime IS NOT INITIAL.
        PERFORM f_get_local_timezone
                   USING   <l_fs_rahd>-cretime
                   CHANGING l_wa_final6-credate
                            <l_fs_rahd>-cretime.
      ENDIF.
*     EOC MOD-017
      UNASSIGN  <l_fs_rahd>.
    ENDIF.

    READ TABLE l_i_status ASSIGNING <l_fs_status> WITH KEY tourid =
    <l_fs_racvhd>-tour_id BINARY SEARCH.
    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
      IF rb_ship EQ abap_true.
        l_wa_final6-shipment = <l_fs_status>-shipment.
      ELSEIF  rb_visi EQ abap_true.
        l_wa_final6-shipment = <l_fs_status>-vlid.
      ENDIF.
      l_wa_final6-status_id = <l_fs_status>-status_id.
      READ TABLE i_inb_stat ASSIGNING <l_fs_stat>
       WITH KEY vlid = <l_fs_status>-vlid  BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
        l_wa_final6-werks = <l_fs_stat>-werks.
        l_wa_final6-route = <l_fs_stat>-route.
        l_wa_final6-date  = <l_fs_stat>-date.
        UNASSIGN <l_fs_stat>.
      ENDIF.

*     BOC MOD-017
*Set lights as per man_proc status
      IF l_wa_final6-man_proc = abap_true.
        l_wa_final6-fsr_status = l_c_green.
      ELSE.
        l_wa_final6-fsr_status = l_c_yellow.
      ENDIF.
*Set Time zone
      IF l_wa_final6-cnguser NOT IN r_user
        AND l_wa_final6-cngtime IS NOT INITIAL
        AND l_wa_final6-cngdate IS NOT INITIAL.
        PERFORM f_get_local_timezone
                   USING   l_wa_final6-cngtime
                   CHANGING l_wa_final6-cngdate
                            l_wa_final6-cngtime.
      ENDIF.
*     EOC MOD-017

*     BOC for MOD-020
*     DSD: Visit List - Item Data
      READ TABLE l_i_vc_vlp TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS
      WITH KEY vlid = <l_fs_racvhd>-tour_id+2(10)
               kunnr = <l_fs_racvhd>-custnr
               BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
*       Visited
        IF l_wa_final6-man_proc = abap_true.
          l_wa_final6-fsr_status = l_c_green.
          l_wa_final6-vlog = c_visited.
        ELSE.
*       Planned but not visited
          l_wa_final6-fsr_status = l_c_yellow.
          l_wa_final6-vlog = c_plan_not_visit.
        ENDIF.
      ELSE.
*       Un Planned
        l_wa_final6-fsr_status = l_c_yellow.
        l_wa_final6-vlog = c_unplanned.
      ENDIF.
*     EOC for MOD-020
      APPEND l_wa_final6 TO i_final6.
      CLEAR l_wa_final6.
      UNASSIGN <l_fs_status>.
    ENDIF.

  ENDLOOP.
  IF <l_fs_racvhd> IS ASSIGNED.
    UNASSIGN <l_fs_racvhd>.
  ENDIF.

*BOC For MOD-014
*Filter Data by Equipment Owner
  IF s_equp IS NOT INITIAL.
    DELETE i_final6 WHERE /scl/equp_ownr NOT IN s_equp.
  ENDIF.

*Filter by customer
  IF s_kunnr IS NOT INITIAL.
    DELETE i_final6 WHERE custnr NOT IN s_kunnr.
  ENDIF.

*Filter by business extension
  IF s_katr4 IS NOT INITIAL.
    DELETE i_final6 WHERE katr4 NOT IN s_katr4.
  ENDIF.
*EOC For MOD-014

*BOC For MOD-017
*Filter FSR Status
***start of commenting for MOD-025
*  IF s_light-low IS NOT INITIAL AND v_fsr_status IS NOT INITIAL.
*    DELETE i_final6 WHERE fsr_status NE v_fsr_status.
***end of commenting for MOD-025
***start of addition for MOD-025
  IF s_light-low IS NOT INITIAL AND r_fsr_status[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    DELETE i_final6 WHERE fsr_status NOT IN r_fsr_status.
***end of addition for MOD-025
  ENDIF.
*EOC For MOD-017

*BOC For MOD-020
*Filter Visit Log Status
  IF s_vlog IS NOT INITIAL.
    DELETE i_final6 WHERE vlog NOT IN s_vlog.
  ENDIF.
*EOC For MOD-020

ENDFORM.                    " F_GET_VISIT_DETAILS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
