*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_kna1  USING    fp_racvhd TYPE tb_t_racvhd
             CHANGING fp_kna1   TYPE tb_t_kna1.
****************Internal Table Declaration****************************
  DATA:l_i_racvhd_tmp TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_hh_racvhd.
**********************************************************************
  l_i_racvhd_tmp[] = fp_racvhd[].
  SORT l_i_racvhd_tmp BY custnr.
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_i_racvhd_tmp COMPARING custnr.
  IF l_i_racvhd_tmp[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT kunnr ktokd
***start of addition for MOD-011
           katr3
***end of addition for MOD-011
           katr4
***start of addition for MOD-002
           /scl/equp_ownr
***end of addition for MOD-002
      FROM kna1 INTO TABLE fp_kna1 FOR ALL
    ENTRIES IN
                     l_i_racvhd_tmp WHERE kunnr = l_i_racvhd_tmp-custnr.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR fp_kna1.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                    " F_KNA1
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_SPLIT_VALUE
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  Split original qty as money type and split ID.
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_split_value CHANGING fp_money TYPE n
                            fp_set TYPE n
                            fp_emoney_code TYPE n
                            fp_split TYPE char17.

*********************Data Declaration*********************************
  DATA:l_v_dec    TYPE char3,                 "#EC NEEDED "Decimal data
       l_v_int    TYPE char9, "Integer data
       l_v_length TYPE i, "Field length
       l_v_len    TYPE i.
*********************Constants Declaration****************************
  CONSTANTS:l_c_ind  TYPE c VALUE '.', "Split Indicator
            l_c_zero TYPE c VALUE '0'.
**********************************************************************
  CLEAR l_v_int.
  CONDENSE fp_split.
  SPLIT fp_split AT l_c_ind INTO l_v_int l_v_dec.
  l_v_length = strlen( l_v_int ).

  IF l_v_length LT 9.
    l_v_len = 9 - l_v_length.

    DO l_v_len TIMES.
      CONCATENATE l_c_zero l_v_int INTO l_v_int.
    ENDDO.
  ENDIF.
  fp_emoney_code = l_v_int+0(4). "First 4 char
  fp_set = l_v_int+4(3). "Next 3 char
  fp_money = l_v_int+7(2). "Next 2 char

ENDFORM.                    " F_SPLIT_VALUE
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_VALIDATION
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*    Selection screen validations
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_validation.
*****************Data Declaration***************************
  DATA:l_v_tknum   TYPE tknum, "Shipment no
       l_v_status  TYPE /dsd/st_status_id, "status
       l_v_tplst   TYPE tplst, "Transportation planning point
       l_v_route   TYPE route, "Route ID
       l_v_vehicle TYPE /dsd/rp_truck, "Vehicle
       l_v_driver  TYPE /dsd/rp_driver1, "Driver
       l_v_visit   TYPE /dsd/vc_vlid, "Visit List
       l_v_plant   TYPE werks_d,    "Plant
       l_v_vkorg   TYPE  ty_vkorg, "Sales organisation
***start of addition for MOD-005
       l_v_days    TYPE i. "No of days
***end of addition for MOD-005
*****************Internal Table Declaration***************************
  DATA:l_i_tplst TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_tplst,
       l_i_vkorg TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_vkorg,
       l_i_visit TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_/ccej/t_inb_stat,
       "BOC For MOD-014
       l_i_dd07v TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF dd07v.
  "EOC For MOD-014
******************Field Symbols Declaration***************************
  FIELD-SYMBOLS:<l_fs_tplst> TYPE ty_tplst,
                <l_fs_vkorg> TYPE ty_vkorg,
                <l_fs_visit> TYPE  ty_/ccej/t_inb_stat.
******************Constants Declaration*******************************
  CONSTANTS:l_c_vttk   TYPE xuobject VALUE 'V_VTTK_TDS',
            "TPP Authorization Obj.
            l_c_trans  TYPE xuobject VALUE 'TPLST', "TPP Field
            l_c_disact TYPE xuobject VALUE 'ACTVT', "Activity field
            l_c_dis    TYPE char2 VALUE '03', "Display
            l_c_vkorg  TYPE xuobject VALUE 'ZVKORG',
            "Sales Org Authorization Obj.
            l_c_sal    TYPE xuobject VALUE 'VKORG', "Sales org. field
            l_c_error  TYPE c VALUE 'E', "Error msg
***start of addition for MOD-005
            l_c_days   TYPE i VALUE '15',
***end of addition for MOD-005
            "BOC For MOD-015
            "FSR Status domain
            l_c_domain TYPE dd07l-domname
                         VALUE '/CCEJ/OTC_PROC_STATUS'.
  "EOC For MOD-015
**********************************************************************

  IF s_werks-low IS INITIAL OR s_route1-low IS INITIAL
      OR s_date[] IS INITIAL.
    IF s_tknum[] IS INITIAL AND s_hht[] IS INITIAL.
**"Showing message if Shipment/Visit list/HHT document empty
***start of changes for MOD-002
*      MESSAGE i080.
      MESSAGE i525.
***end of changes for MOD-002
      LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

***start of changes for MOD-002
*  IF s_tknum[] IS NOT INITIAL OR s_hht[] IS NOT INITIAL.
*    IF s_werks-low IS NOT INITIAL AND s_route1-low IS NOT INITIAL
*        AND s_date[] IS NOT INITIAL.
*****"Showing message if either plant/route/settlement date empty
*      MESSAGE i525.
*      LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
*    ENDIF.
*  ENDIF.

  IF s_tknum[] IS NOT INITIAL OR
     ( s_werks-low IS NOT INITIAL AND s_route1-low IS NOT INITIAL
       AND s_date-low IS NOT INITIAL ).
*    do nothing
  ELSE.
    MESSAGE i525.
    LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
  ENDIF.
***end of changes for MOD-002


  IF s_tknum[] IS INITIAL AND s_hht[] IS  INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE werks FROM t001w INTO l_v_plant
       WHERE werks IN s_werks.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE i012 DISPLAY LIKE l_c_error.
      LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  "When Shipment selected
  IF rb_ship EQ abap_true.

*TPP should be valid
    IF s_tplst[] IS NOT INITIAL.
" *****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_S Conversion **begin of change by EJX9007360 2026.06.11 ATC
* SELECT SINGLE tplst FROM ttds INTO l_v_tplst
      SELECT SINGLE tplst FROM ttds INTO l_v_tplst "#EC CI_USAGE_OK[2270199]
" *****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_S Conversion **end of change by EJX9007360 2026.06.11 ATC
             WHERE tplst IN s_tplst.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        MESSAGE i125 DISPLAY LIKE l_c_error.
        LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

*Shipment no. should be valid
    IF s_tknum[] IS NOT INITIAL.
" *****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_S Conversion **begin of change by EJX9007360 2026.06.11 ATC
* SELECT SINGLE tknum FROM  vttk INTO l_v_tknum
      SELECT SINGLE tknum FROM  vttk INTO l_v_tknum "#EC CI_USAGE_OK[2270199]
" *****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_S Conversion **end of change by EJX9007360 2026.06.11 ATC
                   WHERE tknum IN s_tknum.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        MESSAGE i123 DISPLAY LIKE l_c_error.
        LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

*TPP Authorization Obj.
**    SELECT tplst FROM vttk INTO TABLE l_i_tplst
**                 WHERE tknum IN s_tknum.
**    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
**      LOOP AT l_i_tplst ASSIGNING <l_fs_tplst>.
**        AUTHORITY-CHECK OBJECT l_c_vttk
**                 ID l_c_trans FIELD <l_fs_tplst>-tplst
**                 ID l_c_disact FIELD l_c_dis.           "#EC
*AUTFLD_WRO
**        IF sy-subrc NE 0.
**          MESSAGE i024 WITH text-003 <l_fs_tplst>-tplst DISPLAY LIKE
*l_c_error.
**          LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
**        ENDIF.
**      ENDLOOP.
**      IF <l_fs_tplst> IS ASSIGNED.
**        UNASSIGN <l_fs_tplst>.
**      ENDIF.
**    ENDIF.

*Status should be valid
    IF s_status[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT SINGLE status_id FROM /dsd/st_cstatus INTO l_v_status
             WHERE status_id IN s_status.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        MESSAGE i124 DISPLAY LIKE l_c_error.
        LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

*Route should be valid
    IF s_route[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT SINGLE route FROM tvro INTO l_v_route
             WHERE route IN s_route.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        MESSAGE i126 DISPLAY LIKE l_c_error.
        LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  "When Shipment/Visit list selected
  IF rb_ship EQ abap_true OR rb_visi EQ abap_true.
*Vehicle should be valid
    IF s_vehic[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT SINGLE equnr FROM equi INTO l_v_vehicle
             WHERE equnr IN s_vehic.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        MESSAGE i127 DISPLAY LIKE l_c_error.
        LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

*Driver should be valid
    IF s_driv[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT SINGLE kunnr FROM kna1 INTO l_v_driver
            WHERE kunnr IN s_driv.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        MESSAGE i128 DISPLAY LIKE l_c_error.
        LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  "When Visit list selected
  IF rb_visi EQ abap_true.
    SELECT SINGLE vlid FROM /dsd/vc_vlh INTO l_v_visit
           WHERE vlid IN s_tknum.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      MESSAGE i129 DISPLAY LIKE l_c_error.
      LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
    ENDIF.

*Sales Org Authorization Obj.
**    SELECT sales_org FROM /dsd/vc_vlh INTO TABLE l_i_vkorg
**          WHERE vlid IN s_tknum.
**    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
**      LOOP AT l_i_vkorg ASSIGNING <l_fs_vkorg>.
**        AUTHORITY-CHECK OBJECT l_c_vkorg
**                 ID l_c_sal FIELD <l_fs_vkorg>-vkorg
**                 ID l_c_disact FIELD l_c_dis.           "#EC
*AUTFLD_WRO
**        IF sy-subrc NE 0.
**          MESSAGE i024 WITH text-004 <l_fs_vkorg>-vkorg DISPLAY LIKE
*l_c_error.
**          LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
**        ENDIF.
**      ENDLOOP.
**      IF <l_fs_vkorg> IS ASSIGNED.
**        UNASSIGN <l_fs_vkorg>.
**      ENDIF.
**    ENDIF.

**    IF s_werks[] IS NOT INITIAL AND s_route1[] IS NOT INITIAL
**            AND s_date[] IS NOT INITIAL.
**      SELECT werks
**             route
**             creation_date
**             visitlist FROM /ccej/t_inb_stat
**                           INTO TABLE l_i_visit
**                           WHERE werks IN s_werks AND
**                                 route IN s_route1 AND
**                                 creation_date IN s_date.
**      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
**        DELETE l_i_visit WHERE vlid IS INITIAL.
**        READ TABLE l_i_visit ASSIGNING <l_fs_visit> INDEX 1.
**        IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
***sales org authorization obj.
**          SELECT SINGLE sales_org FROM /dsd/vc_vlh
**                   INTO l_v_vkorg
**                   WHERE vlid = <l_fs_visit>-vlid.
**          IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
**            AUTHORITY-CHECK OBJECT l_c_vkorg
**                     ID l_c_sal FIELD l_v_vkorg
**                 ID l_c_disact FIELD l_c_dis.           "#ECAUTFLD_WRO
**            IF sy-subrc NE 0.
**              MESSAGE i024 WITH text-004 l_v_vkorg DISPLAY LIKE
**    l_c_error.
**              LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
**            ENDIF.
**            IF <l_fs_vkorg> IS ASSIGNED.
**              UNASSIGN <l_fs_vkorg>.
**            ENDIF.
**          ENDIF.
**          UNASSIGN <l_fs_visit>.
**        ENDIF.
**      ENDIF.
**    ENDIF.

    IF s_tknum[] IS NOT INITIAL AND s_hht[] IS NOT INITIAL
      AND rb_slrep NE abap_true."MOD-019
**"Showing message if Shipment/Visit list/HHT document empty
      MESSAGE i522.
      LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

***start of addition for MOD-005
  IF rb_fsdoc EQ abap_true.
    IF s_date-low IS NOT INITIAL
      AND s_date-high IS NOT INITIAL.
***"Getting no. of days b/w two dates
      CALL FUNCTION 'HR_99S_INTERVAL_BETWEEN_DATES'
        EXPORTING
          begda = s_date-low
          endda = s_date-high
        IMPORTING
          days  = l_v_days.

      IF l_v_days GT l_c_days.

****"Message for no. of days restriction
        MESSAGE i548.
        LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    "BOC For MOD-015
    IF s_light-low IS NOT INITIAL.
*--Read domain value
      CALL FUNCTION 'DD_DOMVALUES_GET'
        EXPORTING
          domname        = l_c_domain
          langu          = sy-langu
        TABLES
          dd07v_tab      = l_i_dd07v
        EXCEPTIONS
          wrong_textflag = 1
          OTHERS         = 2.

      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL AND l_i_dd07v IS NOT INITIAL.
        DELETE l_i_dd07v WHERE domvalue_l NOT IN s_light.
        IF l_i_dd07v IS INITIAL.
*Message for invalid processing status
          MESSAGE i671.
          LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    "EOC For MOD-015
  ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-005
ENDFORM.                    " F_VALIDATION
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_DISPLAY_REPORT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Display ALV Report
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_display_report .

  IF rb_ship EQ abap_true AND rb_fsdoc EQ abap_true.
    ""If Shipment and FSR documents selected

    PERFORM f_display_alv USING i_final.
  ELSEIF rb_visi EQ abap_true AND rb_fsdoc EQ abap_true.
    ""If Visit list and FSR documents selected

    PERFORM f_display_alv USING i_final.
  ENDIF.

  IF ( rb_ship EQ abap_true OR rb_visi EQ abap_true ) AND rb_driv
  EQ abap_true. ""If Shipment/Vist List and Tour Details selected
    PERFORM f_display_alv USING i_final7_status.

  ELSEIF ( rb_ship EQ abap_true OR rb_visi EQ abap_true ) AND rb_visit
  EQ abap_true. ""If Shipment/Vist List and Visit Details selected

    PERFORM f_display_alv USING i_final6.
  ELSEIF ( rb_ship EQ abap_true OR rb_visi EQ abap_true ) AND rb_slrep
  EQ abap_true. ""If Shipment/Visit List and sales selected

    PERFORM f_display_alv USING i_final1.
  ELSEIF ( rb_ship EQ abap_true OR rb_visi EQ abap_true ) AND rb_patdt
  EQ abap_true. ""If Shipment/Visit List and payment selected

    PERFORM f_display_alv USING i_final2.
  ELSEIF ( rb_ship EQ abap_true OR rb_visi EQ abap_true ) AND rb_check
  EQ abap_true. ""If Shipment/Visit List and Check Out/In selected

    PERFORM f_display_alv USING i_final3.
  ELSEIF ( rb_ship EQ abap_true OR rb_visi EQ abap_true ) AND rb_money
  EQ abap_true. ""If Shipment/Visit List and Money differences selected

    PERFORM f_display_alv USING i_final4.
  ELSEIF ( rb_ship EQ abap_true OR rb_visi EQ abap_true ) AND rb_quan EQ
  abap_true. ""If Shipment/Visit List and Quantity differences selected

    PERFORM f_display_alv USING i_final5.

***start of addition for MOD-013
  ELSEIF rb_cash EQ abap_true. "cash difference
    PERFORM f_cash_diff.
***end of addition for MOD-013
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " F_DISPLAY_REPORT

FORM f_display_transaction  USING    fp_row TYPE salv_de_row
                                     fp_column TYPE salv_de_column.
******************Work area Declaration**************************
  DATA:l_wa_final         TYPE ty_final,
       l_wa_final2        TYPE ty_final2,
       l_wa_final7        TYPE ty_hh_rahd, "MOD-017
***start of addition for MOD-022
       l_wa_final7_status TYPE ty_hh_rahd1,
       l_r_date           TYPE RANGE OF edi_ccrdat.
***end of addition for MOD-022
******************Constants Declaration**************************
  CONSTANTS:l_c_so      TYPE salv_de_column VALUE 'VBELN', "Sales order field
            l_c_id      TYPE memoryid VALUE 'AUN', "Paramter ID for SO
            l_c_tcode   TYPE sy-tcode VALUE 'VA03', "Sales display Tcode
            l_c_del     TYPE salv_de_column VALUE 'VBELN_D', "Delivery field
            l_c_id1     TYPE memoryid VALUE 'VL', "Paramter ID for Delivery
            l_c_tcode1  TYPE sy-tcode VALUE 'VL03N', "Delivery Tcode
            l_c_mat     TYPE salv_de_column VALUE 'MBLNR', "Mat. Doc.
            l_c_id2     TYPE memoryid VALUE 'MBN', " Mat. Doc.Paramter id
* *********************************************************
" *****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_S Conversion **begin of change by EJX9007360 2026.06.09 ATC
*

* 'MB03'
* , "Mat. Doc. Tcode
*
              l_c_tcode2  TYPE sy-tcode VALUE
'MIGO' "#EC CI_USAGE_OK[1804812]
              , "Mat. Doc. Tcode (MB03 replaced by MIGO per S/4HANA simplification note 1804812)
" *****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_S Conversion **end of change by EJX9007360 2026.06.09 ATC
* *********************************************************
            l_c_inv     TYPE salv_de_column VALUE 'VBELN_I', "Invoice no.
            l_c_id3     TYPE memoryid VALUE 'VF', " Invoice Paramter id
            l_c_tcode3  TYPE sy-tcode VALUE 'VF03', "Invoice Tcode
            l_c_acc     TYPE salv_de_column VALUE 'BELNR', "Acounting Doc.
            l_c_acc1    TYPE salv_de_column VALUE 'OI_CSH_POST', "Acou. Doc.
            l_c_id4     TYPE memoryid VALUE 'BLN', "Accounting Paramter id
            l_c_id5     TYPE memoryid VALUE 'BUK', "Company Code ID
            l_c_id6     TYPE memoryid VALUE 'GJR', "Fiscal Year ID
            l_c_tcode4  TYPE sy-tcode VALUE 'FB03', "Accounting Tcode
            "Empty field
            l_c_blank   TYPE char10 VALUE '-----------',
            "BOC For MOD-014
            "Intercompany Invoice
            l_c_cominv  TYPE salv_de_column VALUE 'COMINV',
            "Intercompany FI
            l_c_comfi   TYPE salv_de_column VALUE 'COMFI',
            "EOC For MOD-014
            "Tour Id
            l_c_tour_id TYPE salv_de_column VALUE 'OBJ_ID'. "MOD-017

  CLEAR:l_wa_final,l_wa_final2.

  ""Execute when Shipment No./Visit List and FSR Documents is selected
  IF ( rb_ship EQ abap_true OR rb_visi EQ abap_true ) AND rb_fsdoc
  EQ abap_true.
    READ TABLE i_final INTO l_wa_final INDEX fp_row.
    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.

      "Calling Sales order display Tcode
      IF ( fp_column EQ l_c_so ) AND ( l_wa_final-vbeln IS NOT INITIAL
         AND l_wa_final-vbeln NE l_c_blank ).
        SET PARAMETER ID l_c_id FIELD l_wa_final-vbeln.
        CALL TRANSACTION l_c_tcode AND SKIP FIRST SCREEN.
        IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
          LEAVE SCREEN.
        ENDIF.
        "Calling Delivery no. display Tcode
      ELSEIF ( fp_column EQ l_c_del ) AND
             ( l_wa_final-vbeln_d IS NOT INITIAL
              AND l_wa_final-vbeln_d NE l_c_blank ).
        SET PARAMETER ID l_c_id1 FIELD l_wa_final-vbeln_d.
        CALL TRANSACTION l_c_tcode1 AND SKIP FIRST SCREEN.
        IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
          LEAVE SCREEN.
        ENDIF.
        "Calling Material doc. display Tcode
      ELSEIF ( fp_column EQ l_c_mat ) AND
        ( l_wa_final-mblnr IS NOT INITIAL
         AND l_wa_final-mblnr NE l_c_blank ).
        SET PARAMETER ID l_c_id2 FIELD l_wa_final-mblnr.
        CALL TRANSACTION l_c_tcode2 AND SKIP FIRST SCREEN.
        IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
          LEAVE SCREEN.
        ENDIF.
        "Calling Invoice no. display Tcode
      ELSEIF ( fp_column EQ l_c_inv ) AND
         ( l_wa_final-vbeln_i IS NOT INITIAL
          AND  l_wa_final-vbeln_i NE l_c_blank ).
        SET PARAMETER ID l_c_id3 FIELD l_wa_final-vbeln_i.
        CALL TRANSACTION l_c_tcode3 AND SKIP FIRST SCREEN.
        IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
          LEAVE SCREEN.
        ENDIF.
        "Calling Accounting Doc. display Tcode
      ELSEIF ( fp_column EQ l_c_acc ) AND
         ( l_wa_final-belnr IS NOT INITIAL
          AND l_wa_final-belnr NE l_c_blank ).
        SET PARAMETER ID: l_c_id4 FIELD l_wa_final-belnr,
                          l_c_id5 FIELD l_wa_final-bukrs,
                          l_c_id6 FIELD l_wa_final-gjahr.
        CALL TRANSACTION l_c_tcode4 AND SKIP FIRST SCREEN.
        IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
          LEAVE SCREEN.
        ENDIF.
        "BOC For MOD-014
*--Intercompany Invoice
        "Calling Invoice no. display Tcode
      ELSEIF ( fp_column EQ l_c_cominv ) AND
      ( l_wa_final-cominv IS NOT INITIAL
      AND l_wa_final-cominv NE l_c_blank ).
        SET PARAMETER ID l_c_id3 FIELD l_wa_final-cominv.
        CALL TRANSACTION l_c_tcode3 AND SKIP FIRST SCREEN.
        IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
          LEAVE SCREEN.
        ENDIF.
*--Intercompany FI
        "Calling Accounting Doc. display Tcode
      ELSEIF ( fp_column EQ l_c_comfi ) AND
      ( l_wa_final-comfi IS NOT INITIAL
      AND l_wa_final-comfi NE l_c_blank ).
        SET PARAMETER ID: l_c_id4 FIELD l_wa_final-comfi,
        l_c_id5 FIELD l_wa_final-comfi_bukrs,
        l_c_id6 FIELD l_wa_final-comfi_gjahr.
        CALL TRANSACTION l_c_tcode4 AND SKIP FIRST SCREEN.
        IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
          LEAVE SCREEN.
        ENDIF.
        "EOC For MOD-014
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  ""Execute when Shipment No./Visit List and Payment details is selected
  IF ( rb_ship EQ abap_true OR rb_visi EQ abap_true ) AND rb_patdt
  EQ abap_true.
    READ TABLE i_final2 INTO l_wa_final2 INDEX fp_row.
    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
      IF ( fp_column EQ l_c_acc1 ) AND
         ( l_wa_final2-oi_csh_post IS NOT INITIAL
          AND l_wa_final2-oi_csh_post NE l_c_blank ).
        SET PARAMETER ID: l_c_id4 FIELD l_wa_final2-oi_csh_post,
                          l_c_id5 FIELD l_wa_final2-compcod,
                          l_c_id6 FIELD l_wa_final2-fisc_year.
        CALL TRANSACTION l_c_tcode4 AND SKIP FIRST SCREEN.
        IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
          LEAVE SCREEN.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

* BOC MOD-017
  ""Execute when Tour Details is selected
  IF rb_driv EQ abap_true.
*   READ TABLE i_final7 INTO l_wa_final7 INDEX fp_row."commented MOD-025
***start of addition for MOD-025
    CLEAR l_wa_final7_status.
    READ TABLE i_final7_status INTO l_wa_final7_status INDEX fp_row.
***end of addition for MOD-025
    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
      IF ( fp_column EQ l_c_tour_id ) AND
***start of commenting for MOD-025
*          l_wa_final7-tour_id IS NOT INITIAL.
**     Display DSD Log
*        PERFORM f_display_dsd_log USING l_wa_final7-tour_id.
***end of commenting for MOD-025
***start of addition for MOD-025
        l_wa_final7_status-tour_id IS NOT INITIAL.
*     Display DSD Log
        PERFORM f_display_dsd_log USING l_wa_final7_status-tour_id.
***end of addition for MOD-025
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
***start of addition for MOD-022
    CLEAR l_r_date.
    READ TABLE i_final7_status INTO l_wa_final7_status INDEX fp_row.
    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL AND
       fp_column EQ c_idoc_number AND
       l_wa_final7_status-idoc_number IS NOT INITIAL.
      SUBMIT rseidoc2
               WITH credat IN l_r_date
               WITH docnum EQ l_wa_final7_status-idoc_number
               AND RETURN.
    ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-022
  ENDIF.
* EOC MOD-017
ENDFORM.                    " F_DISPLAY_TRANSACTION

                 <l_fs_vlh>    TYPE ty_vlh1,

                 <l_fs_rahd1>  TYPE ty_rahd1. "***added for MOD-025

        l_wa_driver LIKE LINE OF l_r_driver.
***end of addition for MOD-024

* BOC MOD-017
*Get FSR Status Selected
  PERFORM f_fsr_status.
*Get Exclustion user list
  PERFORM f_user_name.
* EOC MOD-017

  CLEAR l_i_status.
  " Status ID based on Shipment/Visit List
  PERFORM f_status  CHANGING l_i_status.

  IF l_i_status[] IS NOT INITIAL.

    SELECT tour_id
           obj_id
           driver
           codriver
           plant
           checkout
           checkin
           procstat
           manrel
           origin
           credate
           cretime
           creuser
           cngdate
           cngtime
           cnguser
           pres_procstat FROM  /dsd/hh_rahd
           INTO TABLE i_final7
           FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_status
           WHERE tour_id = l_i_status-tourid.
***start of addition for MOD-008
    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
      l_i_final7[] = i_final7[].
      SORT l_i_final7 BY tour_id.
      DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_i_final7 COMPARING tour_id.
      IF l_i_final7[] IS NOT INITIAL.
        SELECT tour_id checker FROM /dsd/hh_racocihd
                               INTO TABLE l_i_racocihd
                               FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_final7
                               WHERE tour_id EQ l_i_final7-tour_id.
        IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
          SORT l_i_racocihd BY tour_id.
        ENDIF.
*       BOC MOD-017
        SELECT vlid auth
               exdat1 "++MOD-030
               FROM /dsd/vc_vlh
                        INTO TABLE l_i_dsd_vc_vlh
                        FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_final7
                        WHERE vlid EQ l_i_final7-obj_id.
***start of commenting for MOD-022
*                          AND auth EQ l_c_ccejvend.
***end of commenting for MOD-022
        IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
          SORT l_i_dsd_vc_vlh BY vlid.
*          SELECT tour_id visit_id FROM /dsd/hh_racvhd
*                        INTO TABLE l_i_dsd_hh_racvhd
*                        FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_final7
*                        WHERE tour_id EQ l_i_final7-tour_id.
*          IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
*            SORT l_i_dsd_hh_racvhd BY tour_id visit_id.
*          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.

*       EOC MOD-017
      ENDIF.

***start of addition for MOD-012
      IF l_i_final7[] IS NOT INITIAL.
        LOOP AT l_i_final7 ASSIGNING <l_fs_rahd>.
*          update EXTNUMBER
          <l_fs_rahd>-extnumber = <l_fs_rahd>-tour_id.
        ENDLOOP.
        IF <l_fs_rahd> IS ASSIGNED.
          UNASSIGN <l_fs_rahd>.
        ENDIF.
        SELECT lognumber object subobject extnumber
               msg_cnt_e msg_cnt_w tim_stmp
                                FROM balhdr
                                INTO TABLE l_i_balhdr
                                FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_final7
                                WHERE object EQ c_rtacc
                                  AND subobject EQ c_fsr
                                  AND extnumber EQ l_i_final7-extnumber.
        IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
*     keep latest entry as 1st entry- TIM_STMP
          SORT l_i_balhdr BY object subobject extnumber ASCENDING
                             tim_stmp DESCENDING.
*     keep latest entry from BALHDR
          DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_i_balhdr
                          COMPARING object subobject extnumber.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-012
    ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-008
  ENDIF.

***start of addition for MOD-022
*  in case user enters Plant/Route/Settlement date & doesnt enters VL
  IF s_tknum[] IS INITIAL AND
     s_werks-low IS NOT INITIAL AND
     s_route1-low IS NOT INITIAL AND
     s_date[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    PERFORM f_create_auth.
*    create driver
    CONCATENATE c_r5 s_werks-low s_route1-low INTO l_v_driver.
    CONDENSE l_v_driver.

***start of addition for MOD-024
    IF l_v_driver IS NOT INITIAL.
      l_wa_driver-sign = c_i.
      l_wa_driver-low = l_v_driver.
      IF l_v_driver CS c_star. "*
        l_wa_driver-option = c_cp. "CP
      ELSE.
        l_wa_driver-option = c_eq. "EQ
      ENDIF.
      APPEND l_wa_driver TO l_r_driver.
      CLEAR l_wa_driver.
    ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-024

    SELECT vlid exdat driver1 auth FROM /dsd/vc_vlh
                INTO TABLE l_i_vc_vlh
                WHERE exdat IN s_date
***start of changes for MOD-024
*                  AND driver1 EQ l_v_driver
                  AND driver1 IN l_r_driver
***end of changes for MOD-024
                  AND auth IN r_auth.
    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
      SORT l_i_vc_vlh BY vlid.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-022

  IF i_final7[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    SORT l_i_status BY tourid.
    LOOP AT i_final7 ASSIGNING <l_fs_rahd>.

***start of addition for MOD-008
      READ TABLE l_i_racocihd ASSIGNING <l_fs_racocihd> WITH KEY
                                        tour_id = <l_fs_rahd>-tour_id
                                        BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
*       BOC MOD-017
        IF <l_fs_racocihd>-checker IS NOT INITIAL.
*  Count Lengh on string
          CLEAR:l_v_len.
          l_v_len = strlen( <l_fs_racocihd>-checker ).
*         read last Char
          l_v_len = l_v_len - 1.
          l_wa_final7-scenario = <l_fs_racocihd>-checker+l_v_len(1).
          l_wa_final7-drv_swap = <l_fs_racocihd>-checker+0(1).
        ELSE.
          l_wa_final7-drv_swap = l_c_n.
        ENDIF.
*       Check if scenarion other than R/V/H display blank
        IF l_wa_final7-scenario NE l_c_reg
          AND l_wa_final7-scenario NE l_c_ven
          AND l_wa_final7-scenario NE l_c_hyb.
          CLEAR:l_wa_final7-scenario.
        ENDIF.
*       Check if Driver Swap other than B/G/A display N
        IF l_wa_final7-drv_swap NE l_c_b
          AND l_wa_final7-drv_swap NE l_c_g
          AND l_wa_final7-drv_swap NE l_c_a.
          l_wa_final7-drv_swap = l_c_n.
        ENDIF.
*       EOC MOD-017
        UNASSIGN <l_fs_racocihd>.
      ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-008

      READ TABLE i_inb_stat ASSIGNING <l_fs_stat>
       WITH KEY vlid = <l_fs_rahd>-obj_id  BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
        l_wa_final7-werks = <l_fs_stat>-werks.
        l_wa_final7-route = <l_fs_stat>-route.
        l_wa_final7-date = <l_fs_stat>-date.
***start of addition for MOD-022
        l_wa_final7-idoc_number = <l_fs_stat>-idoc_number.
***end of addition for MOD-022
        UNASSIGN <l_fs_stat>.
      ENDIF.
      READ TABLE l_i_status ASSIGNING <l_fs_status>
          WITH KEY tourid =  <l_fs_rahd>-tour_id BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
        MOVE-CORRESPONDING <l_fs_rahd> TO l_wa_final7.  "#EC CI_MOVCPND
        l_wa_final7-status_id = <l_fs_status>-status_id.
        "Check the status
        CASE  <l_fs_status>-status_id.
          WHEN l_c_status.
*           BOC MOD-017
            l_wa_final7-fsr_status = l_c_green.
****start of changes for MOD-012
**            l_wa_final7-fsr_status = l_c_green. "commented for MOD-012
*            READ TABLE l_i_balhdr ASSIGNING <l_fs_balhdr> WITH KEY
*                                        object = c_rtacc
*                                        subobject = c_fsr
*                                        extnumber = <l_fs_rahd>-tour_id
*                                        BINARY SEARCH.
*            IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
*              IF <l_fs_balhdr>-msg_cnt_e IS INITIAL AND
*                 <l_fs_balhdr>-msg_cnt_w IS INITIAL.
** if both error & warning are initial- set status as Green
*                l_wa_final7-fsr_status = l_c_green.
*                l_wa_final7-log_status = c_success.
*              ELSEIF <l_fs_balhdr>-msg_cnt_e IS INITIAL AND
*                     <l_fs_balhdr>-msg_cnt_w IS NOT INITIAL.
** if error is initial and warning is not initial- set status as Yellow
*                l_wa_final7-fsr_status = l_c_yellow.
*                l_wa_final7-log_status = c_error.
*                l_wa_final7-msg_cnt_e = <l_fs_balhdr>-msg_cnt_e.
*                l_wa_final7-msg_cnt_w = <l_fs_balhdr>-msg_cnt_w.
*              ELSE.
** if error is not initial and warning is initial/not initial- set status as Red
*                l_wa_final7-fsr_status = l_c_red.
*                l_wa_final7-log_status = c_error.
*                l_wa_final7-msg_cnt_e = <l_fs_balhdr>-msg_cnt_e.
*                l_wa_final7-msg_cnt_w = <l_fs_balhdr>-msg_cnt_w.
*              ENDIF.
*              UNASSIGN <l_fs_balhdr>.
*            ENDIF.
****end of changes for MOD-012
*         EOC MOD-017
          WHEN l_c_status1.
            l_wa_final7-fsr_status = l_c_red.
*           BOC MOD-017
****start of changes for MOD-012
*            l_wa_final7-log_status = c_error.
*            READ TABLE l_i_balhdr ASSIGNING <l_fs_balhdr> WITH KEY
*                                        object = c_rtacc
*                                        subobject = c_fsr
*                                        extnumber = <l_fs_rahd>-tour_id
*                                        BINARY SEARCH.
*            IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
*              l_wa_final7-msg_cnt_e = <l_fs_balhdr>-msg_cnt_e.
*              l_wa_final7-msg_cnt_w = <l_fs_balhdr>-msg_cnt_w.
*              UNASSIGN <l_fs_balhdr>.
*            ENDIF.
          WHEN l_c_status2.
            l_wa_final7-fsr_status = l_c_yellow.
*           EOC MOD-017
          WHEN OTHERS.
            l_wa_final7-fsr_status = l_c_gray.
***end of changes for MOD-012
            l_wa_final7-log_status = c_not_processed. "added for MOD-012
        ENDCASE.
*    BOC MOD-017
*Set Time zone for creation date/time
        IF l_wa_final7-creuser NOT IN r_user
          AND l_wa_final7-credate IS NOT INITIAL
          AND l_wa_final7-cretime IS NOT INITIAL.
          PERFORM f_get_local_timezone
                     USING   l_wa_final7-cretime
                     CHANGING l_wa_final7-credate
                              l_wa_final7-cretime.
        ENDIF.
*Set Time zone for Change date/time
        IF l_wa_final7-cnguser NOT IN r_user
          AND l_wa_final7-cngdate IS NOT INITIAL
          AND l_wa_final7-cngtime IS NOT INITIAL.
          PERFORM f_get_local_timezone
                     USING   l_wa_final7-cngtime
                     CHANGING l_wa_final7-cngdate
                              l_wa_final7-cngtime.
        ENDIF.
*       Check Planned Route
        READ TABLE l_i_dsd_vc_vlh
***start of changes for MOD-022
*        TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS
         ASSIGNING <l_fs_vc_vlh>
***end of changes for MOD-022
         WITH KEY vlid = l_wa_final7-obj_id BINARY SEARCH.
        IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
**       check multiple entries of the visit
*          CLEAR:l_v_lines.
*          LOOP AT l_i_dsd_hh_racvhd ASSIGNING <l_fs_dsd_hh_racvhd>
*               WHERE tour_id = <l_fs_rahd>-tour_id.
*
*            l_v_lines = l_v_lines + 1.
*            IF l_v_lines GT 1.
*              l_wa_final7-planned = l_c_planned.
*              CLEAR l_v_lines.
*              EXIT.
*            ENDIF.
*          ENDLOOP.
*--Start of addition for MOD-030
          IF <l_fs_vc_vlh>-auth EQ c_auth."CCEJPAPER
            l_wa_final7-scenario = l_c_reg.
            l_wa_final7-drv_swap = l_c_n.
          ENDIF.
          l_wa_final7-exdat1 = <l_fs_vc_vlh>-exdat1.
*--End of addition for MOD-030
***start of changes for MOD-022
          l_wa_final7-planned = l_c_planned.
          CASE <l_fs_vc_vlh>-auth.
            WHEN c_ccejvend. "CCEJVEND
              l_wa_final7-planned = c_process. "'P'
            WHEN c_ccejplan. "CCEPLAN
              l_wa_final7-planned = c_error. "'E'
            WHEN c_ccejmanl. "CCEJMANL
              l_wa_final7-planned = c_unplanned. "'U'
            WHEN OTHERS.
              CLEAR l_wa_final7-planned.
          ENDCASE.
          UNASSIGN <l_fs_vc_vlh>.
***end of changes for MOD-022
        ELSE.
          CLEAR:l_wa_final7-planned.
        ENDIF.
*       Check Driver Swap indicator
        IF l_wa_final7-drv_swap = l_c_r.
          l_wa_final7-drv_swap = l_c_n.
        ENDIF.
*     EOC MOD-017

*     BOC MOD-018
*     Processing Status
        CASE l_wa_final7-fsr_status.
          WHEN c_green.
            l_wa_final7-proc_status = c_process.
          WHEN c_red.
            l_wa_final7-proc_status = c_error.
          WHEN c_yellow.
            l_wa_final7-proc_status = c_mismatch.
***start of addition for MOD-025
          WHEN c_gray.
            l_wa_final7-proc_status = c_notexecuted.
***end of addition for MOD-025
          WHEN OTHERS.
        ENDCASE.
*     EOC MOD-018

***start of addition for MOD-022
        READ TABLE l_i_vc_vlh ASSIGNING <l_fs_vlh> WITH KEY
                                      vlid = l_wa_final7-obj_id
                                      BINARY SEARCH.
        IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
* setting VL as X so that it is excluded as its details are added
          <l_fs_vlh>-auth = abap_true.
          UNASSIGN <l_fs_vlh>.
        ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-022

***start of addition for MOD-025
        l_wa_final7-tour_id = <l_fs_rahd>-tour_id.
***end of addition for MOD-025
***start of addition for MOD-027
        READ TABLE l_i_dsd_vc_vlh ASSIGNING <l_fs_vc_vlh>
                        WITH KEY vlid = l_wa_final7-obj_id
                        BINARY SEARCH.
        IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
          l_wa_final7-auth = <l_fs_vc_vlh>-auth.
          UNASSIGN <l_fs_vc_vlh>.
        ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-027

        APPEND l_wa_final7 TO i_final7_status.
        CLEAR l_wa_final7.
        UNASSIGN <l_fs_status>.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
    IF  <l_fs_rahd> IS ASSIGNED.
      UNASSIGN  <l_fs_rahd>.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF. "***added for MOD-024
***start of addition for MOD-022
*    exclude entries from L_I_VC_VLH which are added in FINAL ITAB
  IF l_i_vc_vlh[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    DELETE l_i_vc_vlh WHERE auth EQ abap_true.
    IF l_i_vc_vlh[] IS NOT INITIAL.
***start of addition for MOD-025
      SELECT tour_id obj_typ obj_id
             FROM /dsd/hh_rahd
             INTO TABLE i_rahd1
             FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_vc_vlh
             WHERE obj_typ EQ c_visitlist
               AND obj_id EQ l_i_vc_vlh-vlid.
      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
        SORT i_rahd1 BY obj_id.
      ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-025

      LOOP AT l_i_vc_vlh ASSIGNING <l_fs_vlh>.
        l_wa_final7-obj_id = <l_fs_vlh>-vlid.
        l_wa_final7-werks = <l_fs_vlh>-driver1+2(4).
        l_wa_final7-route  = <l_fs_vlh>-driver1+6(4).
        l_wa_final7-date = <l_fs_vlh>-exdat.
        l_wa_final7-driver = <l_fs_vlh>-driver1.

        CASE <l_fs_vlh>-auth.
          WHEN c_ccejvend. "CCEJVEND
            l_wa_final7-planned = c_process. "'P'
          WHEN c_ccejplan. "CCEPLAN
            l_wa_final7-planned = c_error. "'E'
          WHEN c_ccejmanl. "CCEJMANL
            l_wa_final7-planned = c_unplanned. "'U'
          WHEN OTHERS.
            CLEAR l_wa_final7-planned.
        ENDCASE.
***start of addition for MOD-024
        READ TABLE i_inb_stat TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS
         WITH KEY vlid = l_wa_final7-obj_id BINARY SEARCH.
        IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
*          IDOC number would be blank for such records
          l_wa_final7-proc_status = c_notexecuted.
          l_wa_final7-fsr_status = l_c_gray.
        ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-024

***start of addition for MOD-025
        READ TABLE i_rahd1 ASSIGNING <l_fs_rahd1> WITH KEY
                                          obj_id = l_wa_final7-obj_id
                                          BINARY SEARCH.
        IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
          l_wa_final7-tour_id = <l_fs_rahd1>-tour_id.
          UNASSIGN <l_fs_rahd1>.
        ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-025
***start of addition for MOD-027
        l_wa_final7-auth = <l_fs_vlh>-auth.
***end of addition for MOD-027
        APPEND l_wa_final7 TO i_final7_status.
        CLEAR l_wa_final7.
      ENDLOOP.
      IF <l_fs_vlh> IS ASSIGNED.
        UNASSIGN <l_fs_vlh>.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-022
*  ENDIF. "***commented for MOD-024
  "BOC For MOD-014
*Filter by scenerio
  IF s_scene IS NOT INITIAL.
    DELETE i_final7_status WHERE scenario  NOT IN s_scene.
  ENDIF.
*Filter by status log
  IF s_log IS NOT INITIAL.
    DELETE i_final7_status WHERE log_status NOT IN s_log.
  ENDIF.
  "EOC For MOD-014
*BOC For MOD-017
*Filter FSR Status
***start of commenting for MOD-025
*  IF s_light-low IS NOT INITIAL AND v_fsr_status IS NOT INITIAL.
*    DELETE i_final7_status WHERE fsr_status NE v_fsr_status.
***end of commenting for MOD-025
***start of addition for MOD-025
  IF s_light[] IS NOT INITIAL AND r_fsr_status[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    DELETE i_final7_status WHERE fsr_status NOT IN r_fsr_status.
***end of addition for MOD-025
  ENDIF.
*Filter by Planned Route
  IF s_plan IS NOT INITIAL.
    DELETE i_final7_status WHERE planned NOT IN s_plan.
  ENDIF.
*Filter by Driver Swap
  IF s_dswap IS NOT INITIAL.
    DELETE i_final7_status WHERE drv_swap NOT IN s_dswap.
  ENDIF.
*EOC For MOD-017
ENDFORM.                    " F_GET_DRIVER_DETAILS

***start of addition for MOD-012
  TRY.
      l_o_column = fp_columns->get_column( l_c_log_status ).
      l_o_column->set_long_text( TEXT-c53 ).
      l_o_column->set_medium_text( space ).
      l_o_column->set_short_text( space ).
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.                            "#EC NO_HANDLER
  ENDTRY.
***end of addition for MOD-012
* BOC MOD-017
* Planned Route
  TRY.
      l_o_column = fp_columns->get_column( l_c_planned ).
      l_o_column->set_long_text( TEXT-c67 ).
      l_o_column->set_medium_text( space ).
      l_o_column->set_short_text( space ).
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.                            "#EC NO_HANDLER
  ENDTRY.
* Driver Swap
  TRY.
      l_o_column = fp_columns->get_column( l_c_drv_swap ).
      l_o_column->set_long_text( TEXT-c68 ).
      l_o_column->set_medium_text( space ).
      l_o_column->set_short_text( space ).
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.                            "#EC NO_HANDLER
  ENDTRY.

* Processing Status
  TRY.
      l_o_column = fp_columns->get_column( l_c_proc_status ).
      l_o_column->set_long_text( TEXT-c69 ).
      l_o_column->set_medium_text( space ).
      l_o_column->set_short_text( space ).
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.                            "#EC NO_HANDLER
  ENDTRY.
* EOC MOD-017

***start of addition for MOD-027
* Visit Group
  TRY.
      l_o_column = fp_columns->get_column( l_c_auth ).
      l_o_column->set_long_text( TEXT-048 ).
      l_o_column->set_medium_text( space ).
      l_o_column->set_short_text( space ).
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.                            "#EC NO_HANDLER
  ENDTRY.
***end of addition for MOD-027

***start of addition for MOD-022

      l_o_col_tab ?= fp_columns->get_column( c_idoc_number ).
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.                            "#EC NO_HANDLER
  ENDTRY.

  TRY.
      CALL METHOD l_o_col_tab->set_cell_type
        EXPORTING
          value = if_salv_c_cell_type=>hotspot.

    CATCH cx_salv_data_error .                          "#EC NO_HANDLER
  ENDTRY.
***end of addition for MOD-022
*--Start of addition for MOD-030
  "Original execution date
  TRY.
      l_o_column = fp_columns->get_column( c_exdat1 ).
      l_o_column->set_visible( abap_false ).
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.                            "#EC NO_HANDLER
  ENDTRY.
*--End of addition for MOD-030
ENDFORM.                    " F_SET_COLUMNS3

FORM f_enable_ship .
***start of commenting for MOD-025

  wa_final-vbeln_i = fp_vbfa_vbeln.

  wa_final-awkey = wa_final-vbeln_i. "added for MOD-001

  "BOC For MOD-014

  "EOC For MOD-014
ENDFORM.                    " F_INVOICE_VALUE

FORM f_disable_ship .

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_GET_VL_2
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Get visit listdata based on plant/route/settlement date
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_get_vl_2  CHANGING fp_stat TYPE tb_t_stat.

***start of addition for MOD-005
  DATA:l_r_visit TYPE RANGE OF /dsd/vc_vlid.
***end of addition for MOD-005

*   get all Visit List based on Plant, route, date
  SELECT werks
         route
         creation_date
         visitlist
***start of addition for MOD-022
         idoc_number
         zzernam
         zzerzzt
***end of addition for MOD-022
          FROM /ccej/t_inb_stat
                            INTO TABLE fp_stat
                            WHERE werks IN s_werks AND
                                  route IN s_route1 AND
                                  creation_date IN s_date
***start of addition for MOD-005
                                AND visitlist IN l_r_visit.
***end of addition for MOD-005
  IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
    CLEAR fp_stat.
  ELSE.
    DELETE fp_stat WHERE vlid IS INITIAL.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " F_GET_VL_2
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_ORDER_TYPE
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      Populate the particular order type
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_order_type  CHANGING fp_option TYPE tb_t_option.
********************Data declaration************************
  DATA:l_v_option TYPE selopt.
********************Constant declaration*********************

  CONSTANTS:
    "Inclusive sign
    l_c_sign   TYPE c VALUE 'I',
    "Equal sign
    l_c_opt    TYPE char2 VALUE 'EQ',
    "Order type ZTCD
    l_c_otype1 TYPE auart VALUE 'ZTCD',
    "Order type ZTDD
    l_c_otype2 TYPE auart VALUE 'ZTDD'.

  "Order type ZTCD
  l_v_option-low = l_c_otype1.
  l_v_option-sign = l_c_sign .
  l_v_option-option = l_c_opt.
  APPEND l_v_option TO fp_option.

  CLEAR l_v_option.
  "Order type ZTDD
  l_v_option-low = l_c_otype2.
  l_v_option-sign = l_c_sign .
  l_v_option-option = l_c_opt.
  APPEND l_v_option TO fp_option.

ENDFORM.                    " F_ORDER_TYPE

*       CLASS lcl_handle_events IMPLEMENTATION
*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
* Implement the events for handling the events of cl_salv_table
*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
CLASS lcl_handle_events IMPLEMENTATION.
  METHOD on_link_click.
    PERFORM f_display_transaction USING row column .
  ENDMETHOD.                    "on_link_click
***start of addition for MOD-001
  METHOD on_click.
**"Clear global table
    PERFORM f_clear.
***start of addition for MOD-027
    CASE abap_true.
      WHEN rb_driv.
        PERFORM f_get_driver_details. ""Tour details data
        PERFORM f_display_alv USING i_final7_status.
      WHEN rb_fsdoc.
***end of addition for MOD-027
        PERFORM f_get_visit_list_data. ""Based on Visit list
        PERFORM f_display_alv USING i_final.
***start of addition for MOD-027
      WHEN OTHERS.
*        do nothing
    ENDCASE.
***end of addition for MOD-027
  ENDMETHOD.                    "on_click
  METHOD on_user_command.
    CASE sy-ucomm.
      WHEN c_back. "'BACK'
        LEAVE TO SCREEN 0.
    ENDCASE.
  ENDMETHOD.                    "on_user_command
***end of addition for MOD-001
***start of addition for MOD-027
  METHOD set_after_events.
    TYPES: BEGIN OF l_ty_seln,
             column TYPE string,
             r_seln TYPE RANGE OF string,
           END OF l_ty_seln.
    DATA: l_o_filters    TYPE REF TO cl_salv_filters,
          l_i_filters    TYPE salv_t_filter_ref,
          l_wa_filter    TYPE salv_s_filter_ref,
          l_i_selns      TYPE salv_t_selopt_ref,
          l_o_seln       TYPE REF TO cl_salv_selopt,
          l_i_sel_range  TYPE HASHED TABLE OF l_ty_seln WITH UNIQUE KEY column,
          l_wa_sel_range TYPE l_ty_seln,
          l_wa_sel       LIKE LINE OF l_wa_sel_range-r_seln,
          l_i_data       TYPE REF TO data,
          l_o_display    TYPE REF TO cl_salv_display_settings. "#EC NEEDED
    FIELD-SYMBOLS: <l_fs_column> TYPE any,
                   <l_fs_i_data> TYPE INDEX TABLE,
                   <l_fs_data>   TYPE any.

    IF e_salv_function EQ c_filt "'&ILT' Apply Filter
       OR e_salv_function EQ c_del_filt. "'&ILD' Delete Filter
      l_o_display = o_table->get_display_settings( ).

      CASE abap_true.
* Build ITAB and work area
        WHEN rb_driv.
          CREATE DATA l_i_data LIKE i_final7_status.
          ASSIGN l_i_data->* TO <l_fs_i_data>.
          CREATE DATA l_i_data LIKE LINE OF i_final7_status.
          ASSIGN l_i_data->* TO <l_fs_data>.
          <l_fs_i_data> = i_final7_status.
        WHEN rb_visit.
          CREATE DATA l_i_data LIKE i_final6.
          ASSIGN l_i_data->* TO <l_fs_i_data>.
          CREATE DATA l_i_data LIKE LINE OF i_final6.
          ASSIGN l_i_data->* TO <l_fs_data>.
          <l_fs_i_data> = i_final6.
        WHEN rb_slrep.
          CREATE DATA l_i_data LIKE i_final1.
          ASSIGN l_i_data->* TO <l_fs_i_data>.
          CREATE DATA l_i_data LIKE LINE OF i_final1.
          ASSIGN l_i_data->* TO <l_fs_data>.
" *****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **begin of change by EJX9007359 03.06.2026 for ATC
* <l_fs_i_data> = i_final1.
          <l_fs_i_data> = i_final1.          "#EC CI_FLDEXT_OK[2215424]
" *****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **end of change by EJX9007359 03.06.2026 for ATC
        WHEN rb_patdt.
          CREATE DATA l_i_data LIKE i_final2.
          ASSIGN l_i_data->* TO <l_fs_i_data>.
          CREATE DATA l_i_data LIKE LINE OF i_final2.
          ASSIGN l_i_data->* TO <l_fs_data>.
" *****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **begin of change by EJX9007359 03.06.2026 for ATC
* <l_fs_i_data> = i_final2.
          <l_fs_i_data> = i_final2.          "#EC CI_FLDEXT_OK[2610650]
" *****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **end of change by EJX9007359 03.06.2026 for ATC
        WHEN rb_check.
          CREATE DATA l_i_data LIKE i_final3.
          ASSIGN l_i_data->* TO <l_fs_i_data>.
          CREATE DATA l_i_data LIKE LINE OF i_final3.
          ASSIGN l_i_data->* TO <l_fs_data>.
" *****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **begin of change by EJX9007359 03.06.2026 for ATC
* <l_fs_i_data> = i_final3.
          <l_fs_i_data> = i_final3.          "#EC CI_FLDEXT_OK[2215424]
" *****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **end of change by EJX9007359 03.06.2026 for ATC
        WHEN rb_money.
          CREATE DATA l_i_data LIKE i_final4.
          ASSIGN l_i_data->* TO <l_fs_i_data>.
          CREATE DATA l_i_data LIKE LINE OF i_final4.
          ASSIGN l_i_data->* TO <l_fs_data>.
          <l_fs_i_data> = i_final4.
        WHEN rb_quan.
          CREATE DATA l_i_data LIKE i_final5.
          ASSIGN l_i_data->* TO <l_fs_i_data>.
          CREATE DATA l_i_data LIKE LINE OF i_final5.
          ASSIGN l_i_data->* TO <l_fs_data>.
" *****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **begin of change by EJX9007359 03.06.2026 for ATC
* <l_fs_i_data> = i_final5.
          <l_fs_i_data> = i_final5.          "#EC CI_FLDEXT_OK[2215424]
" *****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **end of change by EJX9007359 03.06.2026 for ATC
        WHEN rb_fsdoc.
          CREATE DATA l_i_data LIKE i_final.
          ASSIGN l_i_data->* TO <l_fs_i_data>.
          CREATE DATA l_i_data LIKE LINE OF i_final.
          ASSIGN l_i_data->* TO <l_fs_data>.
          <l_fs_i_data> = i_final.
        WHEN rb_cash.
          IF <fs_i_table> IS ASSIGNED.
            CREATE DATA l_i_data LIKE <fs_i_table>.
            ASSIGN l_i_data->* TO <l_fs_i_data>.
            CREATE DATA l_i_data LIKE LINE OF <fs_i_table>.
            ASSIGN l_i_data->* TO <l_fs_data>.
            <l_fs_i_data> = <fs_i_table>.
          ENDIF.
        WHEN OTHERS.
*          do nothing
      ENDCASE.

      l_o_filters = o_table->get_filters( ).
      l_i_filters = l_o_filters->get( ).
* Go through the filters on each columns, build select ranges
      LOOP AT l_i_filters INTO l_wa_filter.
        l_wa_sel_range-column = l_wa_filter-columnname.
        CLEAR l_wa_sel_range-r_seln.
        l_i_selns = l_wa_filter-r_filter->get( ).
        LOOP AT l_i_selns INTO l_o_seln.
          l_wa_sel-sign   = l_o_seln->get_sign( ).
          l_wa_sel-option = l_o_seln->get_option( ).
          l_wa_sel-low    = l_o_seln->get_low( ).
          l_wa_sel-high   = l_o_seln->get_high( ).
          INSERT l_wa_sel INTO TABLE l_wa_sel_range-r_seln.
        ENDLOOP.

        INSERT l_wa_sel_range INTO TABLE l_i_sel_range.
      ENDLOOP.

      IF <l_fs_i_data> IS ASSIGNED.
* Go through the data eliminating records that don't match the filters
        LOOP AT <l_fs_i_data> INTO <l_fs_data>.
          LOOP AT l_i_sel_range INTO l_wa_sel_range.
            ASSIGN COMPONENT l_wa_sel_range-column OF STRUCTURE <l_fs_data> TO <l_fs_column>.
            IF <l_fs_column> NOT IN l_wa_sel_range-r_seln.
              DELETE <l_fs_i_data>.
              EXIT.
            ENDIF.
          ENDLOOP.
        ENDLOOP.
        IF <l_fs_data> IS ASSIGNED.
          UNASSIGN <l_fs_data>.
        ENDIF.

        CLEAR: v_rows, v_string.
        v_rows = lines( <l_fs_i_data> ). "recount the rows
        v_string = v_rows.
        CONDENSE v_string.
        CONCATENATE TEXT-c77 TEXT-047 v_string INTO
                        v_string SEPARATED BY space.
        l_o_display->set_list_header( v_string ).
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDMETHOD.                    "set_after_events
***end of addition for MOD-027
ENDCLASS.                    "lcl_handle_events IMPLEMENTATION
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_CURRENCY_CONVERSION
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Currency conversion
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_currency_conversion  CHANGING fp_price TYPE any
                                     fp_value TYPE bapicurr-bapicurr.

  CONSTANTS: l_c_curr TYPE waers VALUE 'JPY'."currency unit
  CLEAR fp_value.
*--------Conversion of Currency Amounts into External Data Format
  CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_CURRENCY_CONV_TO_EXTERNAL'
    EXPORTING
      currency        = l_c_curr
" *****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **begin of change by EJX9007359 03.06.2026 for ATC
* amount_internal = fp_price
      amount_internal = fp_price "#EC CI_FLDEXT_OK[2610650]
" *****Code Remediation changes S4 2025_1_A Conversion **end of change by EJX9007359 03.06.2026 for ATC
    IMPORTING
      amount_external = fp_value.

  fp_price = fp_value.
ENDFORM.                    " F_CURRENCY_CONVERSION

*&      Form  F_EVENT_LINK_CLK_FSR
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Events for ALV
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_event_link_clk_fsr USING fp_table TYPE REF TO cl_salv_table.
*********************Reference variable Declaration*******************
  DATA: l_o_events TYPE REF TO cl_salv_events_table,
        l_o_handle TYPE REF TO lcl_handle_events.
* get the event reference
  l_o_events = fp_table->get_event( ).
  CREATE OBJECT l_o_handle.
* register to the event LINK_CLICK
  SET HANDLER l_o_handle->on_link_click FOR l_o_events.
* register to the event ON_CLICK
  SET HANDLER l_o_handle->on_click FOR l_o_events.

  SET HANDLER l_o_handle->on_user_command FOR l_o_events.

ENDFORM.                    " F_EVENT_LINK_CLK_FSR

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_FILTER
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*if Plant, Route, Settlement date and HHT document number are entered on
*sel screen then only consider records which exist in /CCEJ/T_INB_STAT
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_filter  USING    fp_v_werks TYPE werks_d "plant
                        fp_v_route TYPE route   "route
                        fp_v_date TYPE erdat    "date
               CHANGING fp_v_flag TYPE abap_bool. "flag

  IF fp_v_werks IN s_werks AND
     fp_v_route IN s_route AND
     fp_v_date IN s_date.
    fp_v_flag = abap_false. "record should not be filtered out
  ELSE.
    fp_v_flag = abap_true. "record should be filtered out
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                    " F_FILTER

***start of addition for MOD-003
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_GET_PRESALES
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Fetching Pre.Sales Documents
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_get_presales CHANGING fp_sd TYPE tb_t_sd
                            fp_rahd TYPE tb_t_rahd.
*********************Internal Table Declaration***********************
  DATA:l_i_radelhd TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_/dsd/hh_radelhd_s,
       l_i_vbfa    TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF vbfa,
       l_i_invoice TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_invoice_s.

*********************Work Area Declaration****************************
  DATA:l_wa_vbco6   TYPE vbco6,
       l_wa_vbfa    TYPE vbfa,
       l_wa_invoice TYPE ty_sd,
***start of addition for MOD-026
       l_i_lips     TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_lips.
***end of addition for MOD-026
*********************Constants Declaration****************************
  CONSTANTS:l_c_doc_typ TYPE /dsd/hh_doc_typ VALUE '20',
            l_c_order   TYPE vbtypl_n VALUE 'C'. "Order type

*********************Field-symbols Declaration************************
  FIELD-SYMBOLS:<l_fs_radelhd> TYPE ty_/dsd/hh_radelhd_s,
                <l_fs_rahd>    TYPE ty_rahd,
***start of addition for MOD-026
                <l_fs_lips>    TYPE ty_lips.
***end of addition for MOD-026


  IF fp_rahd[] IS NOT INITIAL.

    SELECT tour_id visit_id be_delvry obj_typ plant bstkd bstdk
           FROM /dsd/hh_radelhd  INTO TABLE  l_i_radelhd
           FOR ALL ENTRIES IN   fp_rahd
           WHERE tour_id = fp_rahd-tour_id.

    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR l_i_radelhd.
    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.

  IF l_i_radelhd[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    "Delete the entry where no delivery found
    DELETE l_i_radelhd WHERE be_delvry IS INITIAL.
  ENDIF.

***start of addition for MOD-026
  IF l_i_radelhd[] IS NOT INITIAL.
*    fetch Sales Order for Delivery
    SELECT vbeln posnr vgbel vgpos FROM lips
                       INTO TABLE l_i_lips
                       FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_radelhd
                       WHERE vbeln EQ l_i_radelhd-be_delvry.
    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
      SORT l_i_lips BY vbeln posnr.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-026

  LOOP AT l_i_radelhd ASSIGNING <l_fs_radelhd>.

    READ TABLE fp_rahd  ASSIGNING <l_fs_rahd>
       WITH KEY tour_id = <l_fs_radelhd>-tour_id BINARY SEARCH.

    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.

      IF <l_fs_radelhd>-be_delvry IS NOT INITIAL.
***start of commenting for MOD-026
*        l_wa_vbco6-vbeln = <l_fs_radelhd>-be_delvry.
*        "Document flow based on delivery
*        PERFORM f_order_flow  USING  l_wa_vbco6
*                          CHANGING l_i_vbfa.
*
*        READ TABLE l_i_vbfa INTO l_wa_vbfa
*            WITH KEY vbtyp_n = l_c_order.
***end of commenting for MOD-026
***start of addition for MOD-026
        READ TABLE l_i_lips ASSIGNING <l_fs_lips> WITH KEY
                                      vbeln = <l_fs_radelhd>-be_delvry
                                      BINARY SEARCH.
***end of addition for MOD-026
        IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
          l_wa_invoice-obj_id = <l_fs_rahd>-obj_id.
          l_wa_invoice-tour_id = <l_fs_rahd>-tour_id.
          l_wa_invoice-visit_id =  <l_fs_radelhd>-visit_id.
          l_wa_invoice-vehicle = <l_fs_rahd>-vehicle.
          l_wa_invoice-driver = <l_fs_rahd>-driver.
*          l_wa_invoice-vbeln  =  l_wa_vbfa-vbeln."commented for MOD-026
          l_wa_invoice-vbeln  =  <l_fs_lips>-vgbel. "added for MOD-026
          l_wa_invoice-vbeln_d = <l_fs_radelhd>-be_delvry.
          "Set the flag
*--Start of commenting for MOD-023
*          l_wa_invoice-flag = abap_true.
*--End of commenting for MOD-023
          APPEND l_wa_invoice TO fp_sd.
          CLEAR l_wa_invoice.
          UNASSIGN <l_fs_lips>. "added for MOD-026
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      UNASSIGN <l_fs_rahd>.
    ENDIF.
    CLEAR:l_wa_vbco6,l_i_vbfa.
  ENDLOOP.

  IF <l_fs_radelhd> IS ASSIGNED.
    UNASSIGN <l_fs_radelhd>.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " F_GET_PRESALES
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form F_BUILD_TAB_D
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Document flow based on delivery
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_build_tab_d  USING   fp_seqno TYPE i
                          fp_vbak TYPE ty_vbak
                          fp_sd TYPE ty_sd.

*********************Internal Table Declaration***********************
  DATA:l_i_vbfa     TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF vbfa,
       l_i_vbfa_inv TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF vbfa. "***added for MOD-010
*********************Work Area Declaration****************************
  DATA:l_wa_vbco6 TYPE vbco6,
       l_wa_vbfa  TYPE vbfa.
*********************Constants Declaration***************************
  CONSTANTS:
    l_c_mat    TYPE vbtypl_n  VALUE 'R', "material document type
    l_c_inv    TYPE vbtypl_n  VALUE 'M', "Invoice type
    "BOC for MOD-014
    l_c_cominv TYPE vbtypl_n VALUE '5', "Intercom. Invice type
    "EOC for MOD-014
    l_c_debit  TYPE vbtypl_n  VALUE 'P', "Debit Memo
    l_c_credit TYPE vbtypl_n  VALUE 'O', "Credit Memo
    l_c_dot    TYPE c VALUE '.', "Dot
    "BOC for MOD-014
    l_c_equ    TYPE /scl/mdmd_equp_own VALUE '05'. "Equipment owner
  "EOC for MOD-014
**********************************************************************
  wa_final-vbeln = fp_vbak-vbeln.
  wa_final-erdat_o = fp_vbak-erdat.
  "BOC For MOD-015
*--Time zone
  IF fp_vbak-ernam NOT IN r_user.
    PERFORM f_get_local_time
               USING   fp_vbak-erzet
               CHANGING  fp_vbak-erdat.
  ENDIF.
  "EOC For MOD-015
  CONCATENATE wa_final-erdat_o+6(2) wa_final-erdat_o+4(2)
     wa_final-erdat_o(4) INTO wa_final-erdat_o
         SEPARATED BY l_c_dot.
  wa_final-auart = fp_vbak-auart.
  wa_final-seqno = fp_seqno.

  IF fp_vbak-vbeln IS NOT INITIAL.
    l_wa_vbco6-vbeln = fp_sd-vbeln_d.
    PERFORM f_order_flow  USING  l_wa_vbco6
                          CHANGING l_i_vbfa.
***start of addition for MOD-010
    IF l_i_vbfa[] IS NOT INITIAL.
*      Filter relevant Invoices- which have FKSTO and SFAKN as blank
      PERFORM f_filter_inv USING l_i_vbfa
                           CHANGING l_i_vbfa_inv.
    ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-010
  ENDIF.
  wa_final-vbeln_d = fp_sd-vbeln_d." Fetch delivery no.

  CLEAR l_wa_vbfa.

  IF wa_final-vbeln_d IS NOT INITIAL.
    "BOC For MOD-014
*-When equipment owner is 5
    IF wa_final-/scl/equp_ownr EQ l_c_equ.
*-For intercompany invoice
      READ TABLE l_i_vbfa_inv INTO l_wa_vbfa WITH KEY
      vbelv = wa_final-vbeln
      vbtyp_n = l_c_cominv " Fetch invoice no.
      BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
        PERFORM f_com_invoice_value USING l_wa_vbfa-vbeln.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    "EOC For MOD-014
** If delivery found
***start of changes for MOD-010
*    READ TABLE l_i_vbfa INTO l_wa_vbfa WITH KEY vbelv =
*    wa_final-vbeln_d
*    vbtyp_n = l_c_inv. " Fetch invoice no."#EC CI_READTBL
    READ TABLE l_i_vbfa_inv INTO l_wa_vbfa WITH KEY
                                  vbelv = wa_final-vbeln
                                  vbtyp_n = l_c_inv " Fetch invoice no.
                                  BINARY SEARCH.
***end of changes for MOD-010
    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
      PERFORM f_invoice_value USING l_wa_vbfa-vbeln.
    ELSE.
      READ TABLE l_i_vbfa INTO l_wa_vbfa WITH KEY vbelv =
      wa_final-vbeln
      vbtyp_n = l_c_debit. " Fetch Debit Memo "#EC CI_READTBL
      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
        PERFORM f_invoice_value USING l_wa_vbfa-vbeln.
      ELSE.
        READ TABLE l_i_vbfa INTO l_wa_vbfa WITH KEY vbelv =
        wa_final-vbeln
        vbtyp_n = l_c_credit. " Fetch Credit Memo "#EC CI_READTBL
        IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
          PERFORM f_invoice_value USING l_wa_vbfa-vbeln.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    CLEAR l_wa_vbfa.

    READ TABLE l_i_vbfa INTO l_wa_vbfa WITH KEY vbelv =
    wa_final-vbeln
    vbtyp_n = l_c_mat."Fetch material document "#EC CI_READTBL
    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
      wa_final-mblnr = l_wa_vbfa-vbeln.
    ENDIF.
    CLEAR l_wa_vbfa.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " F_BUILD_TAB_D
***end of addition for MOD-003

***start of addition for MOD-004

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_SET_TOTAL
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Display Sub Total
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_L_O_TABLE  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_set_total  USING    fp_l_o_table TYPE REF TO cl_salv_table.

  CONSTANTS:l_c_sales_amt  TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'SALES_AMT', "Sales Amount
            l_c_driver     TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'DRIVER', "Driver
            l_c_cust1      TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'CUST1', "Customer
            l_c_amount     TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'AMOUNT', "Amount
            l_c_quan_plan  TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'QUAN_PLAN', "Target Qty
            l_c_quan_count TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'QUAN_COUNT', "Actual Qty
            l_c_quan_diff  TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'QUAN_DIFF', "Diff Qty
            l_c_check_id   TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'CHECK_ID', "Check ID
            l_c_matnr      TYPE lvc_fname VALUE 'MATNR'. "Material

  "Sales/Replenishment
  IF rb_slrep EQ abap_true.
    PERFORM f_set_total_column USING l_c_sales_amt fp_l_o_table.
    PERFORM f_set_sort_column USING l_c_driver fp_l_o_table.
  ENDIF.

  "Payment Details
  IF rb_patdt EQ abap_true.
    PERFORM f_set_total_column USING l_c_amount fp_l_o_table.
    PERFORM f_set_sort_column USING l_c_cust1 fp_l_o_table.
  ENDIF.

  "Check Out/In
  IF rb_check EQ abap_true.
    PERFORM f_set_total_column USING l_c_quan_plan fp_l_o_table.
    PERFORM f_set_total_column USING l_c_quan_count fp_l_o_table.
    PERFORM f_set_total_column USING l_c_quan_diff fp_l_o_table.
    PERFORM f_set_total_column USING l_c_amount fp_l_o_table.

    PERFORM f_set_sort_column USING l_c_check_id fp_l_o_table.
    PERFORM f_set_sort_column USING l_c_matnr fp_l_o_table.
  ENDIF.

***start of addition for MOD-013
  IF rb_cash EQ abap_true.
    PERFORM f_cash_column USING fp_l_o_table.
  ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-013
ENDFORM.                    " F_SET_TOTAL_SLREP
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_SET_TOTAL_COLUMN
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Set Field name on which total will be calculated
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_FNAME  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_set_total_column  USING    fp_fname TYPE lvc_fname
                                  fp_l_o_table TYPE REF TO cl_salv_table.

  DATA: l_o_aggrs TYPE REF TO cl_salv_aggregations.
  l_o_aggrs = fp_l_o_table->get_aggregations( ). "get aggregations
*   Add TOTAL for COLUMN
  TRY.
      CALL METHOD l_o_aggrs->add_aggregation "add aggregation
        EXPORTING
          columnname  = fp_fname "aggregation column name
          aggregation = if_salv_c_aggregation=>total. "aggregation type

    CATCH cx_salv_data_error .                          "#EC NO_HANDLER
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found .                           "#EC NO_HANDLER
    CATCH cx_salv_existing .                            "#EC NO_HANDLER
  ENDTRY.

ENDFORM.                    " F_SET_TOTAL_COLUMN
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_SET_SORT_COLUMN
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Set Sorting Column for subtotal
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_set_sort_column USING fp_fname TYPE lvc_fname
                             fp_l_o_table TYPE REF TO cl_salv_table.
*   Bring the total line to top
  DATA : l_r_sort TYPE REF TO cl_salv_sorts. "ALV sorts
  CALL METHOD fp_l_o_table->get_sorts "get sorts
    RECEIVING
      value = l_r_sort.

  DATA : l_r_sort_column TYPE REF TO cl_salv_sort. "column sort
  CALL METHOD l_r_sort->add_sort "add column sort
    EXPORTING
      columnname = fp_fname "sort column always keyfield
*     position   =
*     SEQUENCE   = IF_SALV_C_SORT=>SORT_UP
*     SUBTOTAL   = IF_SALV_C_BOOL_SAP=>FALSE
*     GROUP      = IF_SALV_C_SORT=>GROUP_NONE
*     OBLIGATORY = IF_SALV_C_BOOL_SAP=>FALSE
    RECEIVING
      value      = l_r_sort_column.

  CALL METHOD l_r_sort_column->set_subtotal "add subtotal
    EXPORTING
      value = if_salv_c_bool_sap=>true.

ENDFORM.                    " F_SET_SORT_COLUMN
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_FILTER_INV
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Filter relevant Invoices
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_filter_inv  USING    fp_i_vbfa TYPE tb_vbfa
                   CHANGING fp_i_vbfa_inv TYPE tb_vbfa.
  DATA:l_i_vbfa_inv TYPE tb_vbfa,
       l_i_vbrk     TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_vbeln.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS <l_fs_vbfa_inv> TYPE vbfa.
  CONSTANTS: l_c_inv     TYPE vbtypl_n VALUE 'M', "Invoice type
             "BOC For MOD-014
             "Intercompany Invoice type
             l_c_com_inv TYPE vbtypl_n VALUE '5',
             "EOC For MOD-014
*--Start of addition for MOD-023
             l_c_debit   TYPE vbtypl_n  VALUE 'P', "Debit Memo
             l_c_credit  TYPE vbtypl_n  VALUE 'O'. "Credit Memo
*--End of addition for MOD-023
  CLEAR fp_i_vbfa_inv.
  "BOC For MOD-014
  IF r_vbtyp IS INITIAL.
    PERFORM f_vbtyp_n USING l_c_inv "Invoice type
            l_c_com_inv "Inercompany Invoice type
*--Start of addition for MOD-023
            l_c_credit "Credit Memo
            l_c_debit. "Debit Memo
*--End of addition for MOD-023
  ENDIF.
  "EOC For MOD-014
*  keep only Invoice records- VBTYP_N = M
  fp_i_vbfa_inv[] = fp_i_vbfa[].
*  DELETE fp_i_vbfa_inv WHERE vbtyp_n NE l_c_inv."Comment For MOD-014
  DELETE fp_i_vbfa_inv WHERE vbtyp_n NOT IN r_vbtyp."Add For MOD-014
  IF fp_i_vbfa_inv[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    l_i_vbfa_inv[] = fp_i_vbfa_inv[].
    SORT l_i_vbfa_inv BY vbeln.
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM l_i_vbfa_inv COMPARING vbeln.
    IF l_i_vbfa_inv[] IS NOT INITIAL.
*      fetch only those Invoices which have FKSTO and SFAKN as blank
      SELECT vbeln FROM vbrk
                   INTO TABLE l_i_vbrk
                   FOR ALL ENTRIES IN l_i_vbfa_inv
                   WHERE vbeln EQ l_i_vbfa_inv-vbeln
                     AND fksto EQ abap_false
                     AND sfakn EQ space.
      IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
        SORT l_i_vbrk BY vbeln.

*        filter required invoices
        LOOP AT fp_i_vbfa_inv ASSIGNING <l_fs_vbfa_inv>.
          READ TABLE l_i_vbrk TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS WITH KEY
                                   vbeln = <l_fs_vbfa_inv>-vbeln
                                   BINARY SEARCH.
          IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
*            mark this Invoice as X as this needs to be filtered out
            <l_fs_vbfa_inv>-vbeln = abap_true.
          ENDIF.
        ENDLOOP.
        IF <l_fs_vbfa_inv> IS ASSIGNED.
          UNASSIGN <l_fs_vbfa_inv>.
        ENDIF.
        DELETE fp_i_vbfa_inv WHERE vbeln EQ abap_true.
        SORT fp_i_vbfa_inv BY vbelv posnv vbtyp_n.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                    " F_FILTER_INV
"BOC For MOD-014
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form F_COM_INVOICE_VALUE
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
* For Intercompany Invoice
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_com_invoice_value USING fp_vbfa_vbeln TYPE vbeln_nach.
*********************Constants Declaration***************************
  CONSTANTS:l_c_zero TYPE c VALUE '0'. "for deleting Leading Zero's
  wa_final-cominv = fp_vbfa_vbeln.
*  "Deleting leading zero
*  SHIFT wa_final-cominv LEFT DELETING LEADING l_c_zero.
  "Invoice No. Range
  PERFORM f_invoice_range USING wa_final-cominv.
ENDFORM. " F_COM_INVOICE_VALUE

* Invoice range creation
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_invoice_range USING fp_inv TYPE vbeln_vf.
*********************Data Declaration***************************
  DATA:l_wa_inv TYPE ty_inv.
*  "Invoice no. range
  IF fp_inv IS NOT INITIAL.
    l_wa_inv-awkey = fp_inv.
    APPEND l_wa_inv TO i_inv.
    CLEAR l_wa_inv.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM. " F_INVOICE_RANGE
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form F_VBTYP_N
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
* text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_vbtyp_n USING fp_inv TYPE vbtypl_n
fp_com_inv TYPE vbtypl_n
*--Start of addition for MOD-023
fp_credit TYPE vbtypl_n
fp_debit TYPE vbtypl_n.
*--End of addition for MOD-023
*********************Data Declaration***************************
  DATA:l_wa_vbtyp LIKE LINE OF r_vbtyp.
  "Subsequent document category range
  l_wa_vbtyp-sign = c_i.
  l_wa_vbtyp-option = c_eq.
  "Invoice
  l_wa_vbtyp-low = fp_inv.
  APPEND l_wa_vbtyp TO r_vbtyp.
  CLEAR:l_wa_vbtyp-low.
  "Intercompany Invoice
  l_wa_vbtyp-low = fp_com_inv.
  APPEND l_wa_vbtyp TO r_vbtyp.
*--Start of commenting for MOD-023
*  CLEAR l_wa_vbtyp.
*--End of commenting for MOD-023
*--Start of addition for MOD-023
  "Credit Memo
  CLEAR:l_wa_vbtyp-low.
  l_wa_vbtyp-low = fp_credit.
  APPEND l_wa_vbtyp TO r_vbtyp.

  CLEAR:l_wa_vbtyp-low.
  "Debit Memo
  l_wa_vbtyp-low = fp_debit.
  APPEND l_wa_vbtyp TO r_vbtyp.
  CLEAR l_wa_vbtyp.
*--End of addition for MOD-023
ENDFORM. " F_VBTYP_N
"EOC For MOD-014

***start of addition for MOD-013
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_ENABLE_CASH
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       enable fields for cash difference report
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_enable_cash .
***start of commenting for MOD-025
*  CLEAR: s_tplst[],s_route[],s_vehic[],s_driv[],s_status[],s_erdat[],
*         s_hht[].
***end of commenting for MOD-025

  LOOP AT SCREEN.
*    disbale all fields except fields reqd for Cash difference report
    IF screen-group1 EQ c_grp1 OR
       screen-group1 EQ c_grp2 OR
       screen-group1 EQ c_grp3 OR
       screen-group1 EQ c_grp4 OR
       "BOC For MOD-014
       screen-group1 EQ c_grp6 OR
       screen-group1 EQ c_grp7
      "EOC For MOD-014
      "BOC For MOD-016
      OR screen-group1 EQ c_grp8.
      "EOC For MOD-016
      screen-invisible = 1.
      screen-input = 0.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
ENDFORM.                    " F_ENABLE_CASH

FORM f_validate_data .

        l_v_werks TYPE werks_d,

        l_v_katr3 TYPE katr3,

***start of addition for MOD-027
        l_v_blart TYPE blart,
        l_v_bschl TYPE bschl.
***end of addition for MOD-027

  IF rb_cash EQ abap_true.
*  Plant
    IF s_werks[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT SINGLE werks FROM t001w                        "#EC WARNOK
                         INTO l_v_werks
                         WHERE werks IN s_werks.
      IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
*      Enter Valid Plant(s)
        MESSAGE i024 WITH TEXT-005 DISPLAY LIKE c_error.
        LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

*Begin of comment For MOD-014
*  Customer
*    IF s_kunnr[] IS NOT INITIAL.
*      SELECT SINGLE kunnr FROM kna1                         "#EC WARNOK
*                          INTO l_v_kunnr
*                          WHERE kunnr IN s_kunnr.
*      IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
**      Enter valid Customer(s)
*        MESSAGE i024 WITH text-006 DISPLAY LIKE c_error.
*        LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
*      ENDIF.
*    ENDIF.
*
**  Equipment Owner

*      SELECT SINGLE /scl/equp_ownr                          "#EC WARNOK
*                            FROM kna1                   "#EC CI_NOFIELD
*                            INTO l_v_equp
*                            WHERE /scl/equp_ownr IN s_equp.
*      IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
**      Enter valid Equipment Owner
*        MESSAGE i024 WITH text-009 DISPLAY LIKE c_error.
*        LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
*      ENDIF.
*    ENDIF.

**  Business type extension
