*      SELECT SINGLE katr4 FROM tvk4                         "#EC WARNOK
*                          INTO l_v_katr4
*                          WHERE katr4 IN s_katr4.
*      IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
**      Enter valid Business Type Extension
*        MESSAGE i024 WITH text-007 DISPLAY LIKE c_error.
*        LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
*      ENDIF.
*    ENDIF.
*End of comment For MOD-014

*  Business type
    IF s_katr3[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT SINGLE katr3 FROM tvk3                         "#EC WARNOK
                          INTO l_v_katr3
                          WHERE katr3 IN s_katr3.
      IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
*      Enter valid Business Type
        MESSAGE i024 WITH TEXT-008 DISPLAY LIKE c_error.
        LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  "BOC For MOD-014
  IF  ( rb_cash EQ abap_true
    OR rb_visit EQ abap_true
    OR rb_slrep EQ abap_true
    OR rb_patdt EQ abap_true
    OR rb_fsdoc EQ abap_true ).

*  Customer
    IF s_kunnr[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT SINGLE kunnr FROM kna1                         "#EC WARNOK
                          INTO l_v_kunnr
                          WHERE kunnr IN s_kunnr.
      IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
*      Enter valid Customer(s)
        MESSAGE i024 WITH TEXT-006 DISPLAY LIKE c_error.
        LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

*  Equipment Owner
    IF s_equp[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT SINGLE /scl/equp_ownr                          "#EC WARNOK
                            FROM kna1                   "#EC CI_NOFIELD
                            INTO l_v_equp
                            WHERE /scl/equp_ownr IN s_equp.
      IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
*      Enter valid Equipment Owner
        MESSAGE i024 WITH TEXT-009 DISPLAY LIKE c_error.
        LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

*  Business type extension
    IF s_katr4[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      SELECT SINGLE katr4 FROM tvk4                         "#EC WARNOK
                          INTO l_v_katr4
                          WHERE katr4 IN s_katr4.
      IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
*      Enter valid Business Type Extension
        MESSAGE i024 WITH TEXT-007 DISPLAY LIKE c_error.
        LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
  "EOC For MOD-014

***start of addition for MOD-013
*  Visit Type
  IF s_vistyp-low IS NOT INITIAL.
    PERFORM f_get_domnam USING c_visit_type. "Visit Type
    IF s_vistyp-low NOT IN r_visit_type.
*      Enter valid Visit Type
      MESSAGE i024 WITH TEXT-042 DISPLAY LIKE c_error.
      LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

*  Summary Status
  IF s_sum_st-low IS NOT INITIAL.
    PERFORM f_get_domnam USING c_status. "Summary Status
    IF s_sum_st-low NOT IN r_status.
*      Enter valid Summary Status
      MESSAGE i024 WITH TEXT-043 DISPLAY LIKE c_error.
      LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-013
***start of addition for MOD-027
*  Document type
  IF s_blart1[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE blart FROM t003
                        INTO l_v_blart
                        WHERE blart IN s_blart1.
    IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
*      Enter valid Document type
      MESSAGE i024 WITH TEXT-050 DISPLAY LIKE c_error.
      LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

*  Posting Key
  IF s_bschl[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE bschl FROM tbsl
                        INTO l_v_bschl
                        WHERE bschl IN s_bschl.
    IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
*      Enter valid Posting Key
      MESSAGE i024 WITH TEXT-051 DISPLAY LIKE c_error.
      LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-027
ENDFORM.                    " F_VALIDATE_DATA
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_CASH_DIFF
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       cash difference report
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_cash_diff .
  DATA: l_v_value    TYPE  tvarv_val, "Selection value
        l_v_value1   TYPE  tvarv_val, "Selection value
        l_i_rsparams TYPE TABLE OF rsparams, "Parameters
        l_i_pay_data TYPE REF TO data. "Internal table
  FIELD-SYMBOLS:
*                 <l_i_pay_data2> TYPE ANY TABLE, "commented for MOD-027
    <l_fs_tknum> LIKE LINE OF s_tknum,
    <l_fs_date>  LIKE LINE OF s_date.

***start of addition for MOD-027
*  unassign ITAB if assigned
  IF <fs_i_table> IS ASSIGNED.
    UNASSIGN <fs_i_table>.
  ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-027

*Visit List
  IF s_tknum[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    LOOP AT s_tknum ASSIGNING <l_fs_tknum>.
      CLEAR: l_v_value, l_v_value1.
      l_v_value = <l_fs_tknum>-low.
      l_v_value1 = <l_fs_tknum>-high.
      PERFORM f_fill_param USING c_s_vlid "'S_VLID'
                                 c_type_select "'S'
                                 <l_fs_tknum>-sign
                                 <l_fs_tknum>-option
                                 l_v_value
                                 l_v_value1
                           CHANGING l_i_rsparams.
    ENDLOOP.
    IF <l_fs_tknum> IS ASSIGNED.
      UNASSIGN <l_fs_tknum>.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

*Plant
  IF s_werks-low IS NOT INITIAL.
    CLEAR: l_v_value, l_v_value1.
    l_v_value = s_werks-low.
    PERFORM f_fill_param USING   c_s_werks "'S_WERKS'
                                 c_type_select
                                 s_werks-sign
                                 s_werks-option
                                 l_v_value
                                 l_v_value1
                          CHANGING l_i_rsparams.
  ENDIF.

*Route
  IF s_route1-low IS NOT INITIAL.
    CLEAR: l_v_value, l_v_value1.
    l_v_value = s_route1-low.
    PERFORM f_fill_param USING   c_s_route "S_ROUTE"
                                 c_type_select
                                 s_route1-sign
                                 s_route1-option
                                 l_v_value
                                 l_v_value1
                          CHANGING l_i_rsparams.
  ENDIF.

*Settlement Date
  IF s_date[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    LOOP AT s_date ASSIGNING <l_fs_date>.
      CLEAR: l_v_value, l_v_value1.
      l_v_value = <l_fs_date>-low.
      l_v_value1 = <l_fs_date>-high.
      PERFORM f_fill_param USING   c_s_date "'S_DATE'
                                   c_type_select
                                   <l_fs_date>-sign
                                   <l_fs_date>-option
                                   l_v_value
                                   l_v_value1
                            CHANGING l_i_rsparams.
    ENDLOOP.
    IF <l_fs_date> IS ASSIGNED.
      UNASSIGN <l_fs_date>.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

*Customer
  IF s_kunnr-low IS NOT INITIAL.
    CLEAR: l_v_value, l_v_value1.
    l_v_value = s_kunnr-low.
    PERFORM f_fill_param USING   c_s_kunnr "'S_KUNNR'
                                 c_type_select
                                 s_kunnr-sign
                                 s_kunnr-option
                                 l_v_value
                                 l_v_value1
                          CHANGING l_i_rsparams.
  ENDIF.

*Equipment Owner
  IF s_equp-low IS NOT INITIAL.
    CLEAR: l_v_value, l_v_value1.
    l_v_value = s_equp-low.
    PERFORM f_fill_param USING   c_s_equp "'S_EQUP'
                                 c_type_select
                                 s_equp-sign
                                 s_equp-option
                                 l_v_value
                                 l_v_value1
                          CHANGING l_i_rsparams.
  ENDIF.

*Business type extension
  IF s_katr4-low IS NOT INITIAL.
    CLEAR: l_v_value, l_v_value1.
    l_v_value = s_katr4-low.
    PERFORM f_fill_param USING   c_s_katr4 "'S_KATR4'
                                 c_type_select
                                 s_katr4-sign
                                 s_katr4-option
                                 l_v_value
                                 l_v_value1
                          CHANGING l_i_rsparams.
  ENDIF.

*Business type
  IF s_katr3-low IS NOT INITIAL.
    CLEAR: l_v_value, l_v_value1.
    l_v_value = s_katr3-low.
    PERFORM f_fill_param USING   c_s_katr3 "'S_KATR3'
                                 c_type_select
                                 s_katr3-sign
                                 s_katr3-option
                                 l_v_value
                                 l_v_value1
                          CHANGING l_i_rsparams.
  ENDIF.

*Visit type
  IF s_vistyp-low IS NOT INITIAL.
    CLEAR: l_v_value, l_v_value1.
    l_v_value = s_vistyp-low.
    PERFORM f_fill_param USING   c_s_vistyp "'S_VISTYP'
                                 c_type_select
                                 s_vistyp-sign
                                 s_vistyp-option
                                 l_v_value
                                 l_v_value1
                          CHANGING l_i_rsparams.
  ENDIF.

*Summary Status
  IF s_sum_st-low IS NOT INITIAL.
    CLEAR: l_v_value, l_v_value1.
    l_v_value = s_sum_st-low.
    PERFORM f_fill_param USING   c_s_sum_st "'S_STATUS'
                                 c_type_select
                                 s_sum_st-sign
                                 s_sum_st-option
                                 l_v_value
                                 l_v_value1
                          CHANGING l_i_rsparams.
  ENDIF.

  cl_salv_bs_runtime_info=>set( EXPORTING display  = abap_false
                                          metadata = abap_false
                                          data     = abap_true ).

* execute program /CCEJ/RDSDFSVR_CASH_DIFF to trigger the output
  SUBMIT /ccej/rdsdfsvr_cash_diff WITH SELECTION-TABLE l_i_rsparams
                                       EXPORTING LIST TO MEMORY
                                       AND RETURN.

  TRY.
      cl_salv_bs_runtime_info=>get_data_ref( IMPORTING
                                    r_data = l_i_pay_data ).
*      ASSIGN l_i_pay_data->* TO <l_i_pay_data2>. "commented for MOD-027
      ASSIGN l_i_pay_data->* TO <fs_i_table>. "added for MOD-027
    CATCH cx_salv_bs_sc_runtime_info.
      cl_salv_bs_runtime_info=>clear_all( ).
      MESSAGE i071 DISPLAY LIKE c_error.
      LEAVE LIST-PROCESSING.
  ENDTRY.

  cl_salv_bs_runtime_info=>clear_all( ).

***start of commenting for MOD-027
*  IF <l_i_pay_data2> IS ASSIGNED.
*    PERFORM f_display_alv USING <l_i_pay_data2>.
*    UNASSIGN <l_i_pay_data2>.
*  ENDIF.
***end of commenting for MOD-027
***start of addition for MOD-027
  IF <fs_i_table> IS ASSIGNED.
    PERFORM f_display_alv USING <fs_i_table>.
    UNASSIGN <fs_i_table>.
  ENDIF.
***end of addition for MOD-027
ENDFORM.                    " F_CASH_DIFF
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_FILL_PARAM
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Fill the parameters
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_fill_param  USING    fp_v_objky     TYPE rsscr_name "Name
                            fp_v_select    TYPE char1 "Select
                            fp_v_include   TYPE char1 "Include
                            fp_v_equal     TYPE char2 "Equal
                            fp_v_low       TYPE tvarv_val "Value
                            fp_v_high      TYPE tvarv_val "Value
                   CHANGING fp_i_params TYPE  rsparams_tt. "Parameters
  DATA l_wa_rsparams TYPE rsparams.

  l_wa_rsparams-selname = fp_v_objky.
  l_wa_rsparams-kind    = fp_v_select.
  l_wa_rsparams-sign    = fp_v_include.
  l_wa_rsparams-option  = fp_v_equal.
  l_wa_rsparams-low     = fp_v_low.
  l_wa_rsparams-high    = fp_v_high.
  APPEND l_wa_rsparams TO fp_i_params .
  CLEAR l_wa_rsparams.
ENDFORM.                    " F_FILL_PARAM

      l_v_text = TEXT-032.
      l_o_column = fp_columns->get_column( l_c_diff_amt ).
      l_o_column->set_long_text( l_v_text ).
      l_o_column->set_medium_text( space ).
      l_o_column->set_short_text( space ).
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.                            "#EC NO_HANDLER
  ENDTRY.

*  Payment difference status
  TRY.
      CLEAR l_v_text.
      l_v_text = TEXT-c76.
      l_o_column = fp_columns->get_column( l_c_light ).
      l_o_column->set_long_text( l_v_text ).
      l_o_column->set_medium_text( space ).
      l_o_column->set_short_text( space ).
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.                            "#EC NO_HANDLER
  ENDTRY.

*  Driver credit memo
  TRY.
      CLEAR l_v_text.
      l_v_text = TEXT-033.
      l_o_column = fp_columns->get_column( l_c_driver_credit ).
      l_o_column->set_long_text( l_v_text ).
      l_o_column->set_medium_text( space ).
      l_o_column->set_short_text( space ).
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.                            "#EC NO_HANDLER
  ENDTRY.

*  Driver debit memo
  TRY.
      CLEAR l_v_text.
      l_v_text = TEXT-034.
      l_o_column = fp_columns->get_column( l_c_driver_debit ).
      l_o_column->set_long_text( l_v_text ).
      l_o_column->set_medium_text( space ).
      l_o_column->set_short_text( space ).
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.                            "#EC NO_HANDLER
  ENDTRY.

*  Driver Swap Receive
  TRY.
      CLEAR l_v_text.
      l_v_text = TEXT-039.
      l_o_column = fp_columns->get_column( l_c_driver_receive ).
      l_o_column->set_long_text( l_v_text ).
      l_o_column->set_medium_text( space ).
      l_o_column->set_short_text( space ).
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.                            "#EC NO_HANDLER
  ENDTRY.

*  Driver Swap Give
  TRY.
      CLEAR l_v_text.
      l_v_text = TEXT-040.
      l_o_column = fp_columns->get_column( l_c_driver_give ).
      l_o_column->set_long_text( l_v_text ).
      l_o_column->set_medium_text( space ).
      l_o_column->set_short_text( space ).
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.                            "#EC NO_HANDLER
  ENDTRY.

*  Main Driver
  TRY.
      l_o_column = fp_columns->get_column( l_c_main_driver ).
      l_o_column->set_visible( abap_false ).
    CATCH cx_salv_not_found.                            "#EC NO_HANDLER
  ENDTRY.
ENDFORM.                    " F_SET_COLUMNS4

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_GET_DOMNAM
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       check domain value are valid
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_get_domnam  USING    fp_domname TYPE domname. "Domain Name
  DATA: l_i_tab         TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF dd07v, "Table for Domain & values
        l_wa_visit_type LIKE LINE OF r_visit_type, "WA Visit type
        l_wa_status     LIKE LINE OF r_status. "WA Summary Status
  FIELD-SYMBOLS <l_fs_tab> TYPE dd07v. "FS for Domain & values

  CLEAR: r_visit_type, r_status.

*    get domain values
  CALL FUNCTION 'DD_DOMVALUES_GET'
    EXPORTING
      domname        = fp_domname
    TABLES
      dd07v_tab      = l_i_tab
    EXCEPTIONS
      wrong_textflag = 1
      OTHERS         = 2.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
    CLEAR l_i_tab.
  ELSE.
    CASE fp_domname.                                     "#EC CI_OTHERS
      WHEN c_visit_type. "Visit Type
        l_wa_visit_type-sign   = c_i. "'I'
        l_wa_visit_type-option = c_eq. "'EQ'
        LOOP AT l_i_tab ASSIGNING <l_fs_tab>.
          l_wa_visit_type-low = <l_fs_tab>-domvalue_l.
          APPEND l_wa_visit_type TO r_visit_type.
          CLEAR l_wa_visit_type-low.
        ENDLOOP.
      WHEN c_status. "Summary Status
        l_wa_status-sign   = c_i. "'I'
        l_wa_status-option = c_eq. "'EQ'
        LOOP AT l_i_tab ASSIGNING <l_fs_tab>.
          l_wa_status-low = <l_fs_tab>-domvalue_l.
          APPEND l_wa_status TO r_status.
          CLEAR l_wa_status-low.
        ENDLOOP.
      WHEN OTHERS.
*      do nothing
    ENDCASE.
    IF <l_fs_tab> IS ASSIGNED.
      UNASSIGN <l_fs_tab>.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                    " F_GET_DOMNAM
***end of addition for MOD-013

*&      Form  F_FSR_STATUS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Set the value based on selection screen input
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_fsr_status .
***start of commenting for MOD-025
*  IF s_light-low IS NOT INITIAL.
*    "Green light
*    IF s_light-low EQ c_process.
*      v_fsr_status  = c_green.
*      "Red light
*    ELSEIF s_light-low EQ c_error.
*      v_fsr_status  = c_red.
*      "Yellow light
*    ELSEIF s_light-low EQ c_mismatch.
*      v_fsr_status  = c_yellow.
*    ELSEIF s_light-low EQ c_notexecuted.
*      v_fsr_status = c_gray.
*    ENDIF.
***end of commenting for MOD-025
***start of addition for MOD-025
  LOOP AT s_light.
    wa_fsr_status-sign = s_light-sign.
    wa_fsr_status-option = s_light-option.
    CASE s_light-low.
      WHEN c_process.
        wa_fsr_status-low = c_green.
      WHEN c_error.
        wa_fsr_status-low = c_red.
      WHEN c_mismatch.
        wa_fsr_status-low = c_yellow.
      WHEN c_notexecuted.
        wa_fsr_status-low = c_gray.
      WHEN OTHERS.
*          do nothing
    ENDCASE.
    IF wa_fsr_status IS NOT INITIAL.
      APPEND wa_fsr_status TO r_fsr_status.
    ENDIF.
    CLEAR wa_fsr_status.
  ENDLOOP.
***end of addition for MOD-025
*  ENDIF. "***commented for MOD-025
ENDFORM.                    " F_FSR_STATUS

FORM f_get_local_time USING fp_time TYPE erzet
                      CHANGING  fp_date TYPE erdat.

**********************Constant declaration*****************
  CONSTANTS:l_c_tzone TYPE  ttzz-tzone VALUE 'JAPAN'.

  IF fp_date IS NOT INITIAL.
    CALL FUNCTION 'ISU_DATE_TIME_CONVERT_TIMEZONE'
      EXPORTING
        x_date_utc    = fp_date
        x_time_utc    = fp_time
        x_timezone    = l_c_tzone
      IMPORTING
        y_date_lcl    = fp_date
      EXCEPTIONS
        general_fault = 1
        OTHERS        = 2.

  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                    " F_GET_LOCAL_TIME

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_INTERCOM_VALUE
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Intercom value
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_intercom_value  CHANGING fp_value TYPE ty_final.

*******************Constants Declaration***********************
  CONSTANTS:l_c_equ5  TYPE /scl/mdmd_equp_own VALUE '05',
            l_c_auart TYPE auart VALUE 'ZCUP'.

***start of addition for MOD-029
  DATA l_v_flag TYPE abap_bool.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <l_fs_knb1> TYPE ty_knb1,
                 <l_fs_vbak> TYPE ty_vbak.

*  if KNB1-BUKRS = 7827 & VBAK-VKORG = 0801, then skip below
  READ TABLE i_knb1 ASSIGNING <l_fs_knb1> WITH KEY
                                      kunnr = fp_value-/bev1/rpfar1
                                      BINARY SEARCH.
  IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
    READ TABLE i_vbak ASSIGNING <l_fs_vbak> WITH KEY
                                      vbeln = fp_value-vbeln
                                      BINARY SEARCH.
    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
      IF <l_fs_knb1>-bukrs EQ c_jp_bukrs1 AND
         <l_fs_vbak>-vkorg NE c_vkorg1.
        l_v_flag = abap_true.
      ENDIF.
      UNASSIGN <l_fs_vbak>.
    ENDIF.
    UNASSIGN <l_fs_knb1>.
  ENDIF.
  IF l_v_flag EQ abap_false.
***end of addition for MOD-029
    IF  fp_value-/scl/equp_ownr NE l_c_equ5
      OR ( fp_value-/scl/equp_ownr EQ l_c_equ5
         AND fp_value-auart EQ l_c_auart ).
      fp_value-cominv = c_blank.
      fp_value-cominvdate = c_blank.
      fp_value-cominvtyp = c_blank.
      fp_value-comfi = c_blank.
      fp_value-comfidate = c_blank.
      fp_value-comfityp = c_blank.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF. "***added for MOD-029
ENDFORM.                    " F_INTERCOM_VALUE

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_ENABLE_DISABLE_FIELDS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Enable/Disable Screen Fields
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_enable_disable_fields .

  CONSTANTS: l_c_light     TYPE screen-name VALUE 'S_LIGHT-LOW',
             l_c_light_txt TYPE screen-name VALUE '%_S_LIGHT_%_APP_%-TEXT',
             l_c_katr4     TYPE screen-name VALUE 'S_KATR4-LOW',
             l_c_katr4txt  TYPE screen-name VALUE '%_S_KATR4_%_APP_%-TEXT',
             l_c_log       TYPE screen-name VALUE 'S_LOG-LOW',
             l_c_logtxt    TYPE screen-name VALUE '%_S_LOG_%_APP_%-TEXT',
             l_c_matnr     TYPE screen-name VALUE 'S_MATNR-LOW',
             l_c_matnrtxt  TYPE screen-name VALUE '%_S_MATNR_%_APP_%-TEXT',
             l_c_chkid     TYPE screen-name VALUE 'S_CHKID-LOW',
             l_c_chkidtxt  TYPE screen-name VALUE '%_S_CHKID_%_APP_%-TEXT',
             l_c_mat       TYPE screen-name VALUE 'S_MATNR',
             l_c_pay       TYPE screen-name VALUE 'S_PAY',
             l_c_mdiff     TYPE screen-name VALUE 'S_MDIFF',
             l_c_hht       TYPE screen-name VALUE 'S_HHT',
             l_c_docno     TYPE screen-name VALUE 'S_DOCNO',
             l_c_objtyp    TYPE screen-name VALUE 'S_OBJTYP',
*             BOC for MOD-020
             l_c_status    TYPE screen-name VALUE 'S_STATUS',
             l_c_plan      TYPE screen-name VALUE 'S_PLAN',
             l_c_plog      TYPE screen-name VALUE 'S_PLOG',
             l_c_vlog      TYPE screen-name VALUE 'S_VLOG',
             l_c_fsrlog    TYPE screen-name VALUE 'S_FSRLOG'.
*             EOC for MOD-020


*New Logic to Enable/Disable Screen field radio button/field wise
  LOOP AT SCREEN.
*   Field Name
    CASE screen-name.
*     Processing Status Field
      WHEN l_c_light OR l_c_light_txt.
        IF rb_visit = abap_true OR
           rb_driv = abap_true.
*         Enable Field
          screen-invisible = 0.
          screen-input = 1.
        ELSE.
*         Disable Field
          screen-invisible = 1.
          screen-input = 0.
        ENDIF.
*     Business Type Extenstion
      WHEN l_c_katr4 OR l_c_katr4txt.
        IF rb_fsdoc = abap_true.
*         Enable Field
          screen-invisible = 0.
          screen-input = 1.
        ENDIF.
*     Log Status
      WHEN l_c_log OR l_c_logtxt.
*         Disable Field
        screen-invisible = 1.
        screen-input = 0.
*     Matnr/checkid
      WHEN l_c_matnr OR l_c_matnrtxt OR
           l_c_chkid OR l_c_chkidtxt.
        IF rb_check = abap_true
          OR rb_quan = abap_true.
*         Enable Field
          screen-invisible = 0.
          screen-input = 1.
        ELSE.
*         Disable Field
          screen-invisible = 1.
          screen-input = 0.
        ENDIF.
      WHEN OTHERS.
*       Material No
        IF screen-name CS l_c_mat.
          IF rb_check = abap_true
            OR rb_quan = abap_true.
*         Enable Field
            screen-invisible = 0.
            screen-input = 1.
          ELSE.
*         Disable Field
            screen-invisible = 1.
            screen-input = 0.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.

*       Money Difference
        IF screen-name CS l_c_mdiff.
          IF rb_money = abap_true.
*         Enable Field
            screen-invisible = 0.
            screen-input = 1.
          ELSE.
*         Disable Field
            screen-invisible = 1.
            screen-input = 0.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.

*       Payment method
        IF screen-name CS l_c_pay.
          IF rb_money = abap_true.
*         Enable Field
            screen-invisible = 0.
            screen-input = 1.
          ELSE.
*         Disable Field
            screen-invisible = 1.
            screen-input = 0.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.

*HHT No
        IF screen-name CS l_c_hht.
          IF rb_slrep = abap_true.
*         Enable Field
            screen-invisible = 0.
            screen-input = 1.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.

*       Objecy Type
        IF screen-name CS l_c_objtyp.
          IF rb_slrep = abap_true.
*         Enable Field
            screen-invisible = 0.
            screen-input = 1.
          ELSE.
*         Disable Field
            screen-invisible = 1.
            screen-input = 0.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.

*       Document No
        IF screen-name CS l_c_docno.
          IF rb_patdt = abap_true.
*         Enable Field
            screen-invisible = 0.
            screen-input = 1.
          ELSE.
*         Disable Field
            screen-invisible = 1.
            screen-input = 0.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.

*      FSR Status
        IF screen-name CS l_c_status.
          IF rb_driv = abap_true.
*         Disable Field
            screen-invisible = 1.
            screen-input = 0.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.

*      Planned
        IF screen-name CS l_c_plan.
          IF rb_driv = abap_true.
*         Disable Field
            screen-invisible = 1.
            screen-input = 0.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.

*       Payment Log
        IF screen-name CS l_c_plog.
          IF rb_patdt = abap_true.
*         Enable Field
            screen-invisible = 0.
            screen-input = 1.
          ELSE.
*         Disable Field
            screen-invisible = 1.
            screen-input = 0.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.

*       Visit Log
        IF screen-name CS l_c_vlog.
          IF rb_visit = abap_true.
*         Enable Field
            screen-invisible = 0.
            screen-input = 1.
          ELSE.
*         Disable Field
            screen-invisible = 1.
            screen-input = 0.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.

*       FSR Log
        IF screen-name CS l_c_fsrlog.
          IF rb_fsdoc = abap_true.
*         Enable Field
            screen-invisible = 0.
            screen-input = 1.
          ELSE.
*         Disable Field
            screen-invisible = 1.
            screen-input = 0.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.


    ENDCASE.
    MODIFY SCREEN.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " F_ENABLE_DISABLE_FIELDS

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Set the value based on selection screen input
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_plog_status USING fp_log TYPE char1.
  IF fp_log IS NOT INITIAL.
    "Green light
    IF fp_log EQ c_process.
      v_fsr_status  = c_green.
      "Red light
    ELSEIF fp_log EQ c_error.
      v_fsr_status  = c_red.
      "Yellow light
    ELSEIF fp_log EQ c_nprocess.
      v_fsr_status  = c_yellow.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                    " F_FSR_STATUS

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*

*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_value CHANGING fp_proc_status TYPE /ccej/otc_proc_status.
  DATA: l_i_tab    TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF dd07v,
        l_i_proc   TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_proc,
        l_wa_proc  TYPE ty_proc,
        l_i_return TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ddshretval.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <l_fs_tab>    TYPE dd07v,
                 <l_fs_return> TYPE ddshretval.

*    get domain values
  CALL FUNCTION 'DD_DOMVALUES_GET'
    EXPORTING
      domname        = c_dom_proc_status "/CCEJ/OTC_PROC_STATUS
      text           = abap_true
      langu          = sy-langu
    TABLES
      dd07v_tab      = l_i_tab
    EXCEPTIONS
      wrong_textflag = 1
      OTHERS         = 2.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
    CLEAR l_i_tab.
  ELSE.
    LOOP AT l_i_tab ASSIGNING <l_fs_tab>.
*      value N(not executed) should only be shown for Driver radiobutton
      IF rb_driv NE abap_true AND
         <l_fs_tab>-domvalue_l EQ c_notexecuted.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.
      l_wa_proc-value = <l_fs_tab>-domvalue_l.
      l_wa_proc-descp = <l_fs_tab>-ddtext.
      APPEND l_wa_proc TO l_i_proc.
      CLEAR l_wa_proc.
    ENDLOOP.
    IF <l_fs_tab> IS ASSIGNED.
      UNASSIGN <l_fs_tab>.
    ENDIF.
    IF l_i_proc[] IS NOT INITIAL.
      CALL FUNCTION 'F4IF_INT_TABLE_VALUE_REQUEST'
        EXPORTING
          retfield        = c_retfld
          dynpprog        = sy-cprog
          value_org       = c_value_org
        TABLES
          value_tab       = l_i_proc
          return_tab      = l_i_return
        EXCEPTIONS
          parameter_error = 1
          no_values_found = 2
          OTHERS          = 3.
      IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
        REFRESH l_i_proc.
      ELSE.
        READ TABLE l_i_return ASSIGNING <l_fs_return> INDEX 1.
        IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
          fp_proc_status = <l_fs_return>-fieldval.
          UNASSIGN <l_fs_return>.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                    " F_VALUE
***end of addition for MOD-024
***start of addition for MOD-025
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_CLEAR_SCREEN
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Clear field on selection screen based on radiobutton
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_clear_screen .
  CASE abap_true.
* Based on radiobutton selected fill global variable with a unique value
* When value changes- when different radiobutton selected, then clear
* selection screen values except VisitList, Plant, Route, Settlmnt Date
    WHEN rb_driv.
      v_radio = c_green.
    WHEN rb_visit.
      v_radio = c_red.
    WHEN rb_slrep.
      v_radio = c_yellow.
    WHEN rb_patdt.
      v_radio = c_process.
    WHEN rb_check.
      v_radio = c_mismatch.
    WHEN rb_money.
      v_radio = c_nprocess.
    WHEN rb_quan.
      v_radio = c_unplanned.
    WHEN rb_fsdoc.
      v_radio = c_visited.
    WHEN rb_cash.
      v_radio = c_plan_not_visit.
    WHEN OTHERS.
*      do nothing
  ENDCASE.

  IF v_radio1 IS INITIAL.
    v_radio1 = v_radio.
  ENDIF.

*  when radiobutton changes
  IF v_radio NE v_radio1.
    v_radio1 = v_radio.
    CLEAR: s_tplst[], s_route[], s_vehic[], s_driv[], s_hht[],
           s_status[], s_erdat[], s_kunnr[], s_equp[], s_katr4[],
           s_katr3[], s_vistyp[], s_sum_st[], s_auart[], s_so[],
           s_lfart[], s_do[], s_fkart[], s_inv[], s_blart[],
           s_belnr[], s_coityp[], s_coinv[], s_cfityp[], s_comfi[],
           s_log[], s_qty[], s_fsrlog[], s_dummy[], s_plan[],
           s_matnr[], s_chkid[], s_mdiff[], s_pay[], s_objtyp[],
           s_docno[], s_plog[], s_vlog[], s_scene[], s_light[],
           s_dswap[], s_tplst, s_route, s_vehic, s_driv, s_hht,
           s_status, s_erdat, s_kunnr, s_equp, s_katr4,
           s_katr3, s_vistyp, s_sum_st, s_auart, s_so,
           s_lfart, s_do, s_fkart, s_inv, s_blart,
           s_belnr, s_coityp, s_coinv, s_cfityp, s_comfi,
           s_log, s_qty, s_fsrlog, s_dummy, s_plan,
           s_matnr, s_chkid, s_mdiff, s_pay, s_objtyp,
           s_docno, s_plog, s_vlog, s_scene, s_light, s_dswap,
***start of addition for MOD-027
           s_bschl[], s_bschl, s_blart1[], s_blart1.
***end of addition for MOD-027
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                    " F_CLEAR_SCREEN
***end of addition for MOD-025
***start of addition for MOD-027
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F_EVENT_FIL
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       Register event after output generated on ALV
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f_event_fil  USING    fp_table  TYPE REF TO cl_salv_table.
*********************Reference variable Declaration*******************
  DATA: l_o_events TYPE REF TO cl_salv_events_table,
        l_o_handle TYPE REF TO lcl_handle_events.
* get the event reference
  l_o_events = fp_table->get_event( ).
  CREATE OBJECT l_o_handle.

  SET HANDLER l_o_handle->set_after_events FOR l_o_events.
ENDFORM.                    " F_EVENT_FIL
***end of addition for MOD-027
